% ----------------------
\subsection{2 Samuel}
% ----------------------
	\label{2SAMUEL}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{2 Samuel 1} % *2SAMUEL-1
	% -----
		\label{2SAMUEL-1}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:1} % *2SAMUEL-1-1
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-1}

			Now it came to pass after the death of Saul, when David was returned from the slaughter of the Amalekites, and David had abode two days in Ziklag;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֗י אַֽחֲרֵי֙ מ֣וֹת שָׁא֔וּל וְדָוִ֣ד שָׁ֔ב מֵהַכּ֖וֹת אֶת־הָעֲמָלֵ֑ק וַיֵּ֧שֶׁב דָּוִ֛ד בְּצִקְלָ֖ג יָמִ֥ים שְׁנָֽיִם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ΚΑΙ ἐγένετο μετὰ τὸ ἀποθανεῖν Σαούλ, καὶ Δαυεὶδ ἀνέστρεψεν τύπτων τὸν Ἀμαλήκ· καὶ ἐκάθισεν Δαυεὶδ ἐν Σεκελὰκ ἡμέρας δύο.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And it came to pass after the death of Saul, when David was returned from the slaughter of the Amalekites, and David had abode two days in Ziklag;
					\item[NIV] After the death of Saul, David returned from striking down the Amalekites and stayed in Ziklag two days.
					\item[NRSV] After the death of Saul, when David had returned from defeating the Amalekites, David remained two days in Ziklag.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:2} % *2SAMUEL-1-2
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-2}

			It came even to pass on the third day, that, behold, a man came out of the camp from Saul with his clothes rent, and earth upon his head: and so it was, when he came to David, that he fell to the earth, and did obeisance.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֣י ׀ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁ֗י וְהִנֵּה֩ אִ֨ישׁ בָּ֤א מִן־הַֽמַּחֲנֶה֙ מֵעִ֣ם שָׁא֔וּל וּבְגָדָ֣יו קְרֻעִ֔ים וַאֲדָמָ֖ה עַל־רֹאשׁ֑וֹ וַיְהִי֙ בְּבֹא֣וֹ אֶל־דָּוִ֔ד וַיִּפֹּ֥ל אַ֖רְצָה וַיִּשְׁתָּֽחוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐγενήθη τῇ ἡμέρᾳ τῇ τρίτῃ καὶ ἰδοὺ ἀνὴρ ἦλθεν ἐκ τῆς παρεμβολῆς ἐκ τοῦ λαοῦ Σαούλ, καὶ τὰ ἱμάτια αὐτοῦ διερρωγότα καὶ γῆ ἐπὶ τῆς κεφαλῆς αὐτοῦ· καὶ ἐγένετο ἐν τῷ εἰσελθεῖν αὐτὸν πρὸς Δαυεὶδ καὶ ἔπεσεν ἐπὶ τὴν γῆν καὶ προσεκύνησεν αὐτῷ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] it came to pass on the third day, that, behold, a man came out of the camp from Saul, with his clothes rent, and earth upon his head: and so it was, when he came to David, that he fell to the earth, and did obeisance.
					\item[NIV] On the third day a man arrived from Saul’s camp with his clothes torn and dust on his head. When he came to David, he fell to the ground to pay him honor.
					\item[NRSV] On the third day, a man came from Saul’s camp, with his clothes torn and dirt on his head. When he came to David, he fell to the ground and did obeisance.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:3} % *2SAMUEL-1-3
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-3}

			And David said unto him, From whence comest thou? And he said unto him, Out of the camp of Israel am I escaped.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר לוֹ֙ דָּוִ֔ד אֵ֥י מִזֶּ֖ה תָּב֑וֹא וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלָ֔יו מִמַּחֲנֵ֥ה יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל נִמְלָֽטְתִּי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν αὐτῷ Δαυείδ Πόθεν σὺ παραγίνῃ; καὶ εἶπεν πρὸς αὐτόν Ἐκ τῆς παρεμβολῆς Ἰσραὴλ ἐγὼ διασέσωμαι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David said unto him, From whence comest thou? And he said unto him, Out of the camp of Israel am I escaped.
					\item[NIV] “Where have you come from?” David asked him. He answered, “I have escaped from the Israelite camp.”
					\item[NRSV] David said to him, “Where have you come from?” He said to him, “I have escaped from the camp of Israel.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:4} % *2SAMUEL-1-4
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-4}

			And David said unto him, How went the matter? I pray thee, tell me. And he answered, That the people are fled from the battle, and many of the people also are fallen and dead; and Saul and Jonathan his son are dead also.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֨אמֶר אֵלָ֥יו דָּוִ֛ד מֶה־הָיָ֥ה הַדָּבָ֖ר הַגֶּד־נָ֣א לִ֑י וַ֠יֹּאמֶר אֲשֶׁר־נָ֨ס הָעָ֜ם מִן־הַמִּלְחָמָ֗ה וְגַם־הַרְבֵּ֞ה נָפַ֤ל מִן־הָעָם֙ וַיָּמֻ֔תוּ וְגַ֗ם שָׁא֛וּל וִיהוֹנָתָ֥ן בְּנ֖וֹ מֵֽתוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν αὐτῷ Δαυείδ Τίς ὁ λόγος οὗτος; ἀπάγγειλόν μοι. καὶ εἶπεν ὅτι Ἔφυγεν ὁ λαὸς ἐκ τοῦ πολέμου, καὶ πεπτώκασι πολλοὶ ἐκ τοῦ λαοῦ καὶ ἀπέθανον· καὶ ἀπέθανεν καὶ Σαούλ, καὶ Ἰωναθὰν ὁ υἱὸς αὐτοῦ ἀπέθανεν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David said unto him, How went the matter? I pray thee, tell me. And he answered, The people are fled from the battle, and many of the people also are fallen and dead; and Saul and Jonathan his son are dead also.
					\item[NIV] “What happened?” David asked. “Tell me.” “The men fled from the battle,” he replied. “Many of them fell and died. And Saul and his son Jonathan are dead.”
					\item[NRSV] David said to him, “How did things go? Tell me!” He answered, “The army fled from the battle, but also many of the army fell and died; and Saul and his son Jonathan also died.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:5} % *2SAMUEL-1-5
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-5}

			And David said unto the young man that told him, How knowest thou that Saul and Jonathan his son be dead?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֣אמֶר דָּוִ֔ד אֶל־הַנַּ֖עַר הַמַּגִּ֣יד ל֑וֹ אֵ֣יךְ יָדַ֔עְתָּ כִּי־מֵ֥ת שָׁא֖וּל וִיהֽוֹנָתָ֥ן בְּנֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Δαυεὶδ τῷ παιδαρίῳ τῷ ἀπαγγέλλοντι αὐτῷ Πῶς οἶδας ὅτι τέθνηκεν Σαοὺλ καὶ Ἰωναθὰν ὁ υἱὸς αὐτοῦ;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David said unto the young man that told him, How knowest thou that Saul and Jonathan his son are dead?
					\item[NIV] Then David said to the young man who brought him the report, “How do you know that Saul and his son Jonathan are dead?”
					\item[NRSV] Then David asked the young man who was reporting to him, “How do you know that Saul and his son Jonathan died?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:6} % *2SAMUEL-1-6
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-6}

			And the young man that told him said, As I happened by chance upon mount Gilboa, behold, Saul leaned upon his spear; and, lo, the chariots and horsemen followed hard after him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֜אמֶר הַנַּ֣עַר ׀ הַמַּגִּ֣יד ל֗וֹ נִקְרֹ֤א נִקְרֵ֙יתִי֙ בְּהַ֣ר הַגִּלְבֹּ֔עַ וְהִנֵּ֥ה שָׁא֖וּל נִשְׁעָ֣ן עַל־חֲנִית֑וֹ וְהִנֵּ֥ה הָרֶ֛כֶב וּבַעֲלֵ֥י הַפָּרָשִׁ֖ים הִדְבִּקֻֽהוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν τὸ παιδάριον τὸ ἀπαγγέλλον αὐτῷ Περιπτώματι περιέπεσαν ἐν τῷ ὄρει τῷ Γελβοῦε, καὶ ἰδοὺ Σαοὺλ ἐπεστήρικτο ἐπὶ τὸ δόρυ αὐτοῦ, καὶ ἰδοὺ τὰ ἅρματα καὶ οἱ ἱππάρχαι συνῆψαν αὐτῷ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the young man that told him said, As I happened by chance upon mount Gilboa, behold, Saul was leaning upon his spear; and, lo, the chariots and the horsemen followed hard after him.
					\item[NIV] “I happened to be on Mount Gilboa,” the young man said, “and there was Saul, leaning on his spear, with the chariots and their drivers in hot pursuit.
					\item[NRSV] The young man reporting to him said, “I happened to be on Mount Gilboa; and there was Saul leaning on his spear, while the chariots and the horsemen drew close to him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:7} % *2SAMUEL-1-7
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-7}

			And when he looked behind him, he saw me, and called unto me. And I answered, Here am I.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּ֥פֶן אַחֲרָ֖יו וַיִּרְאֵ֑נִי וַיִּקְרָ֣א אֵלָ֔י וָאֹמַ֖ר הִנֵּֽנִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐπέβλεψεν ἐπὶ τὰ ὀπίσω αὐτοῦ, καὶ εἶδεν καὶ ἐκάλεσέν με, καὶ εἶπα Ἰδοὺ ἐγώ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And when he looked behind him, he saw me, and called unto me. And I answered, Here am I.
					\item[NIV] When he turned around and saw me, he called out to me, and I said, ‘What can I do?’
					\item[NRSV] When he looked behind him, he saw me, and called to me. I answered, ‘Here sir.’
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:8} % *2SAMUEL-1-8
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-8}

			And he said unto me, Who art thou? And I answered him, I am an Amalekite.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֥אמֶר לִ֖י מִי־אָ֑תָּה וָאֹמַ֣ר אֵלָ֔יו עֲמָלֵקִ֖י אָנֹֽכִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπέν μοι Τίς εἶ σύ; καὶ εἶπα Ἀμαληκείτης ἐγώ εἰμι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he said unto me, Who art thou? And I answered him, I am an Amalekite.
					\item[NIV] “He asked me, ‘Who are you?’ “’An Amalekite,’ I answered.
					\item[NRSV] And he said to me, ‘Who are you?’ I answered him, ‘I am an Amalekite.’
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:9} % *2SAMUEL-1-9
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-9}

			He said unto me again, Stand, I pray thee, upon me, and slay me: for anguish is come upon me, because my life is yet whole in me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֗י עֲמָד־נָ֤א עָלַי֙ וּמֹ֣תְתֵ֔נִי כִּ֥י אֲחָזַ֖נִי הַשָּׁבָ֑ץ כִּֽי־כָל־ע֥וֹד נַפְשִׁ֖י בִּֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν πρὸς μέ Στῆθι δὴ ἐπάνω μου καὶ θανάτωσόν με, ὅτι κατέσχεν με σκότος δεινόν, ὅτι πᾶσα ἡ ψυχή μου ἐν ἐμοί.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he said unto me, Stand, I pray thee, beside me, and slay me; for anguish hath taken hold of me, because my life is yet whole in me.
					\item[NIV] “Then he said to me, ‘Stand here by me and kill me! I’m in the throes of death, but I’m still alive.’
					\item[NRSV] He said to me, ‘Come, stand over me and kill me; for convulsions have seized me, and yet my life still lingers.’
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:10} % *2SAMUEL-1-10
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-10}

			So I stood upon him, and slew him, because I was sure that he could not live after that he was fallen: and I took the crown that was upon his head, and the bracelet that was on his arm, and have brought them hither unto my lord.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וָאֶעֱמֹ֤ד עָלָיו֙ וַאֲמֹ֣תְתֵ֔הוּ כִּ֣י יָדַ֔עְתִּי כִּ֛י לֹ֥א יִֽחְיֶ֖ה אַחֲרֵ֣י נִפְל֑וֹ וָאֶקַּ֞ח הַנֵּ֣זֶר ׀ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־רֹאשׁ֗וֹ וְאֶצְעָדָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־זְרֹע֔וֹ וָאֲבִיאֵ֥ם אֶל־אֲדֹנִ֖י הֵֽנָּה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐπέστην ἐπ᾽ αὐτὸν καὶ ἐθανάτωσα αὐτόν, ὅτι ᾔδειν ὅτι οὐ ζήσεται μετὰ τὸ πεσεῖν αὐτόν· καὶ ἔλαβον τὸ βασίλειον τὸ ἐπὶ τὴν κεφαλὴν αὐτοῦ καὶ τὸν χλίδωνα τὸν ἐπὶ τοῦ βραχίονος αὐτοῦ, καὶ ἐνήνοχα αὐτὰ τῷ κυρίῳ μου ὧδε.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So I stood beside him, and slew him, because I was sure that he could not live after that he was fallen: and I took the crown that was upon his head, and the bracelet that was on his arm, and have brought them hither unto my lord.
					\item[NIV] “So I stood beside him and killed him, because I knew that after he had fallen he could not survive. And I took the crown that was on his head and the band on his arm and have brought them here to my lord.”
					\item[NRSV] So I stood over him, and killed him, for I knew that he could not live after he had fallen. I took the crown that was on his head and the armlet that was on his arm, and I have brought them here to my lord.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:11} % *2SAMUEL-1-11
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-11}

			Then David took hold on his clothes, and rent them; and likewise all the men that were with him:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּחֲזֵ֥ק דָּוִ֛ד בִּבְגָדָ֖יו וַיִּקְרָעֵ֑ם וְגַ֥ם כָּל־הָאֲנָשִׁ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר אִתּֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐκράτησεν Δαυεὶδ τῶν ἱματίων αὐτοῦ καὶ διέρρηξεν αὐτά, καὶ πάντες οἱ ἄνδρες οἱ μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ διέρρηξαν τὰ ἱμάτια αὐτῶν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then David took hold on his clothes, and rent them; and likewise all the men that were with him:
					\item[NIV] Then David and all the men with him took hold of their clothes and tore them.
					\item[NRSV] Then David took hold of his clothes and tore them; and all the men who were with him did the same.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:12} % *2SAMUEL-1-12
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-12}

			And they mourned, and wept, and fasted until even, for Saul, and for Jonathan his son, and for the people of the LORD, and for the house of Israel; because they were fallen by the sword.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַֽיִּסְפְּדוּ֙ וַיִּבְכּ֔וּ וַיָּצֻ֖מוּ עַד־הָעָ֑רֶב עַל־שָׁא֞וּל וְעַל־יְהוֹנָתָ֣ן בְּנ֗וֹ וְעַל־עַ֤ם יְהוָה֙ וְעַל־בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כִּ֥י נָפְל֖וּ בֶּחָֽרֶב׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐκόψαντο καὶ ἐνήστευσαν καὶ ἔκλαυσαν ἕως δείλης ἐπὶ Σαοὺλ καὶ ἐπὶ Ἰωναθὰν τὸν υἱὸν αὐτοῦ καὶ ἐπὶ τὸν λαὸν Ἰούδα καὶ ἐπὶ τὸν οἶκον Ἰσραήλ, ὅτι ἐπλήγησαν ἐν ῥομφαίᾳ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] and they mourned, and wept, and fasted until even, for Saul, and for Jonathan his son, and for the people of Jehovah, and for the house of Israel; because they were fallen by the sword.
					\item[NIV] They mourned and wept and fasted till evening for Saul and his son Jonathan, and for the army of the LORD and for the nation of Israel, because they had fallen by the sword.
					\item[NRSV] They mourned and wept, and fasted until evening for Saul and for his son Jonathan, and for the army of the LORD and for the house of Israel, because they had fallen by the sword.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:13} % *2SAMUEL-1-13
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-13}

			And David said unto the young man that told him, Whence art thou? And he answered, I am the son of a stranger, an Amalekite.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֣אמֶר דָּוִ֗ד אֶל־הַנַּ֙עַר֙ הַמַּגִּ֣יד ל֔וֹ אֵ֥י מִזֶּ֖ה אָ֑תָּה וַיֹּ֕אמֶר בֶּן־אִ֛ישׁ גֵּ֥ר עֲמָלֵקִ֖י אָנֹֽכִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Δαυεὶδ τῷ παιδαρίῳ τῷ ἀπαγγέλλοντι αὐτῷ Πόθεν εἶ σύ; καὶ εἶπεν Υἱὸς ἀνδρὸς παροίκου Ἀμαληκείτου ἐγώ εἰμι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David said unto the young man that told him, Whence art thou? And he answered, I am the son of a sojourner, an Amalekite.
					\item[NIV] David said to the young man who brought him the report, “Where are you from?” “I am the son of a foreigner, an Amalekite,” he answered.
					\item[NRSV] David said to the young man who had reported to him, “Where do you come from?” He answered, “I am the son of a resident alien, an Amalekite.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:14} % *2SAMUEL-1-14
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-14}

			And David said unto him, How wast thou not afraid to stretch forth thine hand to destroy the LORD’s anointed?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֥אמֶר אֵלָ֖יו דָּוִ֑ד אֵ֚יךְ לֹ֣א יָרֵ֔אתָ לִשְׁלֹ֙חַ֙ יָֽדְךָ֔ לְשַׁחֵ֖ת אֶת־מְשִׁ֥יחַ יְהוָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν αὐτῷ Δαυείδ Πῶς οὐκ ἐφοβήθης ἐπενεγκεῖν χεῖρά σου διαφθεῖραι τὸν χριστὸν Κυρίου;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David said unto him, How wast thou not afraid to put forth thy hand to destroy Jehovah's anointed?
					\item[NIV] David asked him, “Why weren’t you afraid to lift your hand to destroy the LORD’s anointed?”
					\item[NRSV] David said to him, “Were you not afraid to lift your hand to destroy the LORD’s anointed?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:15} % *2SAMUEL-1-15
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-15}

			And David called one of the young men, and said, Go near, and fall upon him. And he smote him that he died.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּקְרָ֣א דָוִ֗ד לְאַחַד֙ מֵֽהַנְּעָרִ֔ים וַיֹּ֖אמֶר גַּ֣שׁ פְּגַע־בּ֑וֹ וַיַּכֵּ֖הוּ וַיָּמֹֽת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐκάλεσεν Δαυεὶδ ἓν τῶν παιδαρίων αὐτοῦ καὶ εἶπεν Προσελθὼν ἀπάντησον αὐτῷ· καὶ ἐπάταξεν αὐτόν, καὶ ἀπέθανεν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David called one of the young men, and said, Go near, and fall upon him. And he smote him, so that he died.
					\item[NIV] Then David called one of his men and said, “Go, strike him down!” So he struck him down, and he died.
					\item[NRSV] Then David called one of the young men and said, “Come here and strike him down.” So he struck him down and he died.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:16} % *2SAMUEL-1-16
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-16}

			And David said unto him, Thy blood be upon thy head; for thy mouth hath testified against thee, saying, I have slain the LORD’s anointed.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר אֵלָיו֙ דָּוִ֔ד דָּמְךָ֖ עַל־רֹאשֶׁ֑ךָ כִּ֣י פִ֗יךָ עָנָ֤ה בְךָ֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר אָנֹכִ֥י מֹתַ֖תִּי אֶת־מְשִׁ֥יחַ יְהוָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Δαυεὶδ πρὸς αὐτόν Τὸ αἷμά σου ἐπὶ τὴν κεφαλήν σου, ὅτι τὸ στόμα σου ἀπεκρίθη κατὰ σοῦ λέγων ὅτι Ἐγὼ ἐθανάτωσα τόν χριστὸν Κυρίου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David said unto him, Thy blood be upon thy head; for thy mouth hath testified against thee, saying, I have slain Jehovah's anointed.
					\item[NIV] For David had said to him, “Your blood be on your own head. Your own mouth testified against you when you said, ‘I killed the LORD’s anointed.’”
					\item[NRSV] David said to him, “Your blood be on your head; for your own mouth has testified against you, saying, ‘I have killed the LORD’s anointed.’”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-17}

			And David lamented with this lamentation over Saul and over Jonathan his son:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְקֹנֵ֣ן דָּוִ֔ד אֶת־הַקִּינָ֖ה הַזֹּ֑את עַל־שָׁא֖וּל וְעַל־יְהוֹנָתָ֥ן בְּנֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ ἐθρήνησεν Δαυεὶδ τὸν θρῆνον τοῦτον ἐπὶ Σαοὺλ καὶ ἐπὶ Ἰωναθὰν τὸν υἱὸν αὐτοῦ,}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David lamented with this lamentation over Saul and over Jonathan his son
					\item[NIV] David took up this lament concerning Saul and his son Jonathan,
					\item[NRSV] David intoned this lamentation over Saul and his son Jonathan.

			(Also he bade them teach the children of Judah the use of the bow: behold, it is written in the book of Jasher.)

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֕אמֶר לְלַמֵּ֥ד בְּנֵֽי־יְהוּדָ֖ה קָ֑שֶׁת הִנֵּ֥ה כְתוּבָ֖ה עַל־סֵ֥פֶר הַיָּשָֽׁר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν τοῦ διδάξαι τοὺς υἱοὺς Ἰούδα· ἰδοὺ γέγραπται ἐπὶ βιβλίου τοῦ εὐθοῦς.}

		\paragraph{2 Samuel 1:19} % *2SAMUEL-1-19
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-1-19}

			The beauty of Israel is slain upon thy high places: how are the mighty fallen!

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַצְּבִי֙ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל עַל־בָּמוֹתֶ֖יךָ חָלָ֑ל אֵ֖יךְ נָפְל֥וּ גִבּוֹרִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Στήλωσον, Ἰσραήλ, ὑπὲρ τῶν τεθνηκότων ἐπὶ τὰ ὕψη σου τραυματιῶν· πῶς ἔπεσαν δυνατοί.}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] “A gazelle lies slain on your heights, Israel. How the mighty have fallen!
					\item[NRSV] Your glory, O Israel, lies slain upon your high places! How the mighty have fallen!
