		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 16:21} % *2SAMUEL-16-21
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-16-21}

			And Ahithophel said unto Absalom, Go in unto thy father’s concubines, which he hath left to keep the house; and all Israel shall hear that thou art abhorred of thy father: then shall the hands of all that are with thee be strong.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר אֲחִיתֹ֙פֶל֙ אֶל־אַבְשָׁלֹ֔ם בּ֚וֹא אֶל־פִּלַגְשֵׁ֣י אָבִ֔יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר הִנִּ֖יחַ לִשְׁמ֣וֹר הַבָּ֑יִת וְשָׁמַ֤ע כָּל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ כִּֽי־נִבְאַ֣שְׁתָּ אֶת־אָבִ֔יךָ וְחָ֣זְק֔וּ יְדֵ֖י כָּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר אִתָּֽךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Ἀχειτόφελ πρὸς Ἀβεσσαλώμ Εἴσελθε πρὸς τὰς παλλακὰς τοῦ πατρός σου ἃς κατέλειπεν φυλάσσειν τὸν οἶκον, καὶ ἀκούσεται πᾶς Ἰσραὴλ ὅτι κατῄσχυνας τὸν πατέρα σου, καὶ ἐνισχύσουσιν αἱ χεῖρες πάντων τῶν μετὰ σοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Ahithophel said unto Absalom, Go in unto thy father's concubines, that he hath left to keep the house; and all Israel will hear that thou art abhorred of thy father: then will the hands of all that are with thee be strong.
					\item[NIV] Ahithophel answered, “Sleep with your father’s concubines whom he left to take care of the palace. Then all Israel will hear that you have made yourself obnoxious to your father, and the hands of everyone with you will be more resolute.”
					\item[NRSV] Ahithophel said to Absalom, “Go in to your father’s concubines, the ones he has left to look after the house; and all Israel will hear that you have made yourself odious to your father, and the hands of all who are with you will be strengthened.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 16:22} % *2SAMUEL-16-22
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-16-22}

			So they spread Absalom a tent upon the top of the house; and Absalom went in unto his father’s concubines in the sight of all Israel.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּטּ֧וּ לְאַבְשָׁל֛וֹם הָאֹ֖הֶל עַל־הַגָּ֑ג וַיָּבֹ֤א אַבְשָׁלוֹם֙ אֶל־פִּֽלַגְשֵׁ֣י אָבִ֔יו לְעֵינֵ֖י כָּל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἔπηξαν τὴν σκηνὴν τῷ Ἀβεσσαλὼμ ἐπὶ τὸ δῶμα, καὶ εἰσῆλθεν Ἀβεσσαλὼμ πρὸς τὰς παλλακὰς τοῦ πατρὸς αὐτοῦ κατ᾽ ὀφθαλμοὺς παντὸς Ἰσραήλ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So they spread Absalom a tent upon the top of the house; and Absalom went in unto his father's concubines in the sight of all Israel.
					\item[NIV] So they pitched a tent for Absalom on the roof, and he slept with his father’s concubines in the sight of all Israel.
					\item[NRSV] So they pitched a tent for Absalom upon the roof; and Absalom went in to his father’s concubines in the sight of all Israel.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 16:23} % *2SAMUEL-16-23
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-16-23}

			And the counsel of Ahithophel, which he counselled in those days, was as if a man had enquired at the oracle of God: so was all the counsel of Ahithophel both with David and with Absalom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַעֲצַ֣ת אֲחִיתֹ֗פֶל אֲשֶׁ֤ר יָעַץ֙ בַּיָּמִ֣ים הָהֵ֔ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר יִשְׁאַל־[אִ֖ישׁ] בִּדְבַ֣ר הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים כֵּ֚ן כָּל־עֲצַ֣ת אֲחִיתֹ֔פֶל גַּם־לְדָוִ֖ד גַּ֥ם לְאַבְשָׁלֹֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἡ βουλὴ Ἀχειτόφελ ἣν ἐβουλεύσατο ἐν ταῖς ἡμέραις ταῖς πρώταις ὃν τρόπον ἐπερωτήσῃ ἐν λόγῳ τοῦ θεοῦ· οὕτως πᾶσα ἡ βουλὴ τῷ Ἀχειτόφελ καί γε τῷ Δαυεὶδ καί γε τῷ Ἀβεσσαλώμ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the counsel of Ahithophel, which he gave in those days, was as if a man inquired at the oracle of God: so was all the counsel of Ahithophel both with David and with Absalom.
					\item[NIV] Now in those days the advice Ahithophel gave was like that of one who inquires of God. That was how both David and Absalom regarded all of Ahithophel’s advice.
					\item[NRSV] Now in those days the counsel that Ahithophel gave was as if one consulted the oracle of God; so all the counsel of Ahithophel was esteemed, both by David and by Absalom.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{2 Samuel 17} % *2SAMUEL-17
	% -----
		\label{2SAMUEL-17}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:1} % *2SAMUEL-17-1
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-1}

			Moreover Ahithophel said unto Absalom, Let me now choose out twelve thousand men, and I will arise and pursue after David this night:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֥אמֶר אֲחִיתֹ֖פֶל אֶל־אַבְשָׁלֹ֑ם אֶבְחֲרָ֣ה נָּ֗א שְׁנֵים־עָשָׂ֥ר אֶ֙לֶף֙ אִ֔ישׁ וְאָק֛וּמָה וְאֶרְדְּפָ֥ה אַחֲרֵי־דָוִ֖ד הַלָּֽיְלָה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ εἶπεν Ἀχειτόφελ πρὸς Ἀβεσσαλώμ Ἐπιλέξω δὴ ἐμαυτῷ δώδεκα χιλιάδας ἀνδρῶν, καὶ ἀναστήσομαι καὶ καταδιώξω ὀπίσω Δαυεὶδ τὴν νύκτα.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Moreover Ahithophel said unto Absalom, Let me now choose out twelve thousand men, and I will arise and pursue after David this night:
					\item[NIV] Ahithophel said to Absalom, “I would choose twelve thousand men and set out tonight in pursuit of David.
					\item[NRSV] Moreover Ahithophel said to Absalom, “Let me choose twelve thousand men, and I will set out and pursue David tonight.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:2} % *2SAMUEL-17-2
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-2}

			And I will come upon him while he is weary and weak handed, and will make him afraid: and all the people that are with him shall flee; and I will smite the king only:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאָב֣וֹא עָלָ֗יו וְה֤וּא יָגֵ֙עַ֙ וּרְפֵ֣ה יָדַ֔יִם וְהַֽחֲרַדְתִּ֣י אֹת֔וֹ וְנָ֖ס כָּל־הָעָ֣ם אֲשֶׁר־אִתּ֑וֹ וְהִכֵּיתִ֥י אֶת־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ לְבַדּֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐπελεύσομαι ἐπ᾽ αὐτόν, καὶ αὐτὸς κοπιῶν καὶ ἐκλελυμένος χερσίν, καὶ ἐκστήσω αὐτόν, καὶ φεύξεται πᾶς ὁ λαὸς ὁ μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ καὶ πατάξω τὸν βασιλέα μονώτατον·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] and I will come upon him while he is weary and weak-handed, and will make him afraid; and all the people that are with him shall flee; and I will smite the king only;
					\item[NIV] I would attack him while he is weary and weak. I would strike him with terror, and then all the people with him will flee. I would strike down only the king
					\item[NRSV] I will come upon him while he is weary and discouraged, and throw him into a panic; and all the people who are with him will flee. I will strike down only the king,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:3} % *2SAMUEL-17-3
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-3}

			And I will bring back all the people unto thee: the man whom thou seekest is as if all returned: so all the people shall be in peace.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאָשִׁ֥יבָה כָל־הָעָ֖ם אֵלֶ֑יךָ כְּשׁ֣וּב הַכֹּ֔ל הָאִישׁ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אַתָּ֣ה מְבַקֵּ֔שׁ כָּל־הָעָ֖ם יִהְיֶ֥ה שָׁלֽוֹם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐπιστρέψω πάντα τὸν λαὸν πρὸς σέ, ὃν τρόπον ἐπιστρέφει ἡ νύμφη πρὸς τὸν ἄνδρα αὐτῆς· πλὴν ψυχὴν ἑνὸς ἀνδρὸς σὺ ζητεῖς, καὶ παντὶ τῷ λαῷ ἔσται ἐν εἰρήνῃ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] and I will bring back all the people unto thee: the man whom thou seekest is as if all returned: so all the people shall be in peace.
					\item[NIV] and bring all the people back to you. The death of the man you seek will mean the return of all; all the people will be unharmed.”
					\item[NRSV] and I will bring all the people back to you as a bride comes home to her husband. You seek the life of only one man, and all the people will be at peace.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:4} % *2SAMUEL-17-4
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-4}

			And the saying pleased Absalom well, and all the elders of Israel.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּישַׁ֥ר הַדָּבָ֖ר בְּעֵינֵ֣י אַבְשָׁלֹ֑ם וּבְעֵינֵ֖י כָּל־זִקְנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εὐθὴς ὁ λόγος ἐν ὀφθαλμοῖς Ἀβεσσαλὼμ καὶ ἐν ὀφθαλμοῖς πάντων τῶν πρεσβυτέρων Ἰσραήλ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the saying pleased Absalom well, and all the elders of Israel.
					\item[NIV] This plan seemed good to Absalom and to all the elders of Israel.
					\item[NRSV] The advice pleased Absalom and all the elders of Israel.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:5} % *2SAMUEL-17-5
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-5}

			Then said Absalom, Call now Hushai the Archite also, and let us hear likewise what he saith.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ אַבְשָׁל֔וֹם קְרָ֣א נָ֔א גַּ֖ם לְחוּשַׁ֣י הָאַרְכִּ֑י וְנִשְׁמְעָ֥ה מַה־בְּפִ֖יו גַּם־הֽוּא׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ εἶπεν Ἀβεσσαλώμ Καλέσατε δὴ καί γε τὸν Χουσεὶ τὸν Ἀραχεί, καὶ ἀκούσωμεν τί ἐν τῷ στόματι αὐτοῦ, καί γε αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then said Absalom, Call now Hushai the Archite also, and let us hear likewise what he saith.
					\item[NIV] But Absalom said, “Summon also Hushai the Arkite, so we can hear what he has to say as well.”
					\item[NRSV] Then Absalom said, “Call Hushai the Archite also, and let us hear too what he has to say.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:6} % *2SAMUEL-17-6
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-6}

			And when Hushai was come to Absalom, Absalom spake unto him, saying, Ahithophel hath spoken after this manner: shall we do after his saying? if not; speak thou.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּבֹ֣א חוּשַׁי֮ אֶל־אַבְשָׁלוֹם֒ וַיֹּאמֶר֩ אַבְשָׁל֨וֹם אֵלָ֜יו לֵאמֹ֗ר כַּדָּבָ֤ר הַזֶּה֙ דִּבֶּ֣ר אֲחִיתֹ֔פֶל הֲנַעֲשֶׂ֖ה אֶת־דְּבָר֑וֹ אִם־אַ֖יִן אַתָּ֥ה דַבֵּֽר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἰσῆλθεν Χουσεὶ πρὸς Ἀβεσσαλώμ· καὶ εἶπεν Ἀβεσσαλὼμ πρὸς αὐτὸν λέγων Κατὰ τὸ ῥῆμα τοῦτο ὃ ἐλάλησεν Ἀχειτόφελ· ποιήσομεν κατὰ τὸν λόγον αὐτοῦ; εἰ δὲ μή, σὺ λάλησον.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And when Hushai was come to Absalom, Absalom spake unto him, saying, Ahithophel hath spoken after this manner: shall we do after his saying? if not, speak thou.
					\item[NIV] When Hushai came to him, Absalom said, “Ahithophel has given this advice. Should we do what he says? If not, give us your opinion.”
					\item[NRSV] When Hushai came to Absalom, Absalom said to him, “This is what Ahithophel has said; shall we do as he advises? If not, you tell us.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:7} % *2SAMUEL-17-7
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-7}

			And Hushai said unto Absalom, The counsel that Ahithophel hath given is not good at this time.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֥אמֶר חוּשַׁ֖י אֶל־אַבְשָׁל֑וֹם לֹֽא־טוֹבָ֧ה הָעֵצָ֛ה אֲשֶׁר־יָעַ֥ץ אֲחִיתֹ֖פֶל בַּפַּ֥עַם הַזֹּֽאת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Χουσεὶ πρὸς Ἀβεσσαλώμ Οὐκ ἀγαθὴ αὕτη ἡ βουλὴ ἣν ἐβουλεύσατο Ἀχειτόφελ τὸ ἅπαξ τοῦτο.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Hushai said unto Absalom, The counsel that Ahithophel hath given this time is not good.
					\item[NIV] Hushai replied to Absalom, “The advice Ahithophel has given is not good this time.
					\item[NRSV] Then Hushai said to Absalom, “This time the counsel that Ahithophel has given is not good.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:8} % *2SAMUEL-17-8
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-8}

			For, said Hushai, thou knowest thy father and his men, that they be mighty men, and they be chafed in their minds, as a bear robbed of her whelps in the field: and thy father is a man of war, and will not lodge with the people.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֣אמֶר חוּשַׁ֗י אַתָּ֣ה יָ֠דַעְתָּ אֶת־אָבִ֨יךָ וְאֶת־אֲנָשָׁ֜יו כִּ֧י גִבֹּרִ֣ים הֵ֗מָּה וּמָרֵ֥י נֶ֙פֶשׁ֙ הֵ֔מָּה כְּדֹ֥ב שַׁכּ֖וּל בַּשָּׂדֶ֑ה וְאָבִ֙יךָ֙ אִ֣ישׁ מִלְחָמָ֔ה וְלֹ֥א יָלִ֖ין אֶת־הָעָֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Χουσεί Σὺ οἶδας τὸν πατέρα σου καὶ τοὺς ἄνδρας αὐτοῦ ὅτι δυνατοί εἰσιν σφόδρα καὶ κατάπικροι τῇ ψυχῇ αὐτῶν, ὡς ἄρκος ἠτεκνωμένη ἐν ἀγρῷ καὶ ὡς ὗς τραχεῖα ἐν τῷ πεδίῳ· καὶ ὁ πατήρ σου ἀνὴρ πολεμιστὴς καὶ οὐ μὴ καταλύσῃ τὸν λαόν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hushai said moreover, Thou knowest thy father and his men, that they are mighty men, and they are chafed in their minds, as a bear robbed of her whelps in the field; and thy father is a man of war, and will not lodge with the people.
					\item[NIV] You know your father and his men; they are fighters, and as fierce as a wild bear robbed of her cubs. Besides, your father is an experienced fighter; he will not spend the night with the troops.
					\item[NRSV] Hushai continued, “You know that your father and his men are warriors, and that they are enraged, like a bear robbed of her cubs in the field. Besides, your father is expert in war; he will not spend the night with the troops.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:9} % *2SAMUEL-17-9
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-9}

			Behold, he is hid now in some pit, or in some other place: and it will come to pass, when some of them be overthrown at the first, that whosoever heareth it will say, There is a slaughter among the people that follow Absalom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הִנֵּ֨ה עַתָּ֤ה הֽוּא־נֶחְבָּא֙ בְּאַחַ֣ת הַפְּחָתִ֔ים א֖וֹ בְּאַחַ֣ד הַמְּקוֹמֹ֑ת וְהָיָ֗ה כִּנְפֹ֤ל בָּהֶם֙ בַּתְּחִלָּ֔ה וְשָׁמַ֤ע הַשֹּׁמֵ֙עַ֙ וְאָמַ֔ר הָֽיְתָה֙ מַגֵּפָ֔ה בָּעָ֕ם אֲשֶׁ֖ר אַחֲרֵ֥י אַבְשָׁלֹֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἰδοὺ γὰρ αὐτὸς νῦν κέκρυπται ἐν ἑνὶ τῶν βουνῶν ἢ ἐν ἑνὶ τῶν τόπων· καὶ ἔσται ἐν τῷ ἐπιπεσεῖν αὐτοῖς ἐν ἀρχῇ καὶ ἀκούσῃ ἀκούων, καὶ εἶπεν Ἐγενήθη θραῦσις ἐν τῷ λαῷ τῷ ὀπίσω Ἀβεσσαλώμ·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Behold, he is hid now in some pit, or in some other place: and it will come to pass, when some of them are fallen at the first, that whosoever heareth it will say, There is a slaughter among the people that follow Absalom.
					\item[NIV] Even now, he is hidden in a cave or some other place. If he should attack your troops first, whoever hears about it will say, ‘There has been a slaughter among the troops who follow Absalom.’
					\item[NRSV] Even now he has hidden himself in one of the pits, or in some other place. And when some of our troops fall at the first attack, whoever hears it will say, ‘There has been a slaughter among the troops who follow Absalom.’
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:10} % *2SAMUEL-17-10
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-10}

			And he also that is valiant, whose heart is as the heart of a lion, shall utterly melt: for all Israel knoweth that thy father is a mighty man, and they which be with him are valiant men.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְה֣וּא גַם־בֶּן־חַ֗יִל אֲשֶׁ֥ר לִבּ֛וֹ כְּלֵ֥ב הָאַרְיֵ֖ה הִמֵּ֣ס יִמָּ֑ס כִּֽי־יֹדֵ֤עַ כָּל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ כִּי־גִבּ֣וֹר אָבִ֔יךָ וּבְנֵי־חַ֖יִל אֲשֶׁ֥ר אִתּֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ γε αὐτὸς υἱὸς δυνάμεως, οὗ ἡ καρδία καθὼς ἡ καρδία τοῦ λέοντος, τηκομένη τακήσεται· ὅτι οἶδεν πᾶς Ἰσραὴλ ὅτι δυνατὸς ὁ πατήρ σου καὶ υἱοὶ δυνάμεως οἱ μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And even he that is valiant, whose heart is as the heart of a lion, will utterly melt; for all Israel knoweth that thy father is a mighty man, and they that are with him are valiant men.
					\item[NIV] Then even the bravest soldier, whose heart is like the heart of a lion, will melt with fear, for all Israel knows that your father is a fighter and that those with him are brave.
					\item[NRSV] Then even the valiant warrior, whose heart is like the heart of a lion, will utterly melt with fear; for all Israel knows that your father is a warrior, and that those who are with him are valiant warriors.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:11} % *2SAMUEL-17-11
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-11}

			Therefore I counsel that all Israel be generally gathered unto thee, from Dan even to Beersheba, as the sand that is by the sea for multitude; and that thou go to battle in thine own person.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֣י יָעַ֗צְתִּי הֵ֠אָסֹף יֵאָסֵ֨ף עָלֶ֤יךָ כָל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ מִדָּן֙ וְעַד־בְּאֵ֣ר שֶׁ֔בַע כַּח֥וֹל אֲשֶׁר־עַל־הַיָּ֖ם לָרֹ֑ב וּפָנֶ֥יךָ הֹלְכִ֖ים בַּקְרָֽב׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι οὕτως συμβουλεύων ἐγὼ συνεβούλευσα· καὶ συναγόμενος συναχθήσεται ἐπὶ σὲ πᾶς Ἰσραὴλ ἀπὸ Δὰν καὶ ἕως Βηρσάβεε, ὡς ἡ ἄμμος ἡ ἐπὶ τῆς θαλάσσης εἰς πλῆθος, καὶ τὸ πρόσωπόν σου πορευόμενον ἐν μέσῳ αὐτῶν·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But I counsel that all Israel be gathered together unto thee, from Dan even to Beer-sheba, as the sand that is by the sea for multitude; and that thou go to battle in thine own person.
					\item[NIV] “So I advise you: Let all Israel, from Dan to Beersheba—as numerous as the sand on the seashore—be gathered to you, with you yourself leading them into battle.
					\item[NRSV] But my counsel is that all Israel be gathered to you, from Dan to Beer-sheba, like the sand by the sea for multitude, and that you go to battle in person.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:12} % *2SAMUEL-17-12
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-12}

			So shall we come upon him in some place where he shall be found, and we will light upon him as the dew falleth on the ground: and of him and of all the men that are with him there shall not be left so much as one.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּבָ֣אנוּ אֵלָ֗יו בְּאַחַ֤ד הַמְּקוֹמֹת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִמְצָ֣א שָׁ֔ם וְנַ֣חְנוּ עָלָ֔יו כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר יִפֹּ֥ל הַטַּ֖ל עַל־הָאֲדָמָ֑ה וְלֹֽא־נ֥וֹתַר בּ֛וֹ וּבְכָל־הָאֲנָשִׁ֥ים אֲשֶׁר־אִתּ֖וֹ גַּם־אֶחָֽד׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἥξομεν πρὸς αὐτὸν εἰς ἕνα τῶν τόπων οὗ ἐὰν εὕρωμεν αὐτὸν ἐκεῖ, καὶ παρεμβαλοῦμεν ἐπ᾽ αὐτὸν ὡς πίπτει ἡ δρόσος ἐπὶ τὴν γῆν, καὶ οὐχ ὑπολειψόμεθα ἐν αὐτῷ καὶ τοῖς ἀνδράσιν τοῖς μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ καί γε ἕνα.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So shall we come upon him in some place where he shall be found, and we will light upon him as the dew falleth on the ground; and of him and of all the men that are with him we will not leave so much as one.
					\item[NIV] Then we will attack him wherever he may be found, and we will fall on him as dew settles on the ground. Neither he nor any of his men will be left alive.
					\item[NRSV] So we shall come upon him in whatever place he may be found, and we shall light on him as the dew falls on the ground; and he will not survive, nor will any of those with him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:13} % *2SAMUEL-17-13
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-13}

			Moreover, if he be gotten into a city, then shall all Israel bring ropes to that city, and we will draw it into the river, until there be not one small stone found there.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאִם־אֶל־עִיר֙ יֵֽאָסֵ֔ף וְהִשִּׂ֧יאוּ כָֽל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל אֶל־הָעִ֥יר הַהִ֖יא חֲבָלִ֑ים וְסָחַ֤בְנוּ אֹתוֹ֙ עַד־הַנַּ֔חַל עַ֛ד אֲשֶֽׁר־לֹא־נִמְצָ֥א שָׁ֖ם גַּם־צְרֽוֹר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐὰν εἰς τὴν πόλιν συναχθῇ, καὶ λήμψεται πᾶς Ἰσραὴλ πρὸς τὴν πόλιν ἐκείνην σχοινία καὶ συροῦμεν αὐτὴν ἕως εἰς τὸν χειμάρρουν, ὅπως μὴ καταλειφθῇ ἐκεῖ μηδὲ λίθος.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Moreover, if he be gotten into a city, then shall all Israel bring ropes to that city, and we will draw it into the river, until there be not one small stone found there.
					\item[NIV] If he withdraws into a city, then all Israel will bring ropes to that city, and we will drag it down to the valley until not so much as a pebble is left.”
					\item[NRSV] If he withdraws into a city, then all Israel will bring ropes to that city, and we shall drag it into the valley, until not even a pebble is to be found there.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:14} % *2SAMUEL-17-14
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-14}

			And Absalom and all the men of Israel said, The counsel of Hushai the Archite is better than the counsel of Ahithophel. For the LORD had appointed to defeat the good counsel of Ahithophel, to the intent that the LORD might bring evil upon Absalom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר אַבְשָׁלוֹם֙ וְכָל־אִ֣ישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל טוֹבָ֗ה עֲצַת֙ חוּשַׁ֣י הָאַרְכִּ֔י מֵעֲצַ֖ת אֲחִיתֹ֑פֶל וַיהוָ֣ה צִוָּ֗ה לְהָפֵ֞ר אֶת־עֲצַ֤ת אֲחִיתֹ֙פֶל֙ הַטּוֹבָ֔ה לְבַעֲב֗וּר הָבִ֧יא יְהוָ֛ה אֶל־אַבְשָׁל֖וֹם אֶת־הָרָעָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Ἀβεσσαλὼμ καὶ πᾶς ἀνὴρ Ἰσραήλ Ἀγαθὴ ἡ βουλὴ Χουσεὶ τοῦ Ἀραχεὶ ὑπὲρ τὴν βουλὴν Ἀχειτόφελ τὴν ἀγαθήν· καὶ Κύριος ἐνετείλατο διασκεδάσαι τὴν βουλὴν Ἀχειτόφελ, ὅπως ἂν ἐπαγάγῃ Κύριος ἐπὶ Ἀβεσσαλὼμ τὰ κακὰ πάντα.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Absalom and all the men of Israel said, The counsel of Hushai the Archite is better than the counsel of Ahithophel. For Jehovah had ordained to defeat the good counsel of Ahithophel, to the intent that Jehovah might bring evil upon Absalom.
					\item[NIV] Absalom and all the men of Israel said, “The advice of Hushai the Arkite is better than that of Ahithophel.” For the LORD had determined to frustrate the good advice of Ahithophel in order to bring disaster on Absalom.
					\item[NRSV] Absalom and all the men of Israel said, “The counsel of Hushai the Archite is better than the counsel of Ahithophel.” For the LORD had ordained to defeat the good counsel of Ahithophel, so that the LORD might bring ruin on Absalom.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:15} % *2SAMUEL-17-15
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-15}

			Then said Hushai unto Zadok and to Abiathar the priests, Thus and thus did Ahithophel counsel Absalom and the elders of Israel; and thus and thus have I counselled.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֣אמֶר חוּשַׁ֗י אֶל־צָד֤וֹק וְאֶל־אֶבְיָתָר֙ הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים כָּזֹ֣את וְכָזֹ֗את יָעַ֤ץ אֲחִיתֹ֙פֶל֙ אֶת־אַבְשָׁלֹ֔ם וְאֵ֖ת זִקְנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְכָזֹ֥את וְכָזֹ֖את יָעַ֥צְתִּי אָֽנִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Χουσεὶ ὁ τοῦ Ἀραχεὶ πρὸς Σαδὼκ καὶ Ἀβιαθὰρ τοὺς ἱερεῖς Οὕτως καὶ οὕτως συνεβούλευσεν Ἀχειτόφελ τῷ Ἀβεσσαλὼμ καὶ τοῖς πρεσβυτέροις Ἰσραήλ, καὶ οὕτως καὶ οὕτως συνεβούλευσα ἐγώ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then said Hushai unto Zadok and to Abiathar the priests, Thus and thus did Ahithophel counsel Absalom and the elders of Israel; and thus and thus have I counselled.
					\item[NIV] Hushai told Zadok and Abiathar, the priests, “Ahithophel has advised Absalom and the elders of Israel to do such and such, but I have advised them to do so and so.
					\item[NRSV] Then Hushai said to the priests Zadok and Abiathar, “Thus and so did Ahithophel counsel Absalom and the elders of Israel; and thus and so I have counseled.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:16} % *2SAMUEL-17-16
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-16}

			Now therefore send quickly, and tell David, saying, Lodge not this night in the plains of the wilderness, but speedily pass over; lest the king be swallowed up, and all the people that are with him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְעַתָּ֡ה שִׁלְח֣וּ מְהֵרָה֩ וְהַגִּ֨ידוּ לְדָוִ֜ד לֵאמֹ֗ר אַל־תָּ֤לֶן הַלַּ֙יְלָה֙ בְּעַֽרְב֣וֹת הַמִּדְבָּ֔ר וְגַ֖ם עָב֣וֹר תַּעֲב֑וֹר פֶּ֚ן יְבֻלַּ֣ע לַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וּלְכָל־הָעָ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר אִתּֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ νῦν ἀποστείλατε ταχὺ καὶ ἀναγγείλατε τῷ Δαυεὶδ λέγοντες Μὴ αὐλισθῇς τὴν νύκτα ἐν ἀραβὼθ τῆς ἐρήμου, καί γε διαβαίνων σπεῦσον, μή ποτε καταπείσῃ τὸν βασιλέα καὶ πάντα τὸν λαὸν τὸν μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now therefore send quickly, and tell David, saying, Lodge not this night at the fords of the wilderness, but by all means pass over; lest the king be swallowed up, and all the people that are with him.
					\item[NIV] Now send a message at once and tell David, ‘Do not spend the night at the fords in the wilderness; cross over without fail, or the king and all the people with him will be swallowed up.’”
					\item[NRSV] Therefore send quickly and tell David, ‘Do not lodge tonight at the fords of the wilderness, but by all means cross over; otherwise the king and all the people who are with him will be swallowed up.’”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:17} % *2SAMUEL-17-17
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-17}

			Now Jonathan and Ahimaaz stayed by Enrogel; for they might not be seen to come into the city: and a wench went and told them; and they went and told king David.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וִיהוֹנָתָ֨ן וַאֲחִימַ֜עַץ עֹמְדִ֣ים בְּעֵין־רֹגֵ֗ל וְהָלְכָ֤ה הַשִּׁפְחָה֙ וְהִגִּ֣ידָה לָהֶ֔ם וְהֵם֙ יֵֽלְכ֔וּ וְהִגִּ֖ידוּ לַמֶּ֣לֶךְ דָּוִ֑ד כִּ֣י לֹ֥א יוּכְל֛וּ לְהֵרָא֖וֹת לָב֥וֹא הָעִֽירָה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ Ἰωναθὰν καὶ Ἀχειμὰς εἱστήκεισαν ἐν τῇ πηγῇ Ῥωγήλ, καὶ ἐπορεύθη ἡ παιδίσκη καὶ ἀνήγγειλεν αὐτοῖς· καὶ αὐτοὶ πορεύονται καὶ ἀναγγέλλουσιν τῷ βασιλεῖ Δαυείδ, ὅτι οὐκ ἐδύναντο ὀφθῆναι τοῦ εἰσελθεῖν εἰς τὴν πόλιν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now Jonathan and Ahimaaz were staying by En-rogel; and a maid-servant used to go and tell them; and they went and told king David: for they might not be seen to come into the city.
					\item[NIV] Jonathan and Ahimaaz were staying at En Rogel. A female servant was to go and inform them, and they were to go and tell King David, for they could not risk being seen entering the city.
					\item[NRSV] Jonathan and Ahimaaz were waiting at En-rogel; a servant-girl used to go and tell them, and they would go and tell King David; for they could not risk being seen entering the city.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:18} % *2SAMUEL-17-18
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-18}

			Nevertheless a lad saw them, and told Absalom: but they went both of them away quickly, and came to a man’s house in Bahurim, which had a well in his court; whither they went down.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֤רְא אֹתָם֙ נַ֔עַר וַיַּגֵּ֖ד לְאַבְשָׁלֹ֑ם וַיֵּלְכוּ֩ שְׁנֵיהֶ֨ם מְהֵרָ֜ה וַיָּבֹ֣אוּ ׀ אֶל־בֵּֽית־אִ֣ישׁ בְּבַחוּרִ֗ים וְל֥וֹ בְאֵ֛ר בַּחֲצֵר֖וֹ וַיֵּ֥רְדוּ שָֽׁם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶδεν αὐτοὺς παιδάριον, καὶ ἀπήγγειλαν τῷ Ἀβεσσαλώμ· καὶ ἐπορεύθησαν οἱ δύο ταχέως καὶ εἰσῆλθαν εἰς οἰκίαν ἀνδρὸς ἐν Βαορείμ, καὶ αὐτῷ λάκκος ἐν τῇ αὐλῇ, καὶ κατέβησαν ἐκεῖ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But a lad saw them, and told Absalom: and they went both of them away quickly, and came to the house of a man in Bahurim, who had a well in his court; and they went down thither.
					\item[NIV] But a young man saw them and told Absalom. So the two of them left at once and went to the house of a man in Bahurim. He had a well in his courtyard, and they climbed down into it.
					\item[NRSV] But a boy saw them, and told Absalom; so both of them went away quickly, and came to the house of a man at Bahurim, who had a well in his courtyard; and they went down into it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:19} % *2SAMUEL-17-19
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-19}

			And the woman took and spread a covering over the well’s mouth, and spread ground corn thereon; and the thing was not known.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתִּקַּ֣ח הָאִשָּׁ֗ה וַתִּפְרֹ֤שׂ אֶת־הַמָּסָךְ֙ עַל־פְּנֵ֣י הַבְּאֵ֔ר וַתִּשְׁטַ֥ח עָלָ֖יו הָֽרִפ֑וֹת וְלֹ֥א נוֹדַ֖ע דָּבָֽר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἔλαβεν ἡ γυνὴ καὶ διεπέτασεν τὸ ἐπικάλυμμα ἐπὶ πρόσωπον τοῦ λάκκου καὶ ἔψυξεν ἐπ᾽ αὐτῷ ἀραφώθ, καὶ οὐκ ἐγνώσθη ῥῆμα.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the woman took and spread the covering over the well's mouth, and strewed bruised grain thereon; and nothing was known.
					\item[NIV] His wife took a covering and spread it out over the opening of the well and scattered grain over it. No one knew anything about it.
					\item[NRSV] The man’s wife took a covering, stretched it over the well’s mouth, and spread out grain on it; and nothing was known of it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:20} % *2SAMUEL-17-20
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-20}

			And when Absalom’s servants came to the woman to the house, they said, Where is Ahimaaz and Jonathan? And the woman said unto them, They be gone over the brook of water. And when they had sought and could not find them, they returned to Jerusalem.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּבֹ֣אוּ עַבְדֵי֩ אַבְשָׁל֨וֹם אֶֽל־הָאִשָּׁ֜ה הַבַּ֗יְתָה וַיֹּֽאמְרוּ֙ אַיֵּ֗ה אֲחִימַ֙עַץ֙ וִיה֣וֹנָתָ֔ן וַתֹּ֤אמֶר לָהֶם֙ הָֽאִשָּׁ֔ה עָבְר֖וּ מִיכַ֣ל הַמָּ֑יִם וַיְבַקְשׁוּ֙ וְלֹ֣א מָצָ֔אוּ וַיָּשֻׁ֖בוּ יְרוּשָׁלִָֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἦλθαν οἱ παῖδες Ἀβεσσαλὼμ πρὸς τὴν γυναῖκα εἰς τὴν οἰκίαν καὶ εἶπαν Ποῦ Ἀχειμὰς καὶ Ἰωναθάν; καὶ εἶπεν αὐτοῖς ἡ γυνή Παρῆλθαν μικρὸν τοῦ ὕδατος· καὶ ἐζήτησαν καὶ οὐχ εὗραν, καὶ ἀνέστρεψαν εἰς Ἰερουσαλήμ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Absalom's servants came to the woman to the house; and they said, Where are Ahimaaz and Jonathan? And the woman said unto them, They are gone over the brook of water. And when they had sought and could not find them, they returned to Jerusalem.
					\item[NIV] When Absalom’s men came to the woman at the house, they asked, “Where are Ahimaaz and Jonathan?” The woman answered them, “They crossed over the brook.” The men searched but found no one, so they returned to Jerusalem.
					\item[NRSV] When Absalom’s servants came to the woman at the house, they said, “Where are Ahimaaz and Jonathan?” The woman said to them, “They have crossed over the brook of water.” And when they had searched and could not find them, they returned to Jerusalem.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:21} % *2SAMUEL-17-21
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-21}

			And it came to pass, after they were departed, that they came up out of the well, and went and told king David, and said unto David, Arise, and pass quickly over the water: for thus hath Ahithophel counselled against you.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֣י ׀ אַחֲרֵ֣י לֶכְתָּ֗ם וַֽיַּעֲלוּ֙ מֵֽהַבְּאֵ֔ר וַיֵּ֣לְכ֔וּ וַיַּגִּ֖דוּ לַמֶּ֣לֶךְ דָּוִ֑ד וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ אֶל־דָּוִ֗ד ק֣וּמוּ וְעִבְר֤וּ מְהֵרָה֙ אֶת־הַמַּ֔יִם כִּי־כָ֛כָה יָעַ֥ץ עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם אֲחִיתֹֽפֶל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐγένετο δὲ μετὰ τὸ ἀπελθεῖν αὐτοὺς καὶ ἀνέβησαν ἐκ τοῦ λάκκου, καὶ ἐπορεύθησαν καὶ ἀνήγγειλαν τῷ βασιλεῖ Δαυείδ, καὶ εἶπαν πρὸς Δαυείδ Ἀνάστητε καὶ διάβητε ταχέως τὸ ὕδωρ, ὅτι οὕτως ἐβουλεύσατο περὶ ὑμῶν Ἀχειτόφελ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And it came to pass, after they were departed, that they came up out of the well, and went and told king David; and they said unto David, Arise ye, and pass quickly over the water; for thus hath Ahithophel counselled against you.
					\item[NIV] After they had gone, the two climbed out of the well and went to inform King David. They said to him, “Set out and cross the river at once; Ahithophel has advised such and such against you.”
					\item[NRSV] After they had gone, the men came up out of the well, and went and told King David. They said to David, “Go and cross the water quickly; for thus and so has Ahithophel counseled against you.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:22} % *2SAMUEL-17-22
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-22}

			Then David arose, and all the people that were with him, and they passed over Jordan: by the morning light there lacked not one of them that was not gone over Jordan.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּ֣קָם דָּוִ֗ד וְכָל־הָעָם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אִתּ֔וֹ וַיַּעַבְר֖וּ אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֑ן עַד־א֣וֹר הַבֹּ֗קֶר עַד־אַחַד֙ לֹ֣א נֶעְדָּ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־עָבַ֖ר אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּֽן׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀνέστη Δαυεὶδ καὶ πᾶς ὁ λαὸς ὁ μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ καὶ διέβησαν τὸν Ἰορδάνην ἕως τοῦ φωτὸς τοῦ πρωί, ἕως ἑνὸς οὐκ ἔλαθεν ὃς οὐ διῆλθεν τὸν Ἰορδάνην.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then David arose, and all the people that were with him, and they passed over the Jordan: by the morning light there lacked not one of them that was not gone over the Jordan.
					\item[NIV] So David and all the people with him set out and crossed the Jordan. By daybreak, no one was left who had not crossed the Jordan.
					\item[NRSV] So David and all the people who were with him set out and crossed the Jordan; by daybreak not one was left who had not crossed the Jordan.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:23} % *2SAMUEL-17-23
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-23}

			And when Ahithophel saw that his counsel was not followed, he saddled his ass, and arose, and gat him home to his house, to his city, and put his household in order, and hanged himself, and died, and was buried in the sepulchre of his father.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַאֲחִיתֹ֣פֶל רָאָ֗ה כִּ֣י לֹ֣א נֶעֶשְׂתָה֮ עֲצָתוֹ֒ וַיַּחֲבֹ֣שׁ אֶֽת־הַחֲמ֗וֹר וַיָּ֜קָם וַיֵּ֤לֶךְ אֶל־בֵּיתוֹ֙ אֶל־עִיר֔וֹ וַיְצַ֥ו אֶל־בֵּית֖וֹ וַיֵּחָנַ֑ק וַיָּ֕מָת וַיִּקָּבֵ֖ר בְּקֶ֥בֶר אָבִֽיו׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ Ἀχειτόφελ εἶδεν ὅτι οὐκ ἐγενήθη ἡ βουλὴ αὐτοῦ, καὶ ἐπέσαξεν τὴν ὄνον αὐτοῦ καὶ ἀνέστη καὶ ἀπῆλθεν εἰς τὸν οἶκον αὐτοῦ εἰς τὴν πόλιν αὐτοῦ· καὶ ἐνετείλατο τῷ οἴκῳ αὐτοῦ, καὶ ἀπήγξατο καὶ ἀπέθανεν, καὶ ἐτάφη ἐν τῷ τάφῳ τοῦ πατρὸς αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And when Ahithophel saw that his counsel was not followed, he saddled his ass, and arose, and gat him home, unto his city, and set his house in order, and hanged himself; and he died, and was buried in the sepulchre of his father.
					\item[NIV] When Ahithophel saw that his advice had not been followed, he saddled his donkey and set out for his house in his hometown. He put his house in order and then hanged himself. So he died and was buried in his father’s tomb.
					\item[NRSV] When Ahithophel saw that his counsel was not followed, he saddled his donkey and went off home to his own city. He set his house in order, and hanged himself; he died and was buried in the tomb of his father.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:24} % *2SAMUEL-17-24
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-24}

			Then David came to Mahanaim. And Absalom passed over Jordan, he and all the men of Israel with him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְדָוִ֖ד בָּ֣א מַחֲנָ֑יְמָה וְאַבְשָׁלֹ֗ם עָבַר֙ אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֔ן ה֕וּא וְכָל־אִ֥ישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל עִמּֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ Δαυεὶδ διῆλθεν εἰς Μανάειμ, καὶ Ἀβεσσαλὼμ διέβη τὸν Ἰορδάνην αὐτὸς καὶ πᾶς ἀνὴρ Ἰσραὴλ μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then David came to Mahanaim. And Absalom passed over the Jordan, he and all the men of Israel with him.
					\item[NIV] David went to Mahanaim, and Absalom crossed the Jordan with all the men of Israel.
					\item[NRSV] Then David came to Mahanaim, while Absalom crossed the Jordan with all the men of Israel.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:25} % *2SAMUEL-17-25
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-25}

			And Absalom made Amasa captain of the host instead of Joab: which Amasa was a man’s son, whose name was Ithra an Israelite, that went in to Abigail the daughter of Nahash, sister to Zeruiah Joab’s mother.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאֶת־עֲמָשָׂ֗א שָׂ֧ם אַבְשָׁלֹ֛ם תַּ֥חַת יוֹאָ֖ב עַל־הַצָּבָ֑א וַעֲמָשָׂ֣א בֶן־אִ֗ישׁ וּשְׁמוֹ֙ יִתְרָ֣א הַיִּשְׂרְאֵלִ֔י אֲשֶׁר־בָּא֙ אֶל־אֲבִיגַ֣ל בַּת־נָחָ֔שׁ אֲח֥וֹת צְרוּיָ֖ה אֵ֥ם יוֹאָֽב׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ τὸν Ἀμεσσεὶ κατέστησεν Ἀβεσσαλὼμ ἀντὶ Ἰωὰβ ἐπὶ τῆς δυνάμεως· καὶ Ἀμεσσεὶ υἱὸς ἀνδρὸς καὶ ὄνομα αὐτῷ Ἰοθὸρ ὁ Ἰσραηλείτης, οὗτος εἰσῆλθεν πρὸς Ἀβειγαίαν θυγατέρα Ναὰς ἀδελφοῦ Σαρουίας μητρὸς Ἰωάβ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Absalom set Amasa over the host instead of Joab. Now Amasa was the son of a man, whose name was Ithra the Israelite, that went in to Abigail the daughter of Nahash, sister to Zeruiah, Joab's mother.
					\item[NIV] Absalom had appointed Amasa over the army in place of Joab. Amasa was the son of Jether, an Ishmaelite who had married Abigail, the daughter of Nahash and sister of Zeruiah the mother of Joab.
					\item[NRSV] Now Absalom had set Amasa over the army in the place of Joab. Amasa was the son of a man named Ithra the Ishmaelite, who had married Abigal daughter of Nahash, sister of Zeruiah, Joab’s mother.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:26} % *2SAMUEL-17-26
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-26}

			So Israel and Absalom pitched in the land of Gilead.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּ֤חַן יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וְאַבְשָׁלֹ֔ם אֶ֖רֶץ הַגִּלְעָֽד׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ παρενέβαλεν πᾶς Ἰσραὴλ καὶ Ἀβεσσαλὼμ εἰς τὴν γῆν Γαλαάδ.}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Israel and Absalom encamped in the land of Gilead.
					\item[NIV] The Israelites and Absalom camped in the land of Gilead.
					\item[NRSV] The Israelites and Absalom encamped in the land of Gilead.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:27} % *2SAMUEL-17-27
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-27}

			And it came to pass, when David was come to Mahanaim, that Shobi the son of Nahash of Rabbah of the children of Ammon, and Machir the son of Ammiel of Lodebar, and Barzillai the Gileadite of Rogelim,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֕י כְּב֥וֹא דָוִ֖ד מַחֲנָ֑יְמָה וְשֹׁבִ֨י בֶן־נָחָ֜שׁ מֵרַבַּ֣ת בְּנֵֽי־עַמּ֗וֹן וּמָכִ֤יר בֶּן־עַמִּיאֵל֙ מִלֹּ֣א דְבָ֔ר וּבַרְזִלַּ֥י הַגִּלְעָדִ֖י מֵרֹגְלִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐγένετο ἡνίκα ἦλθεν Δαυεὶδ εἰς Μαανάειμ, Οὐεσβεὶ υἱὸς Ναὰς ἐκ Ῥαβὰθ υἱῶν Ἀμμὼν καὶ Μαχεὶρ υἱὸς Ἀμειὴλ ἐκ Λωδαβὰρ καὶ Βερζελλεὶ ὁ Γαλααδείτης ἐκ Ῥωγελλεὶμ}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And it came to pass, when David was come to Mahanaim, that Shobi the son of Nahash of Rabbah of the children of Ammon, and Machir the son of Ammiel of Lodebar, and Barzillai the Gileadite of Rogelim,
					\item[NIV] When David came to Mahanaim, Shobi son of Nahash from Rabbah of the Ammonites, and Makir son of Ammiel from Lo Debar, and Barzillai the Gileadite from Rogelim
					\item[NRSV] When David came to Mahanaim, Shobi son of Nahash from Rabbah of the Ammonites, and Machir son of Ammiel from Lo-debar, and Barzillai the Gileadite from Rogelim,

		\paragraph{2 Samuel 17:28} % *2SAMUEL-17-28
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-17-28}

			Brought beds, and basons, and earthen vessels, and wheat, and barley, and flour, and parched corn, and beans, and lentiles, and parched pulse,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִשְׁכָּ֤ב וְסַפּוֹת֙ וּכְלִ֣י יוֹצֵ֔ר וְחִטִּ֥ים וּשְׂעֹרִ֖ים וְקֶ֣מַח וְקָלִ֑י וּפ֥וֹל וַעֲדָשִׁ֖ים וְקָלִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἤνεγκαν δέκα κοίτας καὶ ἀμφιτάπους καὶ λέβητας δέκα καὶ σκεύη κεράμου, καὶ πυροὺς καὶ κριθὰς καὶ ἄλευρον καὶ ἄλφιτον, καὶ κύαμον καὶ φακὸν}

					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NRSV] brought beds, basins, and earthen vessels, wheat, barley, meal, parched grain, beans and lentils,

					% % \item[OTXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
