		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:21} % *2SAMUEL-19-21
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-21}

			But Abishai the son of Zeruiah answered and said, Shall not Shimei be put to death for this, because he cursed the LORD’s anointed?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֨עַן אֲבִישַׁ֤י בֶּן־צְרוּיָה֙ וַיֹּ֔אמֶר הֲתַ֣חַת זֹ֔את לֹ֥א יוּמַ֖ת שִׁמְעִ֑י כִּ֥י קִלֵּ֖ל אֶת־מְשִׁ֥יחַ יְהוָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀπεκρίθη Ἀβεισὰ υἱὸς Σαρουίας καὶ εἶπεν Μὴ ἀντὶ τούτου οὐ θανατωθήσεται Σεμεεί, ὅτι κατηράσατο τὸν χριστὸν Κυρίου;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But Abishai the son of Zeruiah answered and said, Shall not Shimei be put to death for this, because he cursed Jehovah's anointed?
					\item[NIV] Then Abishai son of Zeruiah said, “Shouldn’t Shimei be put to death for this? He cursed the LORD’s anointed.”
					\item[NRSV] Abishai son of Zeruiah answered, “Shall not Shimei be put to death for this, because he cursed the LORD’s anointed?”

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:22} % *2SAMUEL-19-22
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-22}

			And David said, What have I to do with you, ye sons of Zeruiah, that ye should this day be adversaries unto me? shall there any man be put to death this day in Israel? for do not I know that I am this day king over Israel?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֣אמֶר דָּוִ֗ד מַה־לִּ֤י וְלָכֶם֙ בְּנֵ֣י צְרוּיָ֔ה כִּי־תִֽהְיוּ־לִ֥י הַיּ֖וֹם לְשָׂטָ֑ן הַיּ֗וֹם י֤וּמַת אִישׁ֙ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כִּ֚י הֲל֣וֹא יָדַ֔עְתִּי כִּ֥י הַיּ֖וֹם אֲנִי־מֶ֥לֶךְ עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Δαυείδ Τί ἐμοὶ καὶ ὑμῖν, υἱοὶ Σαρουίας, ὅτι γίνεσθέ, μοι σήμερον εἰς ἐπίβουλον; σήμερον οὐ θανατωθήσεταί τις ἀνὴρ ἐξ Ἰσραήλ, ὅτι οὐκ οἶδα εἰ σήμερον βασιλεύω ἐγὼ ἐπὶ τὸν Ἰσραήλ;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David said, What have I to do with you, ye sons of Zeruiah, that ye should this day be adversaries unto me? shall there any man be put to death this day in Israel? for do not I know that I am this day king over Israel?
					\item[NIV] David replied, “What does this have to do with you, you sons of Zeruiah? What right do you have to interfere? Should anyone be put to death in Israel today? Don’t I know that today I am king over Israel?”
					\item[NRSV] But David said, “What have I to do with you, you sons of Zeruiah, that you should today become an adversary to me? Shall anyone be put to death in Israel this day? For do I not know that I am this day king over Israel?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:23} % *2SAMUEL-19-23
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-23}

			Therefore the king said unto Shimei, Thou shalt not die. And the king sware unto him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֧אמֶר הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ אֶל־שִׁמְעִ֖י לֹ֣א תָמ֑וּת וַיִּשָּׁ֥בַֽע ל֖וֹ הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν ὁ βασιλεὺς πρὸς Σεμεεί Οὐ μὴ ἀποθάνῃς· καὶ ὤμοσεν αὐτῷ ὁ βασιλεύς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the king said unto Shimei, Thou shalt not die. And the king sware unto him.
					\item[NIV] So the king said to Shimei, “You shall not die.” And the king promised him on oath.
					\item[NRSV] The king said to Shimei, “You shall not die.” And the king gave him his oath.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:24} % *2SAMUEL-19-24
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-24}

			And Mephibosheth the son of Saul came down to meet the king, and had neither dressed his feet, nor trimmed his beard, nor washed his clothes, from the day the king departed until the day he came again in peace.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּמְפִבֹ֙שֶׁת֙ בֶּן־שָׁא֔וּל יָרַ֖ד לִקְרַ֣את הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וְלֹא־עָשָׂ֨ה רַגְלָ֜יו וְלֹא־עָשָׂ֣ה שְׂפָמ֗וֹ וְאֶת־בְּגָדָיו֙ לֹ֣א כִבֵּ֔ס לְמִן־הַיּוֹם֙ לֶ֣כֶת הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ עַד־הַיּ֖וֹם אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֥א בְשָׁלֽוֹם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ Μεμφιβόσθε υἱὸς υἱοῦ Σαοὺλ κατέβη εἰς ἀπαντὴν τοῦ βασιλέως, καὶ οὐκ ἐθεράπευσεν τοὺς πόδας αὐτοῦ οὐδὲ ὠνυχίσατο οὐδὲ ἐποίησεν τὸν μύστακα αὐτοῦ καὶ τὰ ἱμάτια αὐτοῦ οὐκ ἀπέπλυνεν ἀπὸ τῆς ἡμέρας ἧς ἀπῆλθεν ὁ βασιλεὺς ἕως τῆς ἡμέρας ἧς αὐτὸς παρεγένετο ἐν εἰρήνῃ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Mephibosheth the son of Saul came down to meet the king; and he had neither dressed his feet, nor trimmed his beard, nor washed his clothes, from the day the king departed until the day he came home in peace.
					\item[NIV] Mephibosheth, Saul’s grandson, also went down to meet the king. He had not taken care of his feet or trimmed his mustache or washed his clothes from the day the king left until the day he returned safely.
					\item[NRSV] Mephibosheth grandson of Saul came down to meet the king; he had not taken care of his feet, or trimmed his beard, or washed his clothes, from the day the king left until the day he came back in safety.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:25} % *2SAMUEL-19-25
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-25}

			And it came to pass, when he was come to Jerusalem to meet the king, that the king said unto him, Wherefore wentest not thou with me, Mephibosheth?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֛י כִּי־בָ֥א יְרוּשָׁלִַ֖ם לִקְרַ֣את הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיֹּ֤אמֶר לוֹ֙ הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ לָ֛מָּה לֹא־הָלַ֥כְתָּ עִמִּ֖י מְפִיבֹֽשֶׁת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐγένετο ὅτε εἰσῆλθεν εἰς Ἰερουσαλὴμ εἰς ἀπάντησιν τοῦ βασιλέως, καὶ εἶπεν αὐτῷ ὁ βασιλεύς Τί ὅτι οὐκ ἐπορεύθης μετ᾽ ἐμοῦ, Μεμφιβόσθε;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And it came to pass, when he was come to Jerusalem to meet the king, that the king said unto him, Wherefore wentest not thou with me, Mephibosheth?
					\item[NIV] When he came from Jerusalem to meet the king, the king asked him, “Why didn’t you go with me, Mephibosheth?”
					\item[NRSV] When he came from Jerusalem to meet the king, the king said to him, “Why did you not go with me, Mephibosheth?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:26} % *2SAMUEL-19-26
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-26}

			And he answered, My lord, O king, my servant deceived me: for thy servant said, I will saddle me an ass, that I may ride thereon, and go to the king; because thy servant is lame.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּאמַ֕ר אֲדֹנִ֥י הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ עַבְדִּ֣י רִמָּ֑נִי כִּֽי־אָמַ֨ר עַבְדְּךָ֜ אֶחְבְּשָׁה־לִּי֩ הַחֲמ֨וֹר וְאֶרְכַּ֤ב עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ וְאֵלֵ֣ךְ אֶת־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ כִּ֥י פִסֵּ֖חַ עַבְדֶּֽךָ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν πρὸς αὐτὸν Μεμφιβόσθε Κύριέ μου βασιλεῦ, ὁ δοῦλός σου παρελογίσατό με· ὅτι εἶπεν ὁ παῖς σου αὐτῷ Ἐπίσαξόν μοι τὴν ὄνον καὶ ἐπιβῶ ἐπ᾽ αὐτὴν καὶ πορεύσομαι μετὰ τοῦ βασιλέως, ὅτι χωλὸς ὁ δοῦλός σου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he answered, My lord, O king, my servant deceived me: for thy servant said, I will saddle me an ass, that I may ride thereon, and go with the king; because thy servant is lame.
					\item[NIV] He said, “My lord the king, since I your servant am lame, I said, ‘I will have my donkey saddled and will ride on it, so I can go with the king.’ But Ziba my servant betrayed me.
					\item[NRSV] He answered, “My lord, O king, my servant deceived me; for your servant said to him, ‘Saddle a donkey for me, so that I may ride on it and go with the king.’ For your servant is lame.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:27} % *2SAMUEL-19-27
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-27}

			And he hath slandered thy servant unto my lord the king; but my lord the king is as an angel of God: do therefore what is good in thine eyes.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְרַגֵּ֣ל בְּעַבְדְּךָ֔ אֶל־אֲדֹנִ֖י הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַאדֹנִ֤י הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ כְּמַלְאַ֣ךְ הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים וַעֲשֵׂ֥ה הַטּ֖וֹב בְּעֵינֶֽיךָ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ μεθώδευσεν ὁ δοῦλός σου πρὸς τὸν κύριόν μου, πρὸς τὸν βασιλέα· καὶ ὁ κύριός μου ὁ βασιλεὺς ἐποίησεν τὸ καλὸν ἐνώπιον ὡς ἄγγελος τοῦ θεοῦ. καὶ ποίησον τὸ ἀγαθὸν ἐν ὀφθαλμοῖς σου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he hath slandered thy servant unto my lord the king; but my lord the king is as an angel of God: do therefore what is good in thine eyes.
					\item[NIV] And he has slandered your servant to my lord the king. My lord the king is like an angel of God; so do whatever you wish.
					\item[NRSV] He has slandered your servant to my lord the king. But my lord the king is like the angel of God; do therefore what seems good to you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:28} % *2SAMUEL-19-28
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-28}

			For all of my father’s house were but dead men before my lord the king: yet didst thou set thy servant among them that did eat at thine own table. What right therefore have I yet to cry any more unto the king?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּי֩ לֹ֨א הָיָ֜ה כָּל־בֵּ֣ית אָבִ֗י כִּ֤י אִם־אַנְשֵׁי־מָ֙וֶת֙ לַאדֹנִ֣י הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וַתָּ֙שֶׁת֙ אֶֽת־עַבְדְּךָ֔ בְּאֹכְלֵ֖י שֻׁלְחָנֶ֑ךָ וּמַה־יֶּשׁ־לִ֥י עוֹד֙ צְדָקָ֔ה וְלִזְעֹ֥ק ע֖וֹד אֶל־הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι οὐκ ἦν πᾶς ὁ οἶκος τοῦ πατρός μου ἀλλ᾽ ἢ ὅτι ἄνδρες θανάτου τῷ κυρίῳ μου τῷ βασιλεῖ, καὶ ἔθηκας τὸν δοῦλόν σου ἐν τοῖς ἐσθίουσιν τὴν τράπεζάν σου· καὶ τί ἐστιν μοι ἔτι δικαίωμα καὶ τοῦ κεκραγέναι με ἔτι πρὸς τὸν βασιλέα;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For all my father's house were but dead men before my lord the king; yet didst thou set thy servant among them that did eat at thine own table. What right therefore have I yet that I should cry any more unto the king?
					\item[NIV] All my grandfather’s descendants deserved nothing but death from my lord the king, but you gave your servant a place among those who eat at your table. So what right do I have to make any more appeals to the king?”
					\item[NRSV] For all my father’s house were doomed to death before my lord the king; but you set your servant among those who eat at your table. What further right have I, then, to appeal to the king?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:29} % *2SAMUEL-19-29
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-29}

			And the king said unto him, Why speakest thou any more of thy matters? I have said, Thou and Ziba divide the land.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר לוֹ֙ הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ לָ֛מָּה תְּדַבֵּ֥ר ע֖וֹד דְּבָרֶ֑יךָ אָמַ֕רְתִּי אַתָּ֣ה וְצִיבָ֔א תַּחְלְק֖וּ אֶת־הַשָּׂדֶֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν αὐτῷ ὁ βασιλεύς Ἵνα τί λαλεῖς ἔτι τοὺς λόγους σου; εἶπον Σὺ καὶ Σειβὰ διελεῖσθε τὸν ἀγρόν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the king said unto him, Why speakest thou any more of thy matters? I say, Thou and Ziba divide the land.
					\item[NIV] The king said to him, “Why say more? I order you and Ziba to divide the land.”
					\item[NRSV] The king said to him, “Why speak any more of your affairs? I have decided: you and Ziba shall divide the land.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:30} % *2SAMUEL-19-30
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-30}

			And Mephibosheth said unto the king, Yea, let him take all, forasmuch as my lord the king is come again in peace unto his own house.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר מְפִיבֹ֙שֶׁת֙ אֶל־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ גַּ֥ם אֶת־הַכֹּ֖ל יִקָּ֑ח אַ֠חֲרֵי אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֞א אֲדֹנִ֥י הַמֶּ֛לֶך בְּשָׁל֖וֹם אֶל־בֵּיתֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Μεμφιβόσθε πρὸς τὸν βασιλέα Καί γε τὰ πάντα λαβέτω μετὰ τὸ παραγενέσθαι τὸν κύριόν μου τὸν βασιλέα ἐν εἰρήνῃ εἰς τὸν οἶκον αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Mephibosheth said unto the king, yea, let him take all, forasmuch as my lord the king is come in peace unto his own house.
					\item[NIV] Mephibosheth said to the king, “Let him take everything, now that my lord the king has returned home safely.”
					\item[NRSV] Mephibosheth said to the king, “Let him take it all, since my lord the king has arrived home safely.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:31} % *2SAMUEL-19-31
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-31}

			And Barzillai the Gileadite came down from Rogelim, and went over Jordan with the king, to conduct him over Jordan.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּבַרְזִלַּי֙ הַגִּלְעָדִ֔י יָרַ֖ד מֵרֹגְלִ֑ים וַיַּעֲבֹ֤ר אֶת־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ הַיַּרְדֵּ֔ן לְשַׁלְּח֖וֹ הַיַּרְדֵּֽן׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ Βερζελλεὶ ὁ Γαλααδείτης κατέβη ἐκ Ῥωγελλεὶμ καὶ διέβη μετὰ τοῦ βασιλέως τὸν Ἰορδάνην ἐκπέμψαι αὐτὸν τὸν Ἰορδάνην.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Barzillai the Gileadite came down from Rogelim; and he went over the Jordan with the king, to conduct him over the Jordan.
					\item[NIV] Barzillai the Gileadite also came down from Rogelim to cross the Jordan with the king and to send him on his way from there.
					\item[NRSV] Now Barzillai the Gileadite had come down from Rogelim; he went on with the king to the Jordan, to escort him over the Jordan.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:32} % *2SAMUEL-19-32
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-32}

			Now Barzillai was a very aged man, even fourscore years old: and he had provided the king of sustenance while he lay at Mahanaim; for he was a very great man.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּבַרְזִלַּי֙ זָקֵ֣ן מְאֹ֔ד בֶּן־שְׁמֹנִ֖ים שָׁנָ֑ה וְהֽוּא־כִלְכַּ֤ל אֶת־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ בְּשִׁיבָת֣וֹ בְמַחֲנַ֔יִם כִּֽי־אִ֛ישׁ גָּד֥וֹל ה֖וּא מְאֹֽד׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ Βερζελλεὶ ἀνὴρ πρεσβύτερος σφόδρα, υἱὸς ὀγδοήκοντα ἐτῶν, καὶ αὐτὸς διέθρεψεν τὸν βασιλέα ἐν τῷ οἰκεῖν αὐτὸν ἐν Μανάειμ, ὅτι ἀνὴρ μέγας ἐστὶν σφόδρα.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now Barzillai was a very aged man, even fourscore years old: and he had provided the king with sustenance while he lay at Mahanaim; for he was a very great man.
					\item[NIV] Now Barzillai was very old, eighty years of age. He had provided for the king during his stay in Mahanaim, for he was a very wealthy man.
					\item[NRSV] Barzillai was a very aged man, eighty years old. He had provided the king with food while he stayed at Mahanaim, for he was a very wealthy man.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:33} % *2SAMUEL-19-33
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-33}

			And the king said unto Barzillai, Come thou over with me, and I will feed thee with me in Jerusalem.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֥אמֶר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ אֶל־בַּרְזִלָּ֑י אַתָּה֙ עֲבֹ֣ר אִתִּ֔י וְכִלְכַּלְתִּ֥י אֹתְךָ֛ עִמָּדִ֖י בִּירוּשָׁלִָֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν ὁ βασιλεὺς πρὸς Βερζελλεί Σὺ διαβήσῃ μετ᾽ ἐμοῦ, καὶ διαθρέψω τὸ γῆράς σου μετ᾽ ἐμοῦ ἐν Ἰερουσαλήμ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the king said unto Barzillai, Come thou over with me, and I will sustain thee with me in Jerusalem.
					\item[NIV] The king said to Barzillai, “Cross over with me and stay with me in Jerusalem, and I will provide for you.”
					\item[NRSV] The king said to Barzillai, “Come over with me, and I will provide for you in Jerusalem at my side.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:34} % *2SAMUEL-19-34
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-34}

			And Barzillai said unto the king, How long have I to live, that I should go up with the king unto Jerusalem?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֥אמֶר בַּרְזִלַּ֖י אֶל־הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ כַּמָּ֗ה יְמֵי֙ שְׁנֵ֣י חַיַּ֔י כִּי־אֶעֱלֶ֥ה אֶת־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ יְרוּשָׁלִָֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Βερζελλεὶ πρὸς τὸν βασιλέα Πόσαι ἡμέραι ἡμερῶν ζωῆς μου, ὅτι ἀναβήσομαι μετὰ τοῦ βασιλέως εἰς Ἰερουσαλήμ;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Barzillai said unto the king, How many are the days of the years of my life, that I should go up with the king unto Jerusalem?
					\item[NIV] But Barzillai answered the king, “How many more years will I live, that I should go up to Jerusalem with the king?
					\item[NRSV] But Barzillai said to the king, “How many years have I still to live, that I should go up with the king to Jerusalem?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:35} % *2SAMUEL-19-35
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-35}

			I am this day fourscore years old: and can I discern between good and evil? can thy servant taste what I eat or what I drink? can I hear any more the voice of singing men and singing women? wherefore then should thy servant be yet a burden unto my lord the king?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בֶּן־שְׁמֹנִ֣ים שָׁנָה֩ אָנֹכִ֨י הַיּ֜וֹם הַאֵדַ֣ע ׀ בֵּין־ט֣וֹב לְרָ֗ע אִם־יִטְעַ֤ם עַבְדְּךָ֙ אֶת־אֲשֶׁ֤ר אֹכַל֙ וְאֶת־אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֶשְׁתֶּ֔ה אִם־אֶשְׁמַ֣ע ע֔וֹד בְּק֖וֹל שָׁרִ֣ים וְשָׁר֑וֹת וְלָמָּה֩ יִֽהְיֶ֨ה עַבְדְּךָ֥ עוֹד֙ לְמַשָּׂ֔א אֶל־אֲדֹנִ֖י הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{υἱὸς ὀγδοήκοντα ἐτῶν ἐγώ εἰμι σήμερον· εἰ μὴν γνώσομαι ἀνὰ μέσον ἀγαθοῦ καὶ κακοῦ εἰς πονηρόν; εἰ γεύσεται ὁ δοῦλός σου ἔτι ὃ φάγομαι ἢ πίομαι; ἢ ἀκούσομαι ἔτι φωνὴν ᾀδόντων καὶ ᾀδουσῶν; ἵνα τί ἔσται ἔτι ὁ δοῦλός σου εἰς φορτίον ἐπὶ τὸν κύριόν μου τὸν βασιλέα;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I am this day fourscore years old: can I discern between good and bad? can thy servant taste what I eat or what I drink? can I hear any more the voice of singing men and singing women? wherefore then should thy servant be yet a burden unto my lord the king?
					\item[NIV] I am now eighty years old. Can I tell the difference between what is enjoyable and what is not? Can your servant taste what he eats and drinks? Can I still hear the voices of male and female singers? Why should your servant be an added burden to my lord the king?
					\item[NRSV] Today I am eighty years old; can I discern what is pleasant and what is not? Can your servant taste what he eats or what he drinks? Can I still listen to the voice of singing men and singing women? Why then should your servant be an added burden to my lord the king?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:36} % *2SAMUEL-19-36
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-36}

			Thy servant will go a little way over Jordan with the king: and why should the king recompense it me with such a reward?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּמְעַ֞ט יַעֲבֹ֧ר עַבְדְּךָ֛ אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֖ן אֶת־הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וְלָ֙מָּה֙ יִגְמְלֵ֣נִי הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ הַגְּמוּלָ֖ה הַזֹּֽאת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὡς βραχὺ διαβήσεται ὁ δοῦλός σου τὸν Ἰορδάνην μετὰ τοῦ βασιλέως· καὶ ἵνα τί ἀνταποδίδωσίν μοι ὁ βασιλεὺς τὴν ἀνταπόδοσιν ταύτην;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Thy servant would but just go over the Jordan with the king: and why should the king recompense it me with such a reward?
					\item[NIV] Your servant will cross over the Jordan with the king for a short distance, but why should the king reward me in this way?
					\item[NRSV] Your servant will go a little way over the Jordan with the king. Why should the king recompense me with such a reward?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:37} % *2SAMUEL-19-37
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-37}

			Let thy servant, I pray thee, turn back again, that I may die in mine own city, and be buried by the grave of my father and of my mother. But behold thy servant Chimham; let him go over with my lord the king; and do to him what shall seem good unto thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יָֽשָׁב־נָ֤א עַבְדְּךָ֙ וְאָמֻ֣ת בְּעִירִ֔י עִ֛ם קֶ֥בֶר אָבִ֖י וְאִמִּ֑י וְהִנֵּ֣ה ׀ עַבְדְּךָ֣ כִמְהָ֗ם יַֽעֲבֹר֙ עִם־אֲדֹנִ֣י הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וַעֲשֵׂה־ל֕וֹ אֵ֥ת אֲשֶׁר־ט֖וֹב בְּעֵינֶֽיךָ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καθισάτω δὴ ὁ δοῦλός σου καὶ ἀποθανοῦμαι ἐν τῇ πόλει μου παρὰ τῷ τάφῳ τοῦ πατρός μου καὶ τῆς μητρός μου· καὶ ἰδοὺ ὁ δοῦλός σου Χαμαὰμ διαβήσεται μετὰ τοῦ κυρίου μου τοῦ βασιλέως, καὶ ποίησον αὐτῷ τὸ ἀγαθὸν ἐν ὀφθαλμοῖς σου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let thy servant, I pray thee, turn back again, that I may die in mine own city, by the grave of my father and my mother. But behold, thy servant Chimham; let him go over with my lord the king; and do to him what shall seem good unto thee.
					\item[NIV] Let your servant return, that I may die in my own town near the tomb of my father and mother. But here is your servant Kimham. Let him cross over with my lord the king. Do for him whatever you wish.”
					\item[NRSV] Please let your servant return, so that I may die in my own town, near the graves of my father and my mother. But here is your servant Chimham; let him go over with my lord the king; and do for him whatever seems good to you.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:38} % *2SAMUEL-19-38
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-38}

			And the king answered, Chimham shall go over with me, and I will do to him that which shall seem good unto thee: and whatsoever thou shalt require of me, that will I do for thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֣אמֶר הַמֶּ֗לֶךְ אִתִּי֙ יַעֲבֹ֣ר כִּמְהָ֔ם וַאֲנִי֙ אֶעֱשֶׂה־לּ֔וֹ אֶת־הַטּ֖וֹב בְּעֵינֶ֑יךָ וְכֹ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־תִּבְחַ֥ר עָלַ֖י אֶֽעֱשֶׂה־לָּֽךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν ὁ βασιλεύς Μετ᾽ ἐμοῦ διαβήτω Χαμαάμ, κἀγὼ ποιήσω αὐτῷ τὸ ἀγαθὸν ἐν ὀφθαλμοῖς σου, καὶ πάντα ὅσα ἐκλέξῃ ἐπ᾽ ἐμοὶ ποιήσω σοι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the king answered, Chimham shall go over with me, and I will do to him that which shall seem good unto thee: and whatsoever thou shalt require of me, that will I do for thee.
					\item[NIV] The king said, “Kimham shall cross over with me, and I will do for him whatever you wish. And anything you desire from me I will do for you.”
					\item[NRSV] The king answered, “Chimham shall go over with me, and I will do for him whatever seems good to you; and all that you desire of me I will do for you.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:39} % *2SAMUEL-19-39
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-39}

			And all the people went over Jordan. And when the king was come over, the king kissed Barzillai, and blessed him; and he returned unto his own place.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּעֲבֹ֧ר כָּל־הָעָ֛ם אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֖ן וְהַמֶּ֣לֶךְ עָבָ֑ר וַיִּשַּׁ֨ק הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ לְבַרְזִלַּי֙ וַיְבָ֣רֲכֵ֔הוּ וַיָּ֖שָׁב לִמְקֹמֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ διέβη πᾶς ὁ λαὸς τὸν Ἰορδάνην, καὶ ὁ βασιλεὺς διέβη· καὶ κατεφίλησεν ὁ βασιλεὺς τὸν Βερζελλεὶ καὶ εὐλόγησεν αὐτόν, καὶ ἐπέστρεψεν εἰς τὸν τόπον αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And all the people went over the Jordan, and the king went over: and the king kissed Barzillai, and blessed him; and he returned unto his own place.
					\item[NIV] So all the people crossed the Jordan, and then the king crossed over. The king kissed Barzillai and bid him farewell, and Barzillai returned to his home.
					\item[NRSV] Then all the people crossed over the Jordan, and the king crossed over; the king kissed Barzillai and blessed him, and he returned to his own home.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:40} % *2SAMUEL-19-40
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-40}

			Then the king went on to Gilgal, and Chimham went on with him: and all the people of Judah conducted the king, and also half the people of Israel.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּעֲבֹ֤ר הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ הַגִּלְגָּ֔לָה וְכִמְהָ֖ן עָבַ֣ר עִמּ֑וֹ וְכָל־עַ֤ם יְהוּדָה֙ הֶעֱבִ֣ירוּ אֶת־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וְגַ֕ם חֲצִ֖י עַ֥ם יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ διέβη ὁ βασιλεὺς εἰς Γάλγαλα, καὶ Χαμαὰμ διέβη μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ· καὶ πᾶς ὁ λαὸς Ἰούδα διαβαίνοντες μετὰ τοῦ βασιλέως καί γε τὸ ἥμισυ τοῦ λαοῦ Ἰσραήλ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So the king went over to Gilgal, and Chimham went over with him: and all the people of Judah brought the king over, and also half the people of Israel.
					\item[NIV] When the king crossed over to Gilgal, Kimham crossed with him. All the troops of Judah and half the troops of Israel had taken the king over.
					\item[NRSV] The king went on to Gilgal, and Chimham went on with him; all the people of Judah, and also half the people of Israel, brought the king on his way.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:41} % *2SAMUEL-19-41
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-41}

			And, behold, all the men of Israel came to the king, and said unto the king, Why have our brethren the men of Judah stolen thee away, and have brought the king, and his household, and all David’s men with him, over Jordan?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְהִנֵּ֛ה כָּל־אִ֥ישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בָּאִ֣ים אֶל־הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ אֶל־הַמֶּ֡לֶךְ מַדּוּעַ֩ גְּנָב֨וּךָ אַחֵ֜ינוּ אִ֣ישׁ יְהוּדָ֗ה וַיַּעֲבִ֨רוּ אֶת־הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ וְאֶת־בֵּיתוֹ֙ אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֔ן וְכָל־אַנְשֵׁ֥י דָוִ֖ד עִמּֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἰδοὺ πᾶς ἀνὴρ Ἰσραὴλ παρεγένοντο πρὸς τὸν βασιλέα, καὶ εἶπον πρὸς τὸν βασιλέα Τί ὅτι ἔκλεψάν σε οἱ ἀδελφοὶ ἡμῶν ἀνὴρ Ἰούδα, καὶ διεβίβασαν τὸν βασιλέα καὶ τὸν οἶκον αὐτοῦ τὸν Ἰορδάνην, καὶ πάντες ἄνδρες Δαυεὶδ μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And, behold, all the men of Israel came to the king, and said unto the king, Why have our brethren the men of Judah stolen thee away, and brought the king, and his household, over the Jordan, and all David's men with him?
					\item[NIV] Soon all the men of Israel were coming to the king and saying to him, “Why did our brothers, the men of Judah, steal the king away and bring him and his household across the Jordan, together with all his men?”
					\item[NRSV] Then all the people of Israel came to the king, and said to him, “Why have our kindred the people of Judah stolen you away, and brought the king and his household over the Jordan, and all David’s men with him?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:42} % *2SAMUEL-19-42
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-42}

			And all the men of Judah answered the men of Israel, Because the king is near of kin to us: wherefore then be ye angry for this matter? have we eaten at all of the king’s cost? or hath he given us any gift?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּעַן֩ כָּל־אִ֨ישׁ יְהוּדָ֜ה עַל־אִ֣ישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל כִּֽי־קָר֤וֹב הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ אֵלַ֔י וְלָ֤מָּה זֶּה֙ חָרָ֣ה לְךָ֔ עַל־הַדָּבָ֖ר הַזֶּ֑ה הֶאָכ֤וֹל אָכַ֙לְנוּ֙ מִן־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ אִם־נִשֵּׂ֥את נִשָּׂ֖א לָֽנוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀπεκρίθη πᾶς ἀνὴρ Ἰούδα πρὸς ἄνδρα Ἰσραὴλ καὶ εἶπαν Διότι ἐγγίζει πρὸς μὲ ὁ βασιλεύς; καὶ ἵνα τί οὕτως ἐθυμώθης περὶ τοῦ λόγου τούτου; μὴ βρώσει ἐφάγαμεν ἐκ τοῦ βασιλέως, ἢ δόμα ἔδωκεν ἢ ἄρσιν ἦρεν ἡμῖν;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And all the men of Judah answered the men of Israel, Because the king is near of kin to us: wherefore then are ye angry for this matter? have we eaten at all at the king's cost? or hath he given us any gift?
					\item[NIV] All the men of Judah answered the men of Israel, “We did this because the king is closely related to us. Why are you angry about it? Have we eaten any of the king’s provisions? Have we taken anything for ourselves?”
					\item[NRSV] All the people of Judah answered the people of Israel, “Because the king is near of kin to us. Why then are you angry over this matter? Have we eaten at all at the king’s expense? Or has he given us any gift?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 19:43} % *2SAMUEL-19-43
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-19-43}

			And the men of Israel answered the men of Judah, and said, We have ten parts in the king, and we have also more right in David than ye: why then did ye despise us, that our advice should not be first had in bringing back our king? And the words of the men of Judah were fiercer than the words of the men of Israel.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֣עַן אִֽישׁ־יִשְׂרָאֵל֩ אֶת־אִ֨ישׁ יְהוּדָ֜ה וַיֹּ֗אמֶר עֶֽשֶׂר־יָד֨וֹת לִ֣י בַמֶּלֶךְ֮ וְגַם־בְּדָוִד֮ אֲנִ֣י מִמְּךָ֒ וּמַדּ֙וּעַ֙ הֱקִלֹּתַ֔נִי וְלֹא־הָיָ֨ה דְבָרִ֥י רִאשׁ֛וֹן לִ֖י לְהָשִׁ֣יב אֶת־מַלְכִּ֑י וַיִּ֙קֶשׁ֙ דְּבַר־אִ֣ישׁ יְהוּדָ֔ה מִדְּבַ֖ר אִ֥ישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀπεκρίθη ἀνὴρ Ἰσραὴλ τῷ ἀνδρὶ Ἰούδα καὶ εἶπεν Δέκα χεῖρές μοι ἐν τῷ βασιλεῖ, καὶ πρωτότοκος ἐγὼ ἢ σύ, καί γε ἐν τῷ Δαυείδ εἰμι ὑπὲρ σέ. καὶ ἵνα τί τοῦτο ὕβρισάς με καὶ οὐκ ἐλογίσθη ὁ λόγος μου πρῶτός μοι τοῦ Ἰούδα, ἐπιστρέψαι τὸν βασιλέα ἐμοί; καὶ ἐσκληρύνθη ὁ λόγος ἀνδρὸς Ἰούδα ὑπὲρ τὸν λόγον ἀνδρὸς Ἰσραήλ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the men of Israel answered the men of Judah, and said, We have ten parts in the king, and we have also more right in David than ye: why then did ye despise us, that our advice should not be first had in bringing back our king? And the words of the men of Judah were fiercer than the words of the men of Israel.
					\item[NIV] Then the men of Israel answered the men of Judah, “We have ten shares in the king; so we have a greater claim on David than you have. Why then do you treat us with contempt? Weren’t we the first to speak of bringing back our king?” But the men of Judah pressed their claims even more forcefully than the men of Israel.
					\item[NRSV] But the people of Israel answered the people of Judah, “We have ten shares in the king, and in David also we have more than you. Why then did you despise us? Were we not the first to speak of bringing back our king?” But the words of the people of Judah were fiercer than the words of the people of Israel.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{2 Samuel 20} % *2SAMUEL-20
	% -----
		\label{2SAMUEL-20}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:1} % *2SAMUEL-20-1
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-1}

			And there happened to be there a man of Belial, whose name was Sheba, the son of Bichri, a Benjamite: and he blew a trumpet, and said, We have no part in David, neither have we inheritance in the son of Jesse: every man to his tents, O Israel.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְשָׁ֨ם נִקְרָ֜א אִ֣ישׁ בְּלִיַּ֗עַל וּשְׁמ֛וֹ שֶׁ֥בַע בֶּן־בִּכְרִ֖י אִ֣ישׁ יְמִינִ֑י וַיִּתְקַ֣ע בַּשֹּׁפָ֗ר וַ֠יֹּאמֶר אֵֽין־לָ֨נוּ חֵ֜לֶק בְּדָוִ֗ד וְלֹ֤א נַֽחֲלָה־לָ֙נוּ֙ בְּבֶן־יִשַׁ֔י אִ֥ישׁ לְאֹהָלָ֖יו יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ ἐκεῖ ἐπικαλούμενος υἱὸς παράνομος καὶ ὄνομα αὐτῷ Σάβεε, υἱὸς Βοχορεί, ἀνὴρ ὁ Ἱεμενεί· καὶ ἐσάλπισεν τῇ κερατίνῃ καὶ εἶπεν Οὐκ ἔστιν ἡμῖν μερὶς ἐν Δαυεὶδ οὐδὲ κληρονομία ἡμῖν ἐν τῷ υἱῷ Ἰεσσαί· ἀνὴρ εἰς τὰ σκηνώματά σου, Ἰσραήλ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And there happened to be there a base fellow, whose name was Sheba, the son of Bichri, a Benjamite: and he blew the trumpet, and said, We have no portion in David, neither have we inheritance in the son of Jesse: every man to his tents, O Israel.
					\item[NIV] Now a troublemaker named Sheba son of Bikri, a Benjamite, happened to be there. He sounded the trumpet and shouted, “We have no share in David, no part in Jesse’s son! Every man to his tent, Israel!”
					\item[NRSV] Now a scoundrel named Sheba son of Bichri, a Benjaminite, happened to be there. He sounded the trumpet and cried out, “We have no portion in David, no share in the son of Jesse! Everyone to your tents, O Israel!”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:2} % *2SAMUEL-20-2
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-2}

			So every man of Israel went up from after David, and followed Sheba the son of Bichri: but the men of Judah clave unto their king, from Jordan even to Jerusalem.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֜עַל כָּל־אִ֤ישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ מֵאַחֲרֵ֣י דָוִ֔ד אַחֲרֵ֖י שֶׁ֣בַע בֶּן־בִּכְרִ֑י וְאִ֤ישׁ יְהוּדָה֙ דָּבְק֣וּ בְמַלְכָּ֔ם מִן־הַיַּרְדֵּ֖ן וְעַד־יְרוּשָׁלִָֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀνέβη πᾶς Ἰσραὴλ ἀπὸ ὄπισθεν Δαυεὶδ ὀπίσω Σάβεε υἱοῦ Βοχορεί· καὶ ἀνὴρ Ἰούδα ἐκολλήθη τῷ βασιλεῖ αὐτῶν ἀπὸ τοῦ Ἰορδάνου καὶ ἕως Ἰερουσαλήμ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So all the men of Israel went up from following David, and followed Sheba the son of Bichri; but the men of Judah clave unto their king, from the Jordan even to Jerusalem.
					\item[NIV] So all the men of Israel deserted David to follow Sheba son of Bikri. But the men of Judah stayed by their king all the way from the Jordan to Jerusalem.
					\item[NRSV] So all the people of Israel withdrew from David and followed Sheba son of Bichri; but the people of Judah followed their king steadfastly from the Jordan to Jerusalem.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:3} % *2SAMUEL-20-3
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-3}

			And David came to his house at Jerusalem; and the king took the ten women his concubines, whom he had left to keep the house, and put them in ward, and fed them, but went not in unto them. So they were shut up unto the day of their death, living in widowhood.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּבֹ֨א דָוִ֣ד אֶל־בֵּיתוֹ֮ יְרֽוּשָׁלִַם֒ וַיִּקַּ֣ח הַמֶּ֡לֶךְ אֵ֣ת עֶֽשֶׂר־נָשִׁ֣ים ׀ פִּלַגְשִׁ֡ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר הִנִּיחַ֩ לִשְׁמֹ֨ר הַבַּ֜יִת וַֽיִּתְּנֵ֤ם בֵּית־מִשְׁמֶ֙רֶת֙ וַֽיְכַלְכְּלֵ֔ם וַאֲלֵיהֶ֖ם לֹא־בָ֑א וַתִּהְיֶ֧ינָה צְרֻר֛וֹת עַד־י֥וֹם מֻתָ֖ן אַלְמְנ֥וּת חַיּֽוּת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ εἰσῆλθεν Δαυεὶδ εἰς τὸν οἶκον αὐτοῦ εἰς Ἰερουσαλήμ· καὶ ἔλαβεν ὁ βασιλεὺς τὰς δέκα γυναῖκας τὰς παλλακὰς αὐτοῦ, ἃς ἀφῆκεν φυλάσσειν τὸν οἶκον, καὶ ἔδωκεν αὐτὰς ἐν οἴκῳ φυλακῆς, καὶ διέθρεψεν αὐτὰς καὶ πρὸς αὐτὰς οὐκ εἰσῆλθεν, καὶ ἦσαν συνεχόμεναι ἕως ἡμέρας θανάτου αὐτῶν, χῆραι ζῶσαι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David came to his house at Jerusalem; and the king took the ten women his concubines, whom he had left to keep the house, and put them in ward, and provided them with sustenance, but went not in unto them. So they were shut up unto the day of their death, living in widowhood.
					\item[NIV] When David returned to his palace in Jerusalem, he took the ten concubines he had left to take care of the palace and put them in a house under guard. He provided for them but had no sexual relations with them. They were kept in confinement till the day of their death, living as widows.
					\item[NRSV] David came to his house at Jerusalem; and the king took the ten concubines whom he had left to look after the house, and put them in a house under guard, and provided for them, but did not go in to them. So they were shut up until the day of their death, living as if in widowhood.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:4} % *2SAMUEL-20-4
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-4}

			Then said the king to Amasa, Assemble me the men of Judah within three days, and be thou here present.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ אֶל־עֲמָשָׂ֔א הַזְעֶק־לִ֥י אֶת־אִישׁ־יְהוּדָ֖ה שְׁלֹ֣שֶׁת יָמִ֑ים וְאַתָּ֖ה פֹּ֥ה עֲמֹֽד׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ εἶπεν ὁ βασιλεὺς πρὸς Ἀμεσσαεί Βόησόν μοι τὸν ἄνδρα Ἰούδα τρεῖς ἡμέρας, σὺ δὲ αὐτοῦ στῆθι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then said the king to Amasa, Call me the men of Judah together within three days, and be thou here present.
					\item[NIV] Then the king said to Amasa, “Summon the men of Judah to come to me within three days, and be here yourself.”
					\item[NRSV] Then the king said to Amasa, “Call the men of Judah together to me within three days, and be here yourself.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:5} % *2SAMUEL-20-5
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-5}

			So Amasa went to assemble the men of Judah: but he tarried longer than the set time which he had appointed him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֵּ֥לֶךְ עֲמָשָׂ֖א לְהַזְעִ֣יק אֶת־יְהוּדָ֑ה וַיּ֕וֹחֶר מִן־הַמּוֹעֵ֖ד אֲשֶׁ֥ר יְעָדֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐπορεύθη Ἀμεσσαεὶ τοῦ βοῆσαι τὸν Ἰούδαν, καὶ ἐχρόνισεν ἀπὸ τοῦ καιροῦ οὗ ἐτάξατο αὐτῷ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So Amasa went to call the men of Judah together; but he tarried longer than the set time which he had appointed him.
					\item[NIV] But when Amasa went to summon Judah, he took longer than the time the king had set for him.
					\item[NRSV] So Amasa went to summon Judah; but he delayed beyond the set time that had been appointed him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:6} % *2SAMUEL-20-6
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-6}

			And David said to Abishai, Now shall Sheba the son of Bichri do us more harm than did Absalom: take thou thy lord’s servants, and pursue after him, lest he get him fenced cities, and escape us.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר דָּוִד֙ אֶל־אֲבִישַׁ֔י עַתָּ֗ה יֵ֧רַֽע לָ֛נוּ שֶׁ֥בַע בֶּן־בִּכְרִ֖י מִן־אַבְשָׁל֑וֹם אַ֠תָּה קַ֞ח אֶת־עַבְדֵ֤י אֲדֹנֶ֙יךָ֙ וּרְדֹ֣ף אַחֲרָ֔יו פֶּן־מָ֥צָא ל֛וֹ עָרִ֥ים בְּצֻר֖וֹת וְהִצִּ֥יל עֵינֵֽנוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Δαυεὶδ πρὸς Ἀβεισά Νῦν κακοποιήσει ἡμᾶς Σάβεε υἱὸς Βοχορεὶ ὑπὲρ Ἀβεσσαλώμ· καὶ νῦν σὺ λάβε μετὰ σεαυτοῦ τοὺς παῖδας τοῦ κυρίου σου καὶ καταδίωξον ὀπίσω αὐτοῦ, μή ποτε ἑαυτῷ εὕρῃ πόλεις ὀχυρὰς καὶ σκιάσει τοὺς ὀφθαλμοὺς ἡμῶν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David said to Abishai, Now will Sheba the son of Bichri do us more harm than did Absalom: take thou thy lord's servants, and pursue after him, lest he get him fortified cities, and escape out of our sight.
					\item[NIV] David said to Abishai, “Now Sheba son of Bikri will do us more harm than Absalom did. Take your master’s men and pursue him, or he will find fortified cities and escape from us.”
					\item[NRSV] David said to Abishai, “Now Sheba son of Bichri will do us more harm than Absalom; take your lord’s servants and pursue him, or he will find fortified cities for himself, and escape from us.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:7} % *2SAMUEL-20-7
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-7}

			And there went out after him Joab’s men, and the Cherethites, and the Pelethites, and all the mighty men: and they went out of Jerusalem, to pursue after Sheba the son of Bichri.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֵּצְא֤וּ אַֽחֲרָיו֙ אַנְשֵׁ֣י יוֹאָ֔ב וְהַכְּרֵתִ֥י וְהַפְּלֵתִ֖י וְכָל־הַגִּבֹּרִ֑ים וַיֵּֽצְאוּ֙ מִיר֣וּשָׁלִַ֔ם לִרְדֹּ֕ף אַחֲרֵ֖י שֶׁ֥בַע בֶּן־בִּכְרִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐξῆλθεν ὀπίσω αὐτοῦ Ἀβεσσὰ καὶ οἱ ἄνδρες Ἰωὰβ καὶ ὁ Χελεθθεὶ καὶ ὁ Φελεθθεὶ καὶ πάντες οἱ δυνατοί, καὶ ἐξῆλθαν ἐξ Ἰερουσαλὴμ διῶξαι ὀπίσω Σάβεε υἱοῦ Βοχορεί.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And there went out after him Joab's men, and the Cherethites and the Pelethites, and all the mighty men; and they went out of Jerusalem, to pursue after Sheba the son of Bichri.
					\item[NIV] So Joab’s men and the Kerethites and Pelethites and all the mighty warriors went out under the command of Abishai. They marched out from Jerusalem to pursue Sheba son of Bikri.
					\item[NRSV] Joab’s men went out after him, along with the Cherethites, the Pelethites, and all the warriors; they went out from Jerusalem to pursue Sheba son of Bichri.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:8} % *2SAMUEL-20-8
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-8}

			When they were at the great stone which is in Gibeon, Amasa went before them. And Joab’s garment that he had put on was girded unto him, and upon it a girdle with a sword fastened upon his loins in the sheath thereof; and as he went forth it fell out.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֵ֗ם עִם־הָאֶ֤בֶן הַגְּדוֹלָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּגִבְע֔וֹן וַעֲמָשָׂ֖א בָּ֣א לִפְנֵיהֶ֑ם וְיוֹאָ֞ב חָג֣וּר ׀ מִדּ֣וֹ לְבֻשׁ֗וּ וְעָלָ֞יו חֲג֥וֹר חֶ֙רֶב֙ מְצֻמֶּ֤דֶת עַל־מָתְנָיו֙ בְּתַעְרָ֔הּ וְה֥וּא יָצָ֖א וַתִּפֹּֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ αὐτοὶ παρὰ τῷ λίθῳ τῷ μεγάλῳ τῷ ἐν Γαβαών, καὶ Ἀμεσσαεὶ ἦλθεν ἔμπροσθεν αὐτῶν· καὶ Ἰωὰβ περιεζωσμένος μανδύαν τὸ ἔνδυμα αὐτοῦ, καὶ ἐπ᾽ αὐτῷ περιεζωσμένος μάχαιραν ἐζευγμένην ἐπὶ τῆς ὀσφύος αὐτοῦ ἐν κολεῷ αὐτῆς· καὶ ἡ μάχαιρα ἐξῆλθεν, καὶ αὐτὴ ἐξῆλθεν καὶ ἔπεσεν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When they were at the great stone which is in Gibeon, Amasa came to meet them. And Joab was girded with his apparel of war that he had put on, and thereon was a girdle with a sword fastened upon his loins in the sheath thereof; and as he went forth it fell out.
					\item[NIV] While they were at the great rock in Gibeon, Amasa came to meet them. Joab was wearing his military tunic, and strapped over it at his waist was a belt with a dagger in its sheath. As he stepped forward, it dropped out of its sheath.
					\item[NRSV] When they were at the large stone that is in Gibeon, Amasa came to meet them. Now Joab was wearing a soldier’s garment and over it was a belt with a sword in its sheath fastened at his waist; as he went forward it fell out.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:9} % *2SAMUEL-20-9
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-9}

			And Joab said to Amasa, Art thou in health, my brother? And Joab took Amasa by the beard with the right hand to kiss him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יוֹאָב֙ לַעֲמָשָׂ֔א הֲשָׁל֥וֹם אַתָּ֖ה אָחִ֑י וַתֹּ֜חֶז יַד־יְמִ֥ין יוֹאָ֛ב בִּזְקַ֥ן עֲמָשָׂ֖א לִנְשָׁק־לֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Ἰωὰβ τῷ Ἀμεσσαεί Εἰ ὑγιαίνεις, ἀδελφέ; καὶ ἐκράτησεν ἡ χεὶρ ἡ δεξιὰ Ἰωὰβ τοῦ πώγωνος Ἀμεσσαεὶ τοῦ καταφιλῆσαι αὐτόν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Joab said to Amasa, Is it well with thee, my brother? And Joab took Amasa by the beard with his right hand to kiss him.
					\item[NIV] Joab said to Amasa, “How are you, my brother?” Then Joab took Amasa by the beard with his right hand to kiss him.
					\item[NRSV] Joab said to Amasa, “Is it well with you, my brother?” And Joab took Amasa by the beard with his right hand to kiss him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:10} % *2SAMUEL-20-10
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-10}

			But Amasa took no heed to the sword that was in Joab’s hand: so he smote him therewith in the fifth rib, and shed out his bowels to the ground, and struck him not again; and he died. So Joab and Abishai his brother pursued after Sheba the son of Bichri.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַעֲמָשָׂ֨א לֹֽא־נִשְׁמַ֜ר בַּחֶ֣רֶב ׀ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּיַד־יוֹאָ֗ב וַיַּכֵּהוּ֩ בָ֨הּ אֶל־הַחֹ֜מֶשׁ וַיִּשְׁפֹּ֨ךְ מֵעָ֥יו אַ֛רְצָה וְלֹא־שָׁ֥נָה ל֖וֹ וַיָּמֹ֑ת וְיוֹאָב֙ וַאֲבִישַׁ֣י אָחִ֔יו רָדַ֕ף אַחֲרֵ֖י שֶׁ֥בַע בֶּן־בִּכְרִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ Ἀμεσσαεὶ οὐκ ἐφυλάξατο τὴν μάχαιραν τὴν ἐν τῇ χειρὶ Ἰωάβ· καὶ ἔπαισεν αὐτὸν ἐν αὐτῇ Ἰωὰβ εἰς τὴν ψόαν, καὶ ἐξεχύθη ἡ κοιλία αὐτοῦ εἰς τὴν γῆν, καὶ οὐκ ἐδευτέρωσεν αὐτῷ, καὶ ἀπέθανεν. καὶ Ἰωὰβ καὶ Ἀβεισὰ ὁ ἀδελφὸς αὐτοῦ ἐδίωξεν ὀπίσω Σάβεε υἱοῦ Βοχορεί.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But Amasa took no heed to the sword that was in Joab's hand: so he smote him therewith in the body, and shed out his bowels to the ground, and struck him not again; and he died. And Joab and Abishai his brother pursued after Sheba the son of Bichri.
					\item[NIV] Amasa was not on his guard against the dagger in Joab’s hand, and Joab plunged it into his belly, and his intestines spilled out on the ground. Without being stabbed again, Amasa died. Then Joab and his brother Abishai pursued Sheba son of Bikri.
					\item[NRSV] But Amasa did not notice the sword in Joab’s hand; Joab struck him in the belly so that his entrails poured out on the ground, and he died. He did not strike a second blow. Then Joab and his brother Abishai pursued Sheba son of Bichri.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:11} % *2SAMUEL-20-11
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-11}

			And one of Joab’s men stood by him, and said, He that favoureth Joab, and he that is for David, let him go after Joab.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאִישׁ֙ עָמַ֣ד עָלָ֔יו מִֽנַּעֲרֵ֖י יוֹאָ֑ב וַיֹּ֗אמֶר מִי֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר חָפֵ֧ץ בְּיוֹאָ֛ב וּמִ֥י אֲשֶׁר־לְדָוִ֖ד אַחֲרֵ֥י יוֹאָֽב׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀνὴρ ἔστη ἐπ᾽ αὐτὸν τῶν παιδαρίων Ἰωὰβ καὶ εἶπεν Τίς ὁ βουλόμενος Ἰωάβ, καὶ τίς τοῦ Δαυεὶδ ὀπίσω Ἰωάβ;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And there stood by him one of Joab's young men, and said, He that favoreth Joab, and he that is for David, let him follow Joab.
					\item[NIV] One of Joab’s men stood beside Amasa and said, “Whoever favors Joab, and whoever is for David, let him follow Joab!”
					\item[NRSV] And one of Joab’s men took his stand by Amasa, and said, “Whoever favors Joab, and whoever is for David, let him follow Joab.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:12} % *2SAMUEL-20-12
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-12}

			And Amasa wallowed in blood in the midst of the highway. And when the man saw that all the people stood still, he removed Amasa out of the highway into the field, and cast a cloth upon him, when he saw that every one that came by him stood still.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַעֲמָשָׂ֛א מִתְגֹּלֵ֥ל בַּדָּ֖ם בְּת֣וֹךְ הַֽמְסִּלָּ֑ה וַיַּ֨רְא הָאִ֜ישׁ כִּֽי־עָמַ֣ד כָּל־הָעָ֗ם וַיַּסֵּב֩ אֶת־עֲמָשָׂ֨א מִן־הַֽמְסִלָּ֤ה הַשָּׂדֶה֙ וַיַּשְׁלֵ֤ךְ עָלָיו֙ בֶּ֔גֶד כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר רָאָ֔ה כָּל־הַבָּ֥א עָלָ֖יו וְעָמָֽד׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ Ἀμεσσαεὶ πεφυρμένος ἐν τῷ αἵματι ἐν μέσῳ τῆς τρίβου, καὶ εἶδεν ἀνὴρ ὅτι εἱστήκει πᾶς ὁ λαός· καὶ ἀπέστρεψαν τὸν Ἀμεσσαεὶ ἐκ τῆς τρίβου εἰς ἀγρὸν καὶ ἐπέρριψεν ἐπ᾽ αὐτὸν ἱμάτιον, καθότι εἶδεν πάντα τὸν ἐρχόμενον ἐπ᾽ αὐτὸν ἑστηκότα.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Amasa lay wallowing in his blood in the midst of the highway. And when the man saw that all the people stood still, he carried Amasa out of the highway into the field, and cast a garment over him, when he saw that every one that came by him stood still.
					\item[NIV] Amasa lay wallowing in his blood in the middle of the road, and the man saw that all the troops came to a halt there. When he realized that everyone who came up to Amasa stopped, he dragged him from the road into a field and threw a garment over him.
					\item[NRSV] Amasa lay wallowing in his blood on the highway, and the man saw that all the people were stopping. Since he saw that all who came by him were stopping, he carried Amasa from the highway into a field, and threw a garment over him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:13} % *2SAMUEL-20-13
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-13}

			When he was removed out of the highway, all the people went on after Joab, to pursue after Sheba the son of Bichri.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר הֹגָ֖ה מִן־הַֽמְסִלָּ֑ה עָבַ֤ר כָּל־אִישׁ֙ אַחֲרֵ֣י יוֹאָ֔ב לִרְדֹּ֕ף אַחֲרֵ֖י שֶׁ֥בַע בֶּן־בִּכְרִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἡνίκα δὲ ἔφθασεν ἐκ τῆς τρίβου, παρῆλθεν πᾶς ἀνὴρ Ἰσραὴλ ὀπίσω Ἰωὰβ τοῦ διῶξαι ὀπίσω Σάβεε υἱοῦ Βοχορεὶ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When he was removed out of the highway, all the people went on after Joab, to pursue after Sheba the son of Bichri.
					\item[NIV] After Amasa had been removed from the road, everyone went on with Joab to pursue Sheba son of Bikri.
					\item[NRSV] Once he was removed from the highway, all the people went on after Joab to pursue Sheba son of Bichri.

		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:14} % *2SAMUEL-20-14
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-14}

			And he went through all the tribes of Israel unto Abel, and to Bethmaachah, and all the Berites: and they were gathered together, and went also after him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַֽיַּעֲבֹ֞ר בְּכָל־שִׁבְטֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל אָבֵ֛לָה וּבֵ֥ית מַעֲכָ֖ה וְכָל־הַבֵּרִ֑ים וַיִּקָּ֣הֲל֔וּ וַיָּבֹ֖אוּ אַף־אַחֲרָֽיו׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ διῆλθεν ἐν πάσαις φυλαῖς Ἰσραὴλ εἰς Ἀβὲλ καὶ εἰς Βαιθμαχά, καὶ πάντες ἐν Χαρρεὶ καὶ ἐξεκκλησιάσθησαν· καὶ ἦλθεν κατόπισθεν αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he went through all the tribes of Israel unto Abel, and to Beth-maacah, and all the Berites: and they were gathered together, and went also after him.
					\item[NIV] Sheba passed through all the tribes of Israel to Abel Beth Maakah and through the entire region of the Bikrites, who gathered together and followed him.
					\item[NRSV] Sheba passed through all the tribes of Israel to Abel of Beth-maacah; and all the Bichrites assembled, and followed him inside.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:15} % *2SAMUEL-20-15
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-15}

			And they came and besieged him in Abel of Bethmaachah, and they cast up a bank against the city, and it stood in the trench: and all the people that were with Joab battered the wall, to throw it down.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּבֹ֜אוּ וַיָּצֻ֣רוּ עָלָ֗יו בְּאָבֵ֙לָה֙ בֵּ֣ית הַֽמַּעֲכָ֔ה וַיִּשְׁפְּכ֤וּ סֹֽלְלָה֙ אֶל־הָעִ֔יר וַֽתַּעֲמֹ֖ד בַּחֵ֑ל וְכָל־הָעָם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֶת־יוֹאָ֔ב מַשְׁחִיתִ֖ם לְהַפִּ֥יל הַחוֹמָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ παρεγενήθησαν καὶ ἐπολιόρκουν ἐπ᾽ αὐτὸν ἐν Ἀβὲλ τὴν Βαιθμαχά, καὶ ἐξέχεαν πρόσχωμα πρὸς τὴν πόλιν καὶ ἔστη ἐν τῷ προτειχίσματι· καὶ πᾶς ὁ λαὸς ὁ μετὰ Ἰωὰβ ἐνοοῦσαν καταβαλεῖν τὸ τεῖχος·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And they came and besieged him in Abel of Beth-maacah, and they cast up a mound against the city, and it stood against the rampart; and all the people that were with Joab battered the wall, to throw it down.
					\item[NIV] All the troops with Joab came and besieged Sheba in Abel Beth Maakah. They built a siege ramp up to the city, and it stood against the outer fortifications. While they were battering the wall to bring it down,
					\item[NRSV] Joab’s forces came and besieged him in Abel of Beth-maacah; they threw up a siege ramp against the city, and it stood against the rampart. Joab’s forces were battering the wall to break it down.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:16} % *2SAMUEL-20-16
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-16}

			Then cried a wise woman out of the city, Hear, hear; say, I pray you, unto Joab, Come near hither, that I may speak with thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתִּקְרָ֛א אִשָּׁ֥ה חֲכָמָ֖ה מִן־הָעִ֑יר שִׁמְע֤וּ שִׁמְעוּ֙ אִמְרוּ־נָ֣א אֶל־יוֹאָ֔ב קְרַ֣ב עַד־הֵ֔נָּה וַאֲדַבְּרָ֖ה אֵלֶֽיךָ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐβόησεν γυνὴ σοφὴ ἐκ τοῦ τείχους καὶ εἶπεν Ἀκούσατε ἀκούσατε, εἴπατε δὴ πρὸς Ἰωάβ Ἔγγισον ἕως ὧδε, καὶ λαλήσω πρὸς αὐτόν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then cried a wise woman out of the city, Hear, hear; say, I pray you, unto Joab, Come near hither, that I may speak with thee.
					\item[NIV] a wise woman called from the city, “Listen! Listen! Tell Joab to come here so I can speak to him.”
					\item[NRSV] Then a wise woman called from the city, “Listen! Listen! Tell Joab, ‘Come here, I want to speak to you.’”

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:17} % *2SAMUEL-20-17
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-17}

			And when he was come near unto her, the woman said, Art thou Joab? And he answered, I am he. Then she said unto him, Hear the words of thine handmaid. And he answered, I do hear.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּקְרַ֣ב אֵלֶ֔יהָ וַתֹּ֧אמֶר הָאִשָּׁ֛ה הַאַתָּ֥ה יוֹאָ֖ב וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אָ֑נִי וַתֹּ֣אמֶר ל֗וֹ שְׁמַע֙ דִּבְרֵ֣י אֲמָתֶ֔ךָ וַיֹּ֖אמֶר שֹׁמֵ֥עַ אָנֹֽכִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ προσήγγισεν πρὸς αὐτήν, καὶ εἶπεν ἡ γυνή Εἰ σὺ εἶ Ἰωάβ; ὁ δὲ εἶπεν Ἐγώ. εἶπεν δὲ αὐτῷ Ἄκουσον τοὺς λόγους τῆς δούλης σου· καὶ εἶπεν Ἰωάβ Ἀκούω ἐγώ εἰμι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he came near unto her; and the woman said, Art thou Joab? And he answered, I am. Then she said unto him, Hear the words of thy handmaid. And he answered, I do hear.
					\item[NIV] He went toward her, and she asked, “Are you Joab?” “I am,” he answered. She said, “Listen to what your servant has to say.” “I’m listening,” he said.
					\item[NRSV] He came near her; and the woman said, “Are you Joab?” He answered, “I am.” Then she said to him, “Listen to the words of your servant.” He answered, “I am listening.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:18} % *2SAMUEL-20-18
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-18}

			Then she spake, saying, They were wont to speak in old time, saying, They shall surely ask counsel at Abel: and so they ended the matter.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתֹּ֖אמֶר לֵאמֹ֑ר דַּבֵּ֨ר יְדַבְּר֤וּ בָרִֽאשֹׁנָה֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר שָׁאֹ֧ל יְשָׁאֲל֛וּ בְּאָבֵ֖ל וְכֵ֥ן הֵתַֽמּוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν λέγουσα Λόγον ἐλάλησαν ἐν πρώτοις λέγοντες Ἠρωτημένος ἠρωτήθη ἐν τῇ Ἀβὲλ καὶ ἐν Δὰν εἰ ἐξέλιπον ἃ ἔθεντο οἱ πιστοὶ τοῦ Ἰσραήλ· ἐρῶντες ἐπερωτήσουσιν ἕνα ἐν Ἀβὲλ καὶ οὕτως, εἰ ἐξέλιπον.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then she spake, saying, They were wont to speak in old time, saying, They shall surely ask counsel at Abel: and so they ended the matter.
					\item[NIV] She continued, “Long ago they used to say, ‘Get your answer at Abel,’ and that settled it.
					\item[NRSV] Then she said, “They used to say in the old days, ‘Let them inquire at Abel’; and so they would settle a matter.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:19} % *2SAMUEL-20-19
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-19}

			I am one of them that are peaceable and faithful in Israel: thou seekest to destroy a city and a mother in Israel: why wilt thou swallow up the inheritance of the LORD?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָנֹכִ֕י שְׁלֻמֵ֖י אֱמוּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אַתָּ֣ה מְבַקֵּ֗שׁ לְהָמִ֨ית עִ֤יר וְאֵם֙ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל לָ֥מָּה תְבַלַּ֖ע נַחֲלַ֥ת יְהוָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐγώ εἰμι εἰρηνικὰ τῶν στηριγμάτων Ἰσραήλ, σὺ δὲ ζητεῖς θανατῶσαι πόλιν καὶ μητρόπολιν ἐν Ἰσραήλ· ἵνα τί καταποντίζεις κληρονομίαν Κυρίου;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I am of them that are peaceable and faithful in Israel: thou seekest to destroy a city and a mother in Israel: why wilt thou swallow up the inheritance of Jehovah?
					\item[NIV] We are the peaceful and faithful in Israel. You are trying to destroy a city that is a mother in Israel. Why do you want to swallow up the LORD’s inheritance?”
					\item[NRSV] I am one of those who are peaceable and faithful in Israel; you seek to destroy a city that is a mother in Israel; why will you swallow up the heritage of the LORD?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:20} % *2SAMUEL-20-20
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-20}

			And Joab answered and said, Far be it, far be it from me, that I should swallow up or destroy.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֥עַן יוֹאָ֖ב וַיֹּאמַ֑ר חָלִ֤ילָה חָלִ֙ילָה֙ לִ֔י אִם־אֲבַלַּ֖ע וְאִם־אַשְׁחִֽית׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀπεκρίθη Ἰωὰβ καὶ εἶπεν Ἵλεώς μοι ἵλεώς μοι, εἰ καταποντιῶ καὶ εἰ φθερῶ·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Joab answered and said, Far be it, far be it from me, that I should swallow up or destroy.
					\item[NIV] “Far be it from me!” Joab replied, “Far be it from me to swallow up or destroy!
					\item[NRSV] Joab answered, “Far be it from me, far be it, that I should swallow up or destroy!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:21} % *2SAMUEL-20-21
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-21}

			The matter is not so: but a man of mount Ephraim, Sheba the son of Bichri by name, hath lifted up his hand against the king, even against David: deliver him only, and I will depart from the city. And the woman said unto Joab, Behold, his head shall be thrown to thee over the wall.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־כֵ֣ן הַדָּבָ֗ר כִּ֡י אִישׁ֩ מֵהַ֨ר אֶפְרַ֜יִם שֶׁ֧בַע בֶּן־בִּכְרִ֣י שְׁמ֗וֹ נָשָׂ֤א יָדוֹ֙ בַּמֶּ֣לֶךְ בְּדָוִ֔ד תְּנֽוּ־אֹת֣וֹ לְבַדּ֔וֹ וְאֵלְכָ֖ה מֵעַ֣ל הָעִ֑יר וַתֹּ֤אמֶר הָֽאִשָּׁה֙ אֶל־יוֹאָ֔ב הִנֵּ֥ה רֹאשׁ֛וֹ מֻשְׁלָ֥ךְ אֵלֶ֖יךָ בְּעַ֥ד הַחוֹמָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{οὐχ οὗτος ὁ λόγος· ὅτι ἀνὴρ ἐξ ὄρους Ἐφράιμ, Σάβεε υἱὸς Βοχορεὶ ὄνομα αὐτοῦ, καὶ ἐπῆρεν τὴν χεῖρα αὐτοῦ ἐπὶ τὸν βασιλέα Δαυείδ. δότε αὐτόν μοι μόνον, καὶ ἀπελεύσομαι ἀπάνωθεν τῆς πόλεως. καὶ εἶπεν ἡ γυνὴ πρὸς Ἰωάβ Ἰδοὺ ἡ κεφαλὴ αὐτοῦ ῥιφήσεται πρὸς σὲ διὰ τοῦ τείχους.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The matter is not so: but a man of the hill-country of Ephraim, Sheba the son of Bichri by name, hath lifted up his hand against the king, even against David; deliver him only, and I will depart from the city. And the woman said unto Joab, Behold, his head shall be thrown to thee over the wall.
					\item[NIV] That is not the case. A man named Sheba son of Bikri, from the hill country of Ephraim, has lifted up his hand against the king, against David. Hand over this one man, and I’ll withdraw from the city.” The woman said to Joab, “His head will be thrown to you from the wall.”
					\item[NRSV] That is not the case! But a man of the hill country of Ephraim, called Sheba son of Bichri, has lifted up his hand against King David; give him up alone, and I will withdraw from the city.” The woman said to Joab, “His head shall be thrown over the wall to you.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:22} % *2SAMUEL-20-22
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-22}

			Then the woman went unto all the people in her wisdom. And they cut off the head of Sheba the son of Bichri, and cast it out to Joab. And he blew a trumpet, and they retired from the city, every man to his tent. And Joab returned to Jerusalem unto the king.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתָּבוֹא֩ הָאִשָּׁ֨ה אֶל־כָּל־הָעָ֜ם בְּחָכְמָתָ֗הּ וַֽיִּכְרְת֞וּ אֶת־רֹ֨אשׁ שֶׁ֤בַע בֶּן־בִּכְרִי֙ וַיַּשְׁלִ֣כוּ אֶל־יוֹאָ֔ב וַיִּתְקַע֙ בַּשּׁוֹפָ֔ר וַיָּפֻ֥צוּ מֵֽעַל־הָעִ֖יר אִ֣ישׁ לְאֹהָלָ֑יו וְיוֹאָ֛ב שָׁ֥ב יְרוּשָׁלִַ֖ם אֶל־הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἰσῆλθεν ἡ γυνὴ πρὸς πάντα τὸν λαόν, καὶ ἐλάλησεν πρὸς πᾶσαν τὴν πόλιν ἐν τῇ σοφίᾳ αὐτῆς· καὶ ἀφεῖλεν τὴν κεφαλὴν Σάβεε υἱοῦ Βοχορεί, καὶ ἀφεῖλεν καὶ ἔβαλεν πρὸς Ἰωάβ. καὶ ἐσάλπισεν ἐν κερατίνῃ, καὶ διεσπάρησαν ἀπὸ τῆς πόλεως ἀπ᾽ αὐτοῦ ἀνὴρ εἰς τὰ σκηνώματα αὐτοῦ· καὶ Ἰωὰβ ἀπέστρεψεν εἰς Ἰερουσαλὴμ πρὸς τὸν βασιλέα.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then the woman went unto all the people in her wisdom. And they cut off the head of Sheba the son of Bichri, and threw it out to Joab. And he blew the trumpet, and they were dispersed from the city, every man to his tent. And Joab returned to Jerusalem unto the king.
					\item[NIV] Then the woman went to all the people with her wise advice, and they cut off the head of Sheba son of Bikri and threw it to Joab. So he sounded the trumpet, and his men dispersed from the city, each returning to his home. And Joab went back to the king in Jerusalem.
					\item[NRSV] Then the woman went to all the people with her wise plan. And they cut off the head of Sheba son of Bichri, and threw it out to Joab. So he blew the trumpet, and they dispersed from the city, and all went to their homes, while Joab returned to Jerusalem to the king.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:23} % *2SAMUEL-20-23
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-23}

			Now Joab was over all the host of Israel: and Benaiah the son of Jehoiada was over the Cherethites and over the Pelethites:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְיוֹאָ֕ב אֶ֥ל כָּל־הַצָּבָ֖א יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וּבְנָיָה֙ בֶּן־יְה֣וֹיָדָ֔ע הַכְּרֵתִ֖י וְעַל־הַפְּלֵתִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ Ἰωὰβ πρὸς πάσῃ τῇ δυνάμει Ἰσραήλ, καὶ Βαναίας υἱὸς Ἀχειλοὺθ ἐπὶ τοῦ Χελεθθεὶ καὶ ἐπὶ τοῦ Φελεθθεί,}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now Joab was over all the host of Israel; and Benaiah the son of Jehoiada was over the Cherethites and over the Pelethites;
					\item[NIV] Joab was over Israel’s entire army; Benaiah son of Jehoiada was over the Kerethites and Pelethites;
					\item[NRSV] Now Joab was in command of all the army of Israel; Benaiah son of Jehoiada was in command of the Cherethites and the Pelethites;

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 20:24} % *2SAMUEL-20-24
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-20-24}

			And Adoram was over the tribute: and Jehoshaphat the son of Ahilud was recorder:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַאֲדֹרָ֖ם עַל־הַמַּ֑ס וִיהוֹשָׁפָ֥ט בֶּן־אֲחִיל֖וּד הַמַּזְכִּֽיר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ Ἀδωνειρὰμ ἐπὶ τοῦ φόρου, καὶ Ἰωσαφὰθ υἱὸς Ἀχειλοὺθ ἀναμιμνήσκων,}

					\item[NIV] Adoniram was in charge of forced labor; Jehoshaphat son of Ahilud was recorder;
					\item[NRSV] Adoram was in charge of the forced labor; Jehoshaphat son of Ahilud was the recorder;

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

			And Sheva was scribe: and Zadok and Abiathar were the priests:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּשְׁוָ֖א סֹפֵ֑ר וְצָד֥וֹק וְאֶבְיָתָ֖ר כֹּהֲנִֽים׃}

			And Ira also the Jairite was a chief ruler about David.

			\begin{versebox}
