	% -----
	\subsubsection{2 Samuel 21} % *2SAMUEL-21
	% -----
		\label{2SAMUEL-21}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:1} % *2SAMUEL-21-1
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-1}

			Then there was a famine in the days of David three years, year after year; and David enquired of the LORD. And the LORD answered, It is for Saul, and for his bloody house, because he slew the Gibeonites.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְהִ֣י רָעָב֩ בִּימֵ֨י דָוִ֜ד שָׁלֹ֣שׁ שָׁנִ֗ים שָׁנָה֙ אַחֲרֵ֣י שָׁנָ֔ה וַיְבַקֵּ֥שׁ דָּוִ֖ד אֶת־פְּנֵ֣י יְהוָ֑ה וַיֹּ֣אמֶר יְהוָ֗ה אֶל־שָׁאוּל֙ וְאֶל־בֵּ֣ית הַדָּמִ֔ים עַל־אֲשֶׁר־הֵמִ֖ית אֶת־הַגִּבְעֹנִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ ἐγένετο λιμὸς ἐν ταῖς ἡμέραις Δαυεὶδ τρία ἔτη, ἐνιαυτὸς ἐχόμενος ἐνιαυτοῦ, καὶ ἐζήτησεν Δαυεὶδ τὸ πρόσωπον τοῦ κυρίου· καὶ εἶπεν Κύριος Ἐπὶ Σαοὺλ καὶ ἐπὶ τὸν οἶκον αὐτοῦ ἀδικία διὰ τὸ αὐτὸν θανάτῳ αἱμάτων, περὶ οὗ ἐθανάτωσεν τοὺς Γαβαωνείτας.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And there was a famine in the days of David three years, year after year; and David sought the face of Jehovah. And Jehovah said, It is for Saul, and for his bloody house, because he put to death the Gibeonites.
					\item[NIV] During the reign of David, there was a famine for three successive years; so David sought the face of the LORD. The LORD said, “It is on account of Saul and his blood-stained house; it is because he put the Gibeonites to death.”
					\item[NRSV] Now there was a famine in the days of David for three years, year after year; and David inquired of the LORD. The LORD said, “There is bloodguilt on Saul and on his house, because he put the Gibeonites to death.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:2} % *2SAMUEL-21-2
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-2}

			And the king called the Gibeonites, and said unto them; (now the Gibeonites were not of the children of Israel, but of the remnant of the Amorites; and the children of Israel had sworn unto them: and Saul sought to slay them in his zeal to the children of Israel and Judah.)

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּקְרָ֥א הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ לַגִּבְעֹנִ֖ים וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵיהֶ֑ם וְהַגִּבְעֹנִ֞ים לֹ֣א מִבְּנֵ֧י יִשְׂרָאֵ֣ל הֵ֗מָּה כִּ֚י אִם־מִיֶּ֣תֶר הָאֱמֹרִ֔י וּבְנֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ נִשְׁבְּע֣וּ לָהֶ֔ם וַיְבַקֵּ֤שׁ שָׁאוּל֙ לְהַכֹּתָ֔ם בְּקַנֹּאת֥וֹ לִבְנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל וִיהוּדָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐκάλεσεν ὁ βασιλεὺς Δαυεὶδ τοὺς Γαβαωνείτας καὶ εἶπεν πρὸς αὐτούς· καὶ οἱ Γαβαωνεῖται οὐχ υἱοὶ Ἰσραήλ εἰσιν, ὅτι ἀλλ᾽ ἢ ἐκ τοῦ αἵματος τοῦ Ἀμορραίου, καὶ οἱ υἱοὶ Ἰσραὴλ ὤμοσαν αὐτοῖς· καὶ ἐζήτησεν Σαοὺλ πατάξαι αὐτοὺς ἐν τῷ ζηλῶσαι αὐτὸν τοὺς υἱοὺς Ἰσραὴλ καὶ Ἰούδα·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the king called the Gibeonites, and said unto them (now the Gibeonites were not of the children of Israel, but of the remnant of the Amorites; and the children of Israel had sworn unto them: and Saul sought to slay them in his zeal for the children of Israel and Judah);
					\item[NIV] The king summoned the Gibeonites and spoke to them. (Now the Gibeonites were not a part of Israel but were survivors of the Amorites; the Israelites had sworn to spare them, but Saul in his zeal for Israel and Judah had tried to annihilate them.)
					\item[NRSV] So the king called the Gibeonites and spoke to them. (Now the Gibeonites were not of the people of Israel, but of the remnant of the Amorites; although the people of Israel had sworn to spare them, Saul had tried to wipe them out in his zeal for the people of Israel and Judah.)
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:3} % *2SAMUEL-21-3
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-3}

			Wherefore David said unto the Gibeonites, What shall I do for you? and wherewith shall I make the atonement, that ye may bless the inheritance of the LORD?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֤אמֶר דָּוִד֙ אֶל־הַגִּבְעֹנִ֔ים מָ֥ה אֶעֱשֶׂ֖ה לָכֶ֑ם וּבַמָּ֣ה אֲכַפֵּ֔ר וּבָרְכ֖וּ אֶת־נַחֲלַ֥ת יְהוָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Δαυεὶδ πρὸς τοὺς Γαβαωνείτας Τί ποιήσω ὑμῖν καὶ ἐν τίνι ἐξιλάσωμαι, καὶ εὐλογήσετε τὴν κληρονομίαν Κυρίου;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] and David said unto the Gibeonites, What shall I do for you? and wherewith shall I make atonement, that ye may bless the inheritance of Jehovah?
					\item[NIV] David asked the Gibeonites, “What shall I do for you? How shall I make atonement so that you will bless the LORD’s inheritance?”
					\item[NRSV] David said to the Gibeonites, “What shall I do for you? How shall I make expiation, that you may bless the heritage of the LORD?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:4} % *2SAMUEL-21-4
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-4}

			And the Gibeonites said unto him, We will have no silver nor gold of Saul, nor of his house; neither for us shalt thou kill any man in Israel. And he said, What ye shall say, that will I do for you.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֧אמְרוּ ל֣וֹ הַגִּבְעֹנִ֗ים לָ֜נוּ כֶּ֤סֶף וְזָהָב֙ עִם־שָׁא֣וּל וְעִם־בֵּית֔וֹ וְאֵֽין־לָ֥נוּ אִ֖ישׁ לְהָמִ֣ית בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיֹּ֛אמֶר מָֽה־אַתֶּ֥ם אֹמְרִ֖ים אֶעֱשֶׂ֥ה לָכֶֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπαν αὐτῷ οἱ Γαβαωνεῖται Οὐκ ἔστιν ἡμῖν ἀργύριον καὶ χρυσίον μετὰ Σαοὺλ καὶ μετὰ τοῦ οἴκου αὐτοῦ, καὶ οὐκ ἔστιν ἡμῖν ἀνὴρ θανατῶσαι ἐν Ἰσραήλ. καὶ εἶπεν Τί ὑμεῖς λέγετε καὶ ποιήσω ὑμῖν;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the Gibeonites said unto him, It is no matter of silver or gold between us and Saul, or his house; neither is it for us to put any man to death in Israel. And he said, What ye shall say, that will I do for you.
					\item[NIV] The Gibeonites answered him, “We have no right to demand silver or gold from Saul or his family, nor do we have the right to put anyone in Israel to death.” “What do you want me to do for you?” David asked.
					\item[NRSV] The Gibeonites said to him, “It is not a matter of silver or gold between us and Saul or his house; neither is it for us to put anyone to death in Israel.” He said, “What do you say that I should do for you?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:5} % *2SAMUEL-21-5
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-5}

			And they answered the king, The man that consumed us, and that devised against us that we should be destroyed from remaining in any of the coasts of Israel,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּֽאמְרוּ֙ אֶל־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ הָאִישׁ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר כִּלָּ֔נוּ וַאֲשֶׁ֖ר דִּמָּה־לָ֑נוּ נִשְׁמַ֕דְנוּ מֵֽהִתְיַצֵּ֖ב בְּכָל־גְּבֻ֥ל יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπαν πρὸς τὸν βασιλέα Ὁ ἀνὴρ συνετέλεσεν ἐφ᾽ ἡμᾶς καὶ ἐδίωξεν ἡμᾶς, ὃς παρελογίσατο ἐξολεθρεῦσαι ἡμᾶς· ἀφανίσωμεν αὐτὸν τοῦ μὴ ἑστάναι αὐτὸν ἐν παντὶ ὁρίῳ Ἰσραήλ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And they said unto the king, The man that consumed us, and that devised against us, that we should be destroyed from remaining in any of the borders of Israel,
					\item[NIV] They answered the king, “As for the man who destroyed us and plotted against us so that we have been decimated and have no place anywhere in Israel,
					\item[NRSV] They said to the king, “The man who consumed us and planned to destroy us, so that we should have no place in all the territory of Israel—
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:6} % *2SAMUEL-21-6
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-6}

			Let seven men of his sons be delivered unto us, and we will hang them up unto the LORD in Gibeah of Saul, whom the LORD did choose. And the king said, I will give them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ינתן־[יֻתַּן־] לָ֜נוּ שִׁבְעָ֤ה אֲנָשִׁים֙ מִבָּנָ֔יו וְהוֹקַֽעֲנוּם֙ לַֽיהוָ֔ה בְּגִבְעַ֥ת שָׁא֖וּל בְּחִ֣יר יְהוָ֑ה וַיֹּ֥אמֶר הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ אֲנִ֥י אֶתֵּֽן׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{δότω ἡμῖν ἑπτὰ ἄνδρας ἐκ τῶν υἱῶν αὐτοῦ, καὶ ἐξηλιάσωμεν αὐτοὺς τῷ κυρίῳ ἐν Γαβαὼν Σαοὺλ ἐκλεκτοὺς Κυρίου. καὶ εἶπεν ὁ βασιλεύς Ἐγὼ δώσω.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] let seven men of his sons be delivered unto us, and we will hang them up unto Jehovah in Gibeah of Saul, the chosen of Jehovah. And the king said, I will give them.
					\item[NIV] let seven of his male descendants be given to us to be killed and their bodies exposed before the LORD at Gibeah of Saul—the LORD’s chosen one.” So the king said, “I will give them to you.”
					\item[NRSV] let seven of his sons be handed over to us, and we will impale them before the LORD at Gibeon on the mountain of the LORD.” The king said, “I will hand them over.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:7} % *2SAMUEL-21-7
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-7}

			But the king spared Mephibosheth, the son of Jonathan the son of Saul, because of the LORD’s oath that was between them, between David and Jonathan the son of Saul.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּחְמֹ֣ל הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ עַל־מְפִי־בֹ֖שֶׁת בֶּן־יְהוֹנָתָ֣ן בֶּן־שָׁא֑וּל עַל־שְׁבֻעַ֤ת יְהוָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בֵּֽינֹתָ֔ם בֵּ֣ין דָּוִ֔ד וּבֵ֖ין יְהוֹנָתָ֥ן בֶּן־שָׁאֽוּל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐφείσατο ὁ βασιλεὺς ἐπὶ Μεμφιβόσθε υἱὸν Ἰωναθὰν υἱοῦ Σαοὺλ διὰ τὸν ὅρκον Κυρίου τὸν ἀνὰ μέσον αὐτῶν, καὶ ἀνὰ μέσον Δαυεὶδ καὶ ἀνὰ μέσον Ἰωναθὰν υἱοῦ Σαούλ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But the king spared Mephibosheth, the son of Jonathan the son of Saul, because of Jehovah's oath that was between them, between David and Jonathan the son of Saul.
					\item[NIV] The king spared Mephibosheth son of Jonathan, the son of Saul, because of the oath before the LORD between David and Jonathan son of Saul.
					\item[NRSV] But the king spared Mephibosheth, the son of Saul’s son Jonathan, because of the oath of the LORD that was between them, between David and Jonathan son of Saul.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:8} % *2SAMUEL-21-8
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-8}

			But the king took the two sons of Rizpah the daughter of Aiah, whom she bare unto Saul, Armoni and Mephibosheth; and the five sons of Michal the daughter of Saul, whom she brought up for Adriel the son of Barzillai the Meholathite:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּקַּ֣ח הַמֶּ֡לֶךְ אֶת־שְׁ֠נֵי בְּנֵ֨י רִצְפָּ֤ה בַת־אַיָּה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יָלְדָ֣ה לְשָׁא֔וּל אֶת־אַרְמֹנִ֖י וְאֶת־מְפִבֹ֑שֶׁת וְאֶת־חֲמֵ֗שֶׁת בְּנֵי֙ מִיכַ֣ל בַּת־שָׁא֔וּל אֲשֶׁ֥ר יָלְדָ֛ה לְעַדְרִיאֵ֥ל בֶּן־בַּרְזִלַּ֖י הַמְּחֹלָתִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἔλαβεν ὁ βασιλεὺς τοὺς δύο υἱοὺς Ῥεσφὰ θυγατρὸς Αἰὰ οὓς ἔτεκεν τῷ Σαούλ, τὸν Ἐρμωνοεὶ καὶ τὸν Μεμφιβόσθε, καὶ τοὺς πέντε υἱοὺς Μιχὸλ θυγατρὸς Σαοὺλ οὓς ἔτεκεν τῷ Σερεὶ υἱῷ Βερζελλεὶ τῷ Μωουλαθεί,}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But the king took the two sons of Rizpah the daughter of Aiah, whom she bare unto Saul, Armoni and Mephibosheth; and the five sons of Michal the daughter of Saul, whom she bare to Adriel the son of Barzillai the Meholathite:
					\item[NIV] But the king took Armoni and Mephibosheth, the two sons of Aiah’s daughter Rizpah, whom she had borne to Saul, together with the five sons of Saul’s daughter Merab, whom she had borne to Adriel son of Barzillai the Meholathite.
					\item[NRSV] The king took the two sons of Rizpah daughter of Aiah, whom she bore to Saul, Armoni and Mephibosheth; and the five sons of Merab daughter of Saul, whom she bore to Adriel son of Barzillai the Meholathite;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:9} % *2SAMUEL-21-9
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-9}

			And he delivered them into the hands of the Gibeonites, and they hanged them in the hill before the LORD: and they fell all seven together, and were put to death in the days of harvest, in the first days, in the beginning of barley harvest.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַֽיִּתְּנֵ֞ם בְּיַ֣ד הַגִּבְעֹנִ֗ים וַיֹּקִיעֻ֤ם בָּהָר֙ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהוָ֔ה וַיִּפְּל֥וּ שְׁבַעְתָּ֖ם יָ֑חַד וְהֵ֨מָּה הֻמְת֜וּ בִּימֵ֤י קָצִיר֙ בָּרִ֣אשֹׁנִ֔ים בִּתְחִלַּ֖ת קְצִ֥יר שְׂעֹרִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἔδωκεν αὐτοὺς ἐν χειρὶ τῶν Γαβαωνειτῶν· καὶ ἐξηλίασαν αὐτοὺς ἐν τῷ ὄρει ἔναντι Κυρίου, καὶ ἔπεσαν οἱ ἑπτὰ αὐτοὶ ἐπὶ τὸ αὐτό· καὶ αὐτοὶ δὲ ἐθανατώθησαν ἐν ἡμέραις θερισμοῦ ἐν πρώτοις, ἐν ἀρχῇ θερισμοῦ κριθῶν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] and he delivered them into the hands of the Gibeonites, and they hanged them in the mountain before Jehovah, and they fell all seven together. And they were put to death in the days of harvest, in the first days, at the beginning of barley harvest.
					\item[NIV] He handed them over to the Gibeonites, who killed them and exposed their bodies on a hill before the LORD. All seven of them fell together; they were put to death during the first days of the harvest, just as the barley harvest was beginning.
					\item[NRSV] he gave them into the hands of the Gibeonites, and they impaled them on the mountain before the LORD. The seven of them perished together. They were put to death in the first days of harvest, at the beginning of barley harvest.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:10} % *2SAMUEL-21-10
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-10}

			And Rizpah the daughter of Aiah took sackcloth, and spread it for her upon the rock, from the beginning of harvest until water dropped upon them out of heaven, and suffered neither the birds of the air to rest on them by day, nor the beasts of the field by night.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתִּקַּ֣ח רִצְפָּה֩ בַת־אַיָּ֨ה אֶת־הַשַּׂ֜ק וַתַּטֵּ֨הוּ לָ֤הּ אֶל־הַצּוּר֙ מִתְּחִלַּ֣ת קָצִ֔יר עַ֛ד נִתַּךְ־מַ֥יִם עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם מִן־הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם וְלֹֽא־נָתְנָה֩ ע֨וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֜יִם לָנ֤וּחַ עֲלֵיהֶם֙ יוֹמָ֔ם וְאֶת־חַיַּ֥ת הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה לָֽיְלָה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἔλαβεν Ῥεσφὰ θυγάτηρ Αἰὰ τὸν σάκκον καὶ ἔπηξεν αὐτῇ πρὸς τὴν πέτραν ἐν ἀρχῇ θερισμοῦ κριθῶν ἕως ἔσταξεν ἐπ᾽ αὐτοὺς ὕδωρ ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ, καὶ οὐκ ἔδωκεν τὰ πετεινὰ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ καταπαῦσαι ἐπ᾽ αὐτοὺς ἡμέρας καὶ τὰ θηρία τοῦ ἀγροῦ νυκτός.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Rizpah the daughter of Aiah took sackcloth, and spread it for her upon the rock, from the beginning of harvest until water was poured upon them from heaven; and she suffered neither the birds of the heavens to rest on them by day, nor the beasts of the field by night.
					\item[NIV] Rizpah daughter of Aiah took sackcloth and spread it out for herself on a rock. From the beginning of the harvest till the rain poured down from the heavens on the bodies, she did not let the birds touch them by day or the wild animals by night.
					\item[NRSV] Then Rizpah the daughter of Aiah took sackcloth, and spread it on a rock for herself, from the beginning of harvest until rain fell on them from the heavens; she did not allow the birds of the air to come on the bodies by day, or the wild animals by night.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:11} % *2SAMUEL-21-11
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-11}

			And it was told David what Rizpah the daughter of Aiah, the concubine of Saul, had done.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֻּגַּ֖ד לְדָוִ֑ד אֵ֧ת אֲשֶׁר־עָשְׂתָ֛ה רִצְפָּ֥ה בַת־אַיָּ֖ה פִּלֶ֥גֶשׁ שָׁאֽוּל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀπηγγέλη τῷ Δαυεὶδ ὅσα ἐποίησεν Ῥεσφὰ θυγάτηρ Αἰὰ παλλακὴ Σαούλ· καὶ ἐξελύθησαν, καὶ κατέλαβεν αὐτοὺς Δὰν υἱὸς Ἰωὰ ἐκ τῶν ἀπογόνων τῶν γιγάντων.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And it was told David what Rizpah the daughter of Aiah, the concubine of Saul, had done.
					\item[NIV] When David was told what Aiah’s daughter Rizpah, Saul’s concubine, had done,
					\item[NRSV] When David was told what Rizpah daughter of Aiah, the concubine of Saul, had done,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:12} % *2SAMUEL-21-12
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-12}

			And David went and took the bones of Saul and the bones of Jonathan his son from the men of Jabeshgilead, which had stolen them from the street of Bethshan, where the Philistines had hanged them, when the Philistines had slain Saul in Gilboa:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֵּ֣לֶךְ דָּוִ֗ד וַיִּקַּ֞ח אֶת־עַצְמ֤וֹת שָׁאוּל֙ וְאֶת־עַצְמוֹת֙ יְהוֹנָתָ֣ן בְּנ֔וֹ מֵאֵ֕ת בַּעֲלֵ֖י יָבֵ֣ישׁ גִּלְעָ֑ד אֲשֶׁר֩ גָּנְב֨וּ אֹתָ֜ם מֵרְחֹ֣ב בֵּֽית־שַׁ֗ן אֲשֶׁ֨ר תְּלָא֥וּם שם שָׁ֙מָּה֙ פְּלִשְׁתִּ֔ים בְּי֨וֹם הַכּ֧וֹת פְּלִשְׁתִּ֛ים אֶת־שָׁא֖וּל בַּגִּלְבֹּֽעַ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐπορεύθη Δαυεὶδ καὶ ἔλαβεν τὰ ὀστᾶ Σαοὺλ καὶ τὰ ὀστᾶ Ἰωναθὰν τοῦ υἱοῦ αὐτοῦ παρὰ τῶν ἀνδρῶν υἱῶν Ἰαβεὶς Γαλαάδ, οἳ ἔκλεψαν αὐτοὺς ἐκ τῆς πλατείας Βαίθ, ὅτι ἔστησαν αὐτοὺς ἐκεῖ οἱ ἀλλόφυλοι ἐν ἡμέρᾳ ᾗ ἐπάταξαν οἱ ἀλλόφυλοι τὸν Σαοὺλ ἐν Γελβοῦε.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David went and took the bones of Saul and the bones of Jonathan his son from the men of Jabesh-gilead, who had stolen them from the street of Beth-shan, where the Philistines had hanged them, in the day that the Philistines slew Saul in Gilboa;
					\item[NIV] he went and took the bones of Saul and his son Jonathan from the citizens of Jabesh Gilead. (They had stolen their bodies from the public square at Beth Shan, where the Philistines had hung them after they struck Saul down on Gilboa.)
					\item[NRSV] David went and took the bones of Saul and the bones of his son Jonathan from the people of Jabesh-gilead, who had stolen them from the public square of Beth-shan, where the Philistines had hung them up, on the day the Philistines killed Saul on Gilboa.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:13} % *2SAMUEL-21-13
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-13}

			And he brought up from thence the bones of Saul and the bones of Jonathan his son; and they gathered the bones of them that were hanged.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּ֤עַל מִשָּׁם֙ אֶת־עַצְמ֣וֹת שָׁא֔וּל וְאֶת־עַצְמ֖וֹת יְהוֹנָתָ֣ן בְּנ֑וֹ וַיַּ֣אַסְפ֔וּ אֶת־עַצְמ֖וֹת הַמּוּקָעִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀνήνεγκεν ἐκεῖθεν τὰ ὀστᾶ Σαοὺλ καὶ τὰ ὀστᾶ Ἰωναθὰν τοῦ υἱοῦ αὐτοῦ, καὶ συνήγαγεν τὰ ὀστᾶ τῶν ἐξηλιασμένων.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] and he brought up from thence the bones of Saul and the bones of Jonathan his son: and they gathered the bones of them that were hanged.
					\item[NIV] David brought the bones of Saul and his son Jonathan from there, and the bones of those who had been killed and exposed were gathered up.
					\item[NRSV] He brought up from there the bones of Saul and the bones of his son Jonathan; and they gathered the bones of those who had been impaled.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:14} % *2SAMUEL-21-14
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-14}

			And the bones of Saul and Jonathan his son buried they in the country of Benjamin in Zelah, in the sepulchre of Kish his father: and they performed all that the king commanded. And after that God was intreated for the land.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּקְבְּר֣וּ אֶת־עַצְמוֹת־שָׁא֣וּל וִיהוֹנָֽתָן־בְּ֠נוֹ בְּאֶ֨רֶץ בִּנְיָמִ֜ן בְּצֵלָ֗ע בְּקֶ֙בֶר֙ קִ֣ישׁ אָבִ֔יו וַֽיַּעֲשׂ֔וּ כֹּ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֖ה הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיֵּעָתֵ֧ר אֱלֹהִ֛ים לָאָ֖רֶץ אַֽחֲרֵי־כֵֽן׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἔθαψαν τὰ ὀστᾶ Σαοὺλ καὶ τὰ ὀστᾶ Ἰωναθὰν τοῦ υἱοῦ αὐτοῦ καὶ τῶν ἡλιασθέντων τῶν ἐν γῇ Βενιαμεὶν ἐν τῇ πλευρᾷ ἐν τῷ τάφῳ Κεὶς τοῦ πατρὸς αὐτοῦ· καὶ ἐποίησαν πάντα ὅσα ἐνετείλατο ὁ βασιλεύς, καὶ ἐπήκουσεν ὁ θεὸς τῇ γῇ μετὰ ταῦτα.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And they buried the bones of Saul and Jonathan his son in the country of Benjamin in Zela, in the sepulchre of Kish his father: and they performed all that the king commanded. And after that God was entreated for the land.
					\item[NIV] They buried the bones of Saul and his son Jonathan in the tomb of Saul’s father Kish, at Zela in Benjamin, and did everything the king commanded. After that, God answered prayer in behalf of the land.
					\item[NRSV] They buried the bones of Saul and of his son Jonathan in the land of Benjamin in Zela, in the tomb of his father Kish; they did all that the king commanded. After that, God heeded supplications for the land.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:15} % *2SAMUEL-21-15
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-15}

			Moreover the Philistines had yet war again with Israel; and David went down, and his servants with him, and fought against the Philistines: and David waxed faint.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתְּהִי־ע֧וֹד מִלְחָמָ֛ה לַפְּלִשְׁתִּ֖ים אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיֵּ֨רֶד דָּוִ֜ד וַעֲבָדָ֥יו עִמּ֛וֹ וַיִּלָּחֲמ֥וּ אֶת־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֖ים וַיָּ֥עַף דָּוִֽד׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ ἐγενήθη ἔτι πόλεμος τοῖς ἀλλοφύλοις μετὰ Ἰσραήλ· καὶ κατέβη Δαυεὶδ καὶ οἱ παῖδες αὐτοῦ μετ᾽ αὐτοῦ καὶ ἐπολέμησαν μετὰ τῶν ἀλλοφύλων· καὶ ἐπορεύθη Δαυείδ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the Philistines had war again with Israel; and David went down, and his servants with him, and fought against the Philistines. And David waxed faint;
					\item[NIV] Once again there was a battle between the Philistines and Israel. David went down with his men to fight against the Philistines, and he became exhausted.
					\item[NRSV] The Philistines went to war again with Israel, and David went down together with his servants. They fought against the Philistines, and David grew weary.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:16} % *2SAMUEL-21-16
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-16}

			And Ishbibenob, which was of the sons of the giant, the weight of whose spear weighed three hundred shekels of brass in weight, he being girded with a new sword, thought to have slain David.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְיִשְׁבִּ֨י בְּנֹ֜ב אֲשֶׁ֣ר ׀ בִּילִידֵ֣י הָרָפָ֗ה וּמִשְׁקַ֤ל קֵינוֹ֙ שְׁלֹ֤שׁ מֵאוֹת֙ מִשְׁקַ֣ל נְחֹ֔שֶׁת וְה֖וּא חָג֣וּר חֲדָשָׁ֑ה וַיֹּ֖אמֶר לְהַכּ֥וֹת אֶת־דָּוִֽד׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ Ἰεσβὶ ὃς ἦν ἐν τοῖς ἐκγόνοις τοῦ Ῥαφά, καὶ ὁ σταθμὸς τοῦ δόρατος αὐτοῦ τριακοσίων σίκλων ὁλκῇ χαλκοῦ, καὶ αὐτὸς περιεζωσμένος κορύνην, καὶ διενοεῖτο πατάξαι τὸν Δαυείδ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] and Ishbibenob, who was of the sons of the giant, the weight of whose spear was three hundred shekels of brass in weight, he being girded with a new sword, thought to have slain David.
					\item[NIV] And Ishbi-Benob, one of the descendants of Rapha, whose bronze spearhead weighed three hundred shekels and who was armed with a new sword, said he would kill David.
					\item[NRSV] Ishbi-benob, one of the descendants of the giants, whose spear weighed three hundred shekels of bronze, and who was fitted out with new weapons, said he would kill David.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:17} % *2SAMUEL-21-17
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-17}

			But Abishai the son of Zeruiah succoured him, and smote the Philistine, and killed him. Then the men of David sware unto him, saying, Thou shalt go no more out with us to battle, that thou quench not the light of Israel.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיַּֽעֲזָר־לוֹ֙ אֲבִישַׁ֣י בֶּן־צְרוּיָ֔ה וַיַּ֥ךְ אֶת־הַפְּלִשְׁתִּ֖י וַיְמִיתֵ֑הוּ אָ֣ז נִשְׁבְּעוּ֩ אַנְשֵׁי־דָוִ֨ד ל֜וֹ לֵאמֹ֗ר לֹא־תֵצֵ֨א ע֤וֹד אִתָּ֙נוּ֙ לַמִּלְחָמָ֔ה וְלֹ֥א תְכַבֶּ֖ה אֶת־נֵ֥ר יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐβοήθησεν αὐτῷ Ἀβεσσὰ υἱὸς Σαρουίας, καὶ ἐπάταξεν τὸν ἀλλόφυλον καὶ ἐθανάτωσεν αὐτόν· τότε ὤμοσαν οἱ ἄνδρες Δαυεὶδ λέγοντες Οὐκ ἐξελεύσῃ ἔτι μεθ᾽ ἡμῶν εἰς πόλεμον, καὶ οὐ μὴ σβέσῃς τὸν λύχνον Ἰσραήλ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But Abishai the son of Zeruiah succored him, and smote the Philistine, and killed him. Then the men of David sware unto him, saying, Thou shalt go no more out with us to battle, that thou quench not the lamp of Israel.
					\item[NIV] But Abishai son of Zeruiah came to David’s rescue; he struck the Philistine down and killed him. Then David’s men swore to him, saying, “Never again will you go out with us to battle, so that the lamp of Israel will not be extinguished.”
					\item[NRSV] But Abishai son of Zeruiah came to his aid, and attacked the Philistine and killed him. Then David’s men swore to him, “You shall not go out with us to battle any longer, so that you do not quench the lamp of Israel.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:18} % *2SAMUEL-21-18
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-18}

			And it came to pass after this, that there was again a battle with the Philistines at Gob: then Sibbechai the Hushathite slew Saph, which was of the sons of the giant.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַֽיְהִי֙ אַֽחֲרֵי־כֵ֔ן וַתְּהִי־ע֧וֹד הַמִּלְחָמָ֛ה בְּג֖וֹב עִם־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֑ים אָ֣ז הִכָּ֗ה סִבְּכַי֙ הַחֻ֣שָׁתִ֔י אֶת־סַ֕ף אֲשֶׁ֖ר בִּילִדֵ֥י הָרָפָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ ἐγενήθη μετὰ ταῦτα ἔτι πόλεμος ἐν Γὲθ μετὰ τῶν ἀλλοφύλων· τότε ἐπάταξεν Ὀεβοχὰ ὁ Ἁστατωθεὶ τὸν Σὲφ τὸν ἐν τοῖς ἐκγόνοις τοῦ Ῥαφά.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And it came to pass after this, that there was again war with the Philistines at Gob: then Sibbecai the Hushathite slew Saph, who was of the sons of the giant.
					\item[NIV] In the course of time, there was another battle with the Philistines, at Gob. At that time Sibbekai the Hushathite killed Saph, one of the descendants of Rapha.
					\item[NRSV] After this a battle took place with the Philistines, at Gob; then Sibbecai the Hushathite killed Saph, who was one of the descendants of the giants.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:19} % *2SAMUEL-21-19
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-19}

			And there was again a battle in Gob with the Philistines, where Elhanan the son of Jaareoregim, a Bethlehemite, slew the brother of Goliath the Gittite, the staff of whose spear was like a weaver’s beam.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתְּהִי־ע֧וֹד הַמִּלְחָמָ֛ה בְּג֖וֹב עִם־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֑ים וַיַּ֡ךְ אֶלְחָנָן֩ בֶּן־יַעְרֵ֨י אֹרְגִ֜ים בֵּ֣ית הַלַּחְמִ֗י אֵ֚ת גָּלְיָ֣ת הַגִּתִּ֔י וְעֵ֣ץ חֲנִית֔וֹ כִּמְנ֖וֹר אֹרְגִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐγένετο ὁ πόλεμος ἐν Ῥὸμ μετὰ τῶν ἀλλοφύλων· καὶ ἐπάταξεν Ἐλεανὰν υἱὸς Ἀριωργεὶμ ὁ Βαιθλεεμμείτης τὸν Γοδολίαν τὸν Χετταῖον, καὶ τὸ ξύλον τοῦ δόρατος αὐτοῦ ὡς ἀντίον ὑφαινόντων.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And there was again war with the Philistines at Gob; and Elhanan the son of Jaareoregim the Beth-lehemite slew Goliath the Gittite, the staff of whose spear was like a weaver's beam.
					\item[NIV] In another battle with the Philistines at Gob, Elhanan son of Jair the Bethlehemite killed the brother of Goliath the Gittite, who had a spear with a shaft like a weaver’s rod.
					\item[NRSV] Then there was another battle with the Philistines at Gob; and Elhanan son of Jaare-oregim, the Bethlehemite, killed Goliath the Gittite, the shaft of whose spear was like a weaver’s beam.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:20} % *2SAMUEL-21-20
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-20}

			And there was yet a battle in Gath, where was a man of great stature, that had on every hand six fingers, and on every foot six toes, four and twenty in number; and he also was born to the giant.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתְּהִי־ע֥וֹד מִלְחָמָ֖ה בְּגַ֑ת וַיְהִ֣י ׀ אִ֣ישׁ מָד֗וֹן וְאֶצְבְּעֹ֣ת יָדָיו֩ וְאֶצְבְּעֹ֨ת רַגְלָ֜יו שֵׁ֣שׁ וָשֵׁ֗שׁ עֶשְׂרִ֤ים וְאַרְבַּע֙ מִסְפָּ֔ר וְגַם־ה֖וּא יֻלַּ֥ד לְהָרָפָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐγένετο ἔτι πόλεμος ἐν Γέθ· καὶ ἦν ἀνὴρ Μαδών, καὶ οἱ δάκτυλοι τῶν χειρῶν αὐτοῦ καὶ οἱ δάκτυλοι τῶν ποδῶν αὐτοῦ ἓξ καὶ ἕξ, εἴκοσι τέσσαρες ἀριθμῷ· καί γε αὐτὸς ἐτέχθη τῷ Ῥαφά.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And there was again war at Gath, where was a man of great stature, that had on every hand six fingers, and on every foot six toes, four and twenty in number; and he also was born to the giant.
					\item[NIV] In still another battle, which took place at Gath, there was a huge man with six fingers on each hand and six toes on each foot—twenty-four in all. He also was descended from Rapha.
					\item[NRSV] There was again war at Gath, where there was a man of great size, who had six fingers on each hand, and six toes on each foot, twenty-four in number; he too was descended from the giants.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:21} % *2SAMUEL-21-21
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-21}

			And when he defied Israel, Jonathan the son of Shimeah the brother of David slew him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְחָרֵ֖ף אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיַּכֵּ֙הוּ֙ יְה֣וֹנָתָ֔ן שִׁמְעָ֖ה אֲחִ֥י דָוִֽד׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ὠνείδισεν τὸν Ἰσραήλ, καὶ ἐπάταξεν αὐτὸν Ἰωναθὰν υἱός Σεμεεὶ ἀδελφοῦ Δαυείδ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And when he defied Israel, Jonathan the son of Shimei, David's brother, slew him.
					\item[NIV] When he taunted Israel, Jonathan son of Shimeah, David’s brother, killed him.
					\item[NRSV] When he taunted Israel, Jonathan son of David’s brother Shimei, killed him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 21:22} % *2SAMUEL-21-22
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-21-22}

			These four were born to the giant in Gath, and fell by the hand of David, and by the hand of his servants.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֶת־אַרְבַּ֥עַת אֵ֛לֶּה יֻלְּד֥וּ לְהָרָפָ֖ה בְּגַ֑ת וַיִּפְּל֥וּ בְיַד־דָּוִ֖ד וּבְיַ֥ד עֲבָדָֽיו׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{οἱ τέσσαρες οὗτοι ἐτέχθησαν ἀπόγονοι τῶν γιγάντων ἐν Γὲθ τῷ Ῥαφὰ οἶκος, καὶ ἔπεσαν ἐν χειρὶ Δαυεὶδ καὶ ἐν χειρὶ τῶν δούλων αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] These four were born to the giant in Gath; and they fell by the hand of David, and by the hand of his servants.
					\item[NIV] These four were descendants of Rapha in Gath, and they fell at the hands of David and his men.
					\item[NRSV] These four were descended from the giants in Gath; they fell by the hands of David and his servants.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{2 Samuel 22} % *2SAMUEL-22
	% -----
		\label{2SAMUEL-22}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:1} % *2SAMUEL-22-1
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-1}

			And David spake unto the LORD the words of this song in the day that the LORD had delivered him out of the hand of all his enemies, and out of the hand of Saul:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיְדַבֵּ֤ר דָּוִד֙ לַֽיהוָ֔ה אֶת־דִּבְרֵ֖י הַשִּׁירָ֣ה הַזֹּ֑את בְּיוֹם֩ הִצִּ֨יל יְהוָ֥ה אֹת֛וֹ מִכַּ֥ף כָּל־אֹיְבָ֖יו וּמִכַּ֥ף שָׁאֽוּל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ ἐλάλησεν Δαυεὶδ τῷ κυρίῳ τοὺς λόγους τῆς ᾠδῆς ταύτης ἐν ᾗ ἡμέρᾳ ἐξείλατο αὐτὸν Κύριος ἐκ χειρὸς πάντων τῶν ἐχθρῶν αὐτοῦ καὶ ἐκ χειρὸς Σαούλ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David spake unto Jehovah the words of this song in the day that Jehovah delivered him out of the hand of all his enemies, and out of the hand of Saul:
					\item[NIV] David sang to the LORD the words of this song when the LORD delivered him from the hand of all his enemies and from the hand of Saul.
					\item[NRSV] David spoke to the LORD the words of this song on the day when the LORD delivered him from the hand of all his enemies, and from the hand of Saul.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:2} % *2SAMUEL-22-2
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-2}

			And he said, The LORD is my rock, and my fortress, and my deliverer;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּאמַ֑ר יְהוָ֛ה סַֽלְעִ֥י וּמְצֻדָתִ֖י וּמְפַלְטִי־לִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν ᾨδή. Κύριε, πέτρα μου καὶ ὀχύρωμά μου, καὶ ἐξαιρούμενός με ἐμοί·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] and he said, Jehovah is my rock, and my fortress, and my deliverer, even mine;
					\item[NIV] He said: “The LORD is my rock, my fortress and my deliverer;
					\item[NRSV] He said: The LORD is my rock, my fortress, and my deliverer,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:3} % *2SAMUEL-22-3
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-3}

			The God of my rock; in him will I trust: he is my shield, and the horn of my salvation, my high tower, and my refuge, my saviour; thou savest me from violence.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֱלֹהֵ֥י צוּרִ֖י אֶחֱסֶה־בּ֑וֹ מָגִנִּ֞י וְקֶ֣רֶן יִשְׁעִ֗י מִשְׂגַּבִּי֙ וּמְנוּסִ֔י מֹשִׁעִ֕י מֵחָמָ֖ס תֹּשִׁעֵֽנִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὁ θεός μου φύλαξ ἔσται μου, πεποιθὼς ἔσομαι ἐπ᾽ αὐτῷ· ὑπερασπιστής μου καὶ κέρας σωτηρίας μου, ἀντιλήμπτωρ μου καὶ καταφυγή μου σωτηρίας μου· ἐξ ἀδίκου σώσεις με.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] God, my rock, in him will I take refuge; My shield, and the horn of my salvation, my high tower, and my refuge; My saviour, thou savest me from violence.
					\item[NIV] my God is my rock, in whom I take refuge, my shield and the horn of my salvation. He is my stronghold, my refuge and my savior— from violent people you save me.
					\item[NRSV] my God, my rock, in whom I take refuge, my shield and the horn of my salvation, my stronghold and my refuge, my savior; you save me from violence.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:4} % *2SAMUEL-22-4
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-4}

			I will call on the LORD, who is worthy to be praised: so shall I be saved from mine enemies.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְהֻלָּ֖ל אֶקְרָ֣א יְהוָ֑ה וּמֵאֹיְבַ֖י אִוָּשֵֽׁעַ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{αἰνετὸν ἐπικαλέσομαι Κύριον, καὶ ἐκ τῶν ἐχθρῶν μου σωθήσομαι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I will call upon Jehovah, who is worthy to be praised: So shall I be saved from mine enemies.
					\item[NIV] “I called to the LORD, who is worthy of praise, and have been saved from my enemies.
					\item[NRSV] I call upon the LORD, who is worthy to be praised, and I am saved from my enemies.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:5} % *2SAMUEL-22-5
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-5}

			When the waves of death compassed me, the floods of ungodly men made me afraid;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֥י אֲפָפֻ֖נִי מִשְׁבְּרֵי־מָ֑וֶת נַחֲלֵ֥י בְלִיַּ֖עַל יְבַעֲתֻֽנִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι περιέσχον με συντριμμοὶ θανάτου, χείμαρροι ἀνομίας ἐθάμβησάν με·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For the waves of death compassed me; The floods of ungodliness made me afraid:
					\item[NIV] The waves of death swirled about me; the torrents of destruction overwhelmed me.
					\item[NRSV] For the waves of death encompassed me, the torrents of perdition assailed me;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:6} % *2SAMUEL-22-6
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-6}

			The sorrows of hell compassed me about; the snares of death prevented me;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חֶבְלֵ֥י שְׁא֖וֹל סַבֻּ֑נִי קִדְּמֻ֖נִי מֹֽקְשֵׁי־מָֽוֶת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὠδῖνες θανάτου ἐκύκλωσάν με, προέφθασάν με σκληρότητες θανάτου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The cords of Sheol were round about me; The snares of death came upon me.
					\item[NIV] The cords of the grave coiled around me; the snares of death confronted me.
					\item[NRSV] the cords of Sheol entangled me, the snares of death confronted me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:7} % *2SAMUEL-22-7
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-7}

			In my distress I called upon the LORD, and cried to my God: and he did hear my voice out of his temple, and my cry did enter into his ears.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּצַּר־לִי֙ אֶקְרָ֣א יְהוָ֔ה וְאֶל־אֱלֹהַ֖י אֶקְרָ֑א וַיִּשְׁמַ֤ע מֵהֵֽיכָלוֹ֙ קוֹלִ֔י וְשַׁוְעָתִ֖י בְּאָזְנָֽיו׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐν τῷ θλίβεσθαί με ἐπικαλέσομαι Κύριον, καὶ πρὸς τὸν θεόν μου βοήσομαι, καὶ ἐπακούσεται ἐκ ναοῦ αὐτοῦ φωνῆς μου, καὶ ἡ κραυγή μου ἐν τοῖς ὠσὶν αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In my distress I called upon Jehovah; Yea, I called unto my God: And he heard my voice out of his temple, And my cry came into his ears.
					\item[NIV] “In my distress I called to the LORD; I called out to my God. From his temple he heard my voice; my cry came to his ears.
					\item[NRSV] In my distress I called upon the LORD; to my God I called. From his temple he heard my voice, and my cry came to his ears.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:8} % *2SAMUEL-22-8
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-8}

			Then the earth shook and trembled; the foundations of heaven moved and shook, because he was wroth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּתְגָּעַ֤שׁ וַתִּרְעַשׁ֙ הָאָ֔רֶץ מוֹסְד֥וֹת הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם יִרְגָּ֑זוּ וַיִּֽתְגָּעֲשׁ֖וּ כִּֽי־חָ֥רָה לֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐταράχθη καὶ ἐσείσθη ἡ γῆ, καὶ τὰ θεμέλια τοῦ οὐρανοῦ συνεταράχθησαν, καὶ ἐσπαράχθησαν, ὃτι ἐθυμώθη Κύριος αὐτοῖς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then the earth shook and trembled, The foundations of heaven quaked And were shaken, because he was wroth.
					\item[NIV] The earth trembled and quaked, the foundations of the heavens shook; they trembled because he was angry.
					\item[NRSV] Then the earth reeled and rocked; the foundations of the heavens trembled and quaked, because he was angry.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:9} % *2SAMUEL-22-9
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-9}

			There went up a smoke out of his nostrils, and fire out of his mouth devoured: coals were kindled by it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עָלָ֤ה עָשָׁן֙ בְּאַפּ֔וֹ וְאֵ֥שׁ מִפִּ֖יו תֹּאכֵ֑ל גֶּחָלִ֖ים בָּעֲר֥וּ מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἀνέβη καπνὸς ἐν τῇ ὀργῇ αὐτοῦ, καὶ πῦρ ἐκ στόματος αὐτοῦ κατέδεται· ἄνθρακες ἐξεκαύθησαν ἀπ᾽ αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There went up a smoke out of his nostrils, And fire out of his mouth devoured: Coals were kindled by it.
					\item[NIV] Smoke rose from his nostrils; consuming fire came from his mouth, burning coals blazed out of it.
					\item[NRSV] Smoke went up from his nostrils, and devouring fire from his mouth; glowing coals flamed forth from him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:10} % *2SAMUEL-22-10
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-10}

			He bowed the heavens also, and came down; and darkness was under his feet.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֵּ֥ט שָׁמַ֖יִם וַיֵּרַ֑ד וַעֲרָפֶ֖ל תַּ֥חַת רַגְלָֽיו׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἔκλινεν οὐρανοὺς καὶ κατέβη, καὶ γνόφος ὑποκάτω τῶν ποδῶν αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He bowed the heavens also, and came down; And thick darkness was under his feet.
					\item[NIV] He parted the heavens and came down; dark clouds were under his feet.
					\item[NRSV] He bowed the heavens, and came down; thick darkness was under his feet.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:11} % *2SAMUEL-22-11
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-11}

			And he rode upon a cherub, and did fly: and he was seen upon the wings of the wind.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּרְכַּ֥ב עַל־כְּר֖וּב וַיָּעֹ֑ף וַיֵּרָ֖א עַל־כַּנְפֵי־רֽוּחַ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐπεκάθισεν ἐπὶ χερουβεὶν καὶ ἐπετάσθη, καὶ ὤφθη ἐπὶ πτερύγων ἀνέμου·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he rode upon a cherub, and did fly; Yea, he was seen upon the wings of the wind.
					\item[NIV] He mounted the cherubim and flew; he soared on the wings of the wind.
					\item[NRSV] He rode on a cherub, and flew; he was seen upon the wings of the wind.

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:12} % *2SAMUEL-22-12
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-12}

			And he made darkness pavilions round about him, dark waters, and thick clouds of the skies.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּ֥שֶׁת חֹ֛שֶׁךְ סְבִיבֹתָ֖יו סֻכּ֑וֹת חַֽשְׁרַת־מַ֖יִם עָבֵ֥י שְׁחָקִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἔθετο σκότος ἀποκρυφῆς αὐτοῦ κύκλῳ αὐτοῦ, ἡ σκηνὴ αὐτοῦ σκότος ὑδάτων· ἐπάχυνεν ἐν νεφέλαις ἀέρος.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he made darkness pavilions round about him, Gathering of waters, thick clouds of the skies.
					\item[NIV] He made darkness his canopy around him— the dark rain clouds of the sky.
					\item[NRSV] He made darkness around him a canopy, thick clouds, a gathering of water.

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:13} % *2SAMUEL-22-13
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-13}

			Through the brightness before him were coals of fire kindled.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִנֹּ֖גַהּ נֶגְדּ֑וֹ בָּעֲר֖וּ גַּחֲלֵי־אֵֽשׁ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἀπὸ τοῦ φέγγους ἐναντίον αὐτοῦ ἐξεκαύθησαν ἄνθρακες πυρός.}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] Out of the brightness of his presence bolts of lightning blazed forth.
					\item[NRSV] Out of the brightness before him coals of fire flamed forth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:14} % *2SAMUEL-22-14
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-14}

			The LORD thundered from heaven, and the most High uttered his voice.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יַרְעֵ֥ם מִן־שָׁמַ֖יִם יְהוָ֑ה וְעֶלְי֖וֹן יִתֵּ֥ן קוֹלֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐβρόντησεν ἐξ οὐρανοῦ Κύριος, καὶ ὁ ὕψιστος ἔδωκεν φωνὴν αὐτοῦ·}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Jehovah thundered from heaven, And the Most High uttered his voice.
					\item[NIV] The LORD thundered from heaven; the voice of the Most High resounded.
					\item[NRSV] The LORD thundered from heaven; the Most High uttered his voice.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
