		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:15} % *2SAMUEL-22-15
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-15}

			And he sent out arrows, and scattered them; lightning, and discomfited them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּשְׁלַ֥ח חִצִּ֖ים וַיְפִיצֵ֑ם בָּרָ֖ק וַיָּהֹֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀπέστειλεν βέλη καὶ ἐσκόρπισεν αὐτούς, ἀστραπὴν καὶ ἐξέστησεν αὐτούς·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he sent out arrows, and scattered them; Lightning, and discomfited them.
					\item[NIV] He shot his arrows and scattered the enemy, with great bolts of lightning he routed them.
					\item[NRSV] He sent out arrows, and scattered them —lightning, and routed them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:16} % *2SAMUEL-22-16
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-16}

			And the channels of the sea appeared, the foundations of the world were discovered, at the rebuking of the LORD, at the blast of the breath of his nostrils.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֵּֽרָאוּ֙ אֲפִ֣קֵי יָ֔ם יִגָּל֖וּ מֹסְד֣וֹת תֵּבֵ֑ל בְּגַעֲרַ֣ת יְהוָ֔ה מִנִּשְׁמַ֖ת ר֥וּחַ אַפּֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ὤφθησαν ἀφέσεις θαλάσσης καὶ ἀπεκαλύφθη θεμέλια τῆς οἰκουμένης, ἐν τῇ ἐπιτιμήσει Κυρίου, ἀπὸ πνοῆς πνεύματος θυμοῦ αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then the channels of the sea appeared, The foundations of the world were laid bare, By the rebuke of Jehovah, At the blast of the breath of his nostrils.
					\item[NIV] The valleys of the sea were exposed and the foundations of the earth laid bare at the rebuke of the LORD, at the blast of breath from his nostrils.
					\item[NRSV] Then the channels of the sea were seen, the foundations of the world were laid bare at the rebuke of the LORD, at the blast of the breath of his nostrils.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:17} % *2SAMUEL-22-17
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-17}

			He sent from above, he took me; he drew me out of many waters;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִשְׁלַ֥ח מִמָּר֖וֹם יִקָּחֵ֑נִי יַֽמְשֵׁ֖נִי מִמַּ֥יִם רַבִּֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἀπέστειλεν ἐξ ὕψους καὶ ἔλαβέν με, εἵλκυσέν με ἐξ ὑδάτων πολλῶν·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He sent from on high, he took me; He drew me out of many waters;
					\item[NIV] “He reached down from on high and took hold of me; he drew me out of deep waters.
					\item[NRSV] He reached from on high, he took me, he drew me out of mighty waters.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:18} % *2SAMUEL-22-18
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-18}

			He delivered me from my strong enemy, and from them that hated me: for they were too strong for me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יַצִּילֵ֕נִי מֵאֹיְבִ֖י עָ֑ז מִשֹּׂ֣נְאַ֔י כִּ֥י אָמְצ֖וּ מִמֶּֽנִּי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐρύσατό με ἐξ ἐχθρῶν μου ἰσχύος, ἐκ τῶν μισούντων με, ὅτι ἐκραταιώθησαν ὑπὲρ ἐμέ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He delivered me from my strong enemy, From them that hated me; for they were too mighty for me.
					\item[NIV] He rescued me from my powerful enemy, from my foes, who were too strong for me.
					\item[NRSV] He delivered me from my strong enemy, from those who hated me; for they were too mighty for me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:19} % *2SAMUEL-22-19
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-19}

			They prevented me in the day of my calamity: but the LORD was my stay.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יְקַדְּמֻ֖נִי בְּי֣וֹם אֵידִ֑י וַיְהִ֧י יְהוָ֛ה מִשְׁעָ֖ן לִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{προέφθασάν με ἡμέραι θλίψεώς μου, καὶ ἐγένετο Κύριος ἐπιστήριγμά μου·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They came upon me in the day of my calamity; But Jehovah was my stay.
					\item[NIV] They confronted me in the day of my disaster, but the LORD was my support.
					\item[NRSV] They came upon me in the day of my calamity, but the LORD was my stay.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:20} % *2SAMUEL-22-20
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-20}

			He brought me forth also into a large place: he delivered me, because he delighted in me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּצֵ֥א לַמֶּרְחָ֖ב אֹתִ֑י יְחַלְּצֵ֖נִי כִּי־חָ֥פֵֽץ בִּֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐξήγαγέν με εἰς πλατυσμόν, καὶ ἐξείλατό με, ὅτι εὐδόκησεν ἐν ἐμοί·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He brought me forth also into a large place; He delivered me, because he delighted in me.
					\item[NIV] He brought me out into a spacious place; he rescued me because he delighted in me.
					\item[NRSV] He brought me out into a broad place; he delivered me, because he delighted in me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:21} % *2SAMUEL-22-21
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-21}

			The LORD rewarded me according to my righteousness: according to the cleanness of my hands hath he recompensed me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִגְמְלֵ֥נִי יְהוָ֖ה כְּצִדְקָתִ֑י כְּבֹ֥ר יָדַ֖י יָשִׁ֥יב לִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀνταπέδωκέν μοι Κύριος κατὰ τὴν δικαιοσύνην μου, κατὰ τὴν καθαριότητα τῶν χειρῶν μου ἀνταπέδωκέν μοι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Jehovah rewarded me according to my righteousness; According to the cleanness of my hands hath he recompensed me.
					\item[NIV] “The LORD has dealt with me according to my righteousness; according to the cleanness of my hands he has rewarded me.
					\item[NRSV] The LORD rewarded me according to my righteousness; according to the cleanness of my hands he recompensed me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:22} % *2SAMUEL-22-22
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-22}

			For I have kept the ways of the LORD, and have not wickedly departed from my God.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֥י שָׁמַ֖רְתִּי דַּרְכֵ֣י יְהוָ֑ה וְלֹ֥א רָשַׁ֖עְתִּי מֵאֱלֹהָֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι ἐφύλαξα ὁδοὺς Κυρίου, καὶ οὐκ ἠσέβησα ἀπὸ τοῦ θεοῦ μου·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For I have kept the ways of Jehovah, And have not wickedly departed from my God.
					\item[NIV] For I have kept the ways of the LORD; I am not guilty of turning from my God.
					\item[NRSV] For I have kept the ways of the LORD, and have not wickedly departed from my God.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:23} % *2SAMUEL-22-23
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-23}

			For all his judgments were before me: and as for his statutes, I did not depart from them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֥י מִשְׁפָּטָ֖יו לְנֶגְדִּ֑י וְחֻקֹּתָ֖יו לֹא־אָס֥וּר מִמֶּֽנָּה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι πάντα τὰ κρίματα αὐτοῦ κατεναντίον μου, καὶ τὰ δικαιώματα αὐτοῦ, οὐκ ἀπέστην ἀπ᾽ αὐτῶν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For all his ordinances were before me; And as for his statutes, I did not depart from them.
					\item[NIV] All his laws are before me; I have not turned away from his decrees.
					\item[NRSV] For all his ordinances were before me, and from his statutes I did not turn aside.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:24} % *2SAMUEL-22-24
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-24}

			I was also upright before him, and have kept myself from mine iniquity.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וָאֶהְיֶ֥ה תָמִ֖ים ל֑וֹ וָאֶשְׁתַּמְּרָ֖ה מֵעֲוֺנִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἔσομαι ἄμωμος αὐτῷ καὶ προφυλάξομαι ἀπὸ τῆς ἀνομίας μου·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I was also perfect toward him; And I kept myself from mine iniquity.
					\item[NIV] I have been blameless before him and have kept myself from sin.
					\item[NRSV] I was blameless before him, and I kept myself from guilt.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:25} % *2SAMUEL-22-25
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-25}

			Therefore the LORD hath recompensed me according to my righteousness; according to my cleanness in his eye sight.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיָּ֧שֶׁב יְהוָ֛ה לִ֖י כְּצִדְקָתִ֑י כְּבֹרִ֖י לְנֶ֥גֶד עֵינָֽיו׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀποδώσει μοι Κύριος κατὰ τὴν δικαιοσύνην μου καὶ κατὰ τὴν καθαριότητα τῶν χειρῶν μου ἐνώπιον τῶν ὀφθαλμῶν αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Therefore hath Jehovah recompensed me according to my righteousness, According to my cleanness in his eyesight.
					\item[NIV] The LORD has rewarded me according to my righteousness, according to my cleanness in his sight.
					\item[NRSV] Therefore the LORD has recompensed me according to my righteousness, according to my cleanness in his sight.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:26} % *2SAMUEL-22-26
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-26}

			With the merciful thou wilt shew thyself merciful, and with the upright man thou wilt shew thyself upright.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עִם־חָסִ֖יד תִּתְחַסָּ֑ד עִם־גִּבּ֥וֹר תָּמִ֖ים תִּתַּמָּֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{μετὰ ὁσίου ὁσιωθήσῃ, καὶ μετὰ ἀνδρὸς τελείου τελειωθήσῃ·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] With the merciful thou wilt show thyself merciful; With the perfect man thou wilt show thyself perfect;
					\item[NIV] “To the faithful you show yourself faithful, to the blameless you show yourself blameless,
					\item[NRSV] With the loyal you show yourself loyal; with the blameless you show yourself blameless;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:27} % *2SAMUEL-22-27
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-27}

			With the pure thou wilt shew thyself pure; and with the froward thou wilt shew thyself unsavoury.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עִם־נָבָ֖ר תִּתָּבָ֑ר וְעִם־עִקֵּ֖שׁ תִּתַּפָּֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ μετὰ ἐκλεκτοῦ ἐκλεκτὸς ἔσῃ, καὶ μετὰ στρεβλοῦ στρεβλωθήσῃ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] With the pure thou wilt show thyself pure; And with the perverse thou wilt show thyself froward.
					\item[NIV] to the pure you show yourself pure, but to the devious you show yourself shrewd.
					\item[NRSV] with the pure you show yourself pure, and with the crooked you show yourself perverse.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:28} % *2SAMUEL-22-28
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-28}

			And the afflicted people thou wilt save: but thine eyes are upon the haughty, that thou mayest bring them down.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאֶת־עַ֥ם עָנִ֖י תּוֹשִׁ֑יעַ וְעֵינֶ֖יךָ עַל־רָמִ֥ים תַּשְׁפִּֽיל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ τὸν λαὸν τὸν πτωχὸν σώσεις, καὶ ὀφθαλμοὺς ἐπὶ μετεώρων ταπεινώσεις.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the afflicted people thou wilt save; But thine eyes are upon the haughty, that thou mayest bring them down.
					\item[NIV] You save the humble, but your eyes are on the haughty to bring them low.
					\item[NRSV] You deliver a humble people, but your eyes are upon the haughty to bring them down.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:29} % *2SAMUEL-22-29
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-29}

			For thou art my lamp, O LORD: and the LORD will lighten my darkness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־אַתָּ֥ה נֵירִ֖י יְהוָ֑ה וַיהוָ֖ה יַגִּ֥יהַּ חָשְׁכִּֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι σὺ ὁ λύχνος μου, Κύριε, καὶ Κύριος ἐκλάμψει μοι τὸ σκότος μου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For thou art my lamp, O Jehovah; And Jehovah will lighten my darkness.
					\item[NIV] You, LORD, are my lamp; the LORD turns my darkness into light.
					\item[NRSV] Indeed, you are my lamp, O LORD, the LORD lightens my darkness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:30} % *2SAMUEL-22-30
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-30}

			For by thee I have run through a troop: by my God have I leaped over a wall.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֥י בְכָ֖ה אָר֣וּץ גְּד֑וּד בֵּאלֹהַ֖י אֲדַלֶּג־שֽׁוּר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι ἐν σοὶ δραμοῦμαι μονόζωνος, καὶ ἐν τῷ θεῷ μου ὑπερβήσομαι τεῖχος.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For by thee I run upon a troop; By my God do I leap over a wall.
					\item[NIV] With your help I can advance against a troop; with my God I can scale a wall.
					\item[NRSV] By you I can crush a troop, and by my God I can leap over a wall.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:31} % *2SAMUEL-22-31
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-31}

			As for God, his way is perfect; the word of the LORD is tried: he is a buckler to all them that trust in him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָאֵ֖ל תָּמִ֣ים דַּרְכּ֑וֹ אִמְרַ֤ת יְהוָה֙ צְרוּפָ֔ה מָגֵ֣ן ה֔וּא לְכֹ֖ל הַחֹסִ֥ים בּֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὁ ἰσχυρός, ἄμωμος ἡ ὁδὸς αὐτοῦ, τὸ ῥῆμα Κυρίου κραταιόν, πεπυρωμένον· ὑπερασπιστής ἐστιν πᾶσιν τοῖς πεποιθόσιν ἐπ᾽ αὐτῷ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As for God, his way is perfect: The word of Jehovah is tried; He is a shield unto all them that take refuge in him.
					\item[NIV] “As for God, his way is perfect: The LORD’s word is flawless; he shields all who take refuge in him.
					\item[NRSV] This God—his way is perfect; the promise of the LORD proves true; he is a shield for all who take refuge in him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:32} % *2SAMUEL-22-32
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-32}

			For who is God, save the LORD? and who is a rock, save our God?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֥י מִי־אֵ֖ל מִבַּלְעֲדֵ֣י יְהוָ֑ה וּמִ֥י צ֖וּר מִֽבַּלְעֲדֵ֥י אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{τίς ἰσχυρὸς πλὴν Κυρίου; καὶ τίς κτίστης ἔσται πλὴν τοῦ θεοῦ ἡμῶν;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For who is God, save Jehovah? And who is a rock, save our God?
					\item[NIV] For who is God besides the LORD? And who is the Rock except our God?
					\item[NRSV] For who is God, but the LORD? And who is a rock, except our God?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:33} % *2SAMUEL-22-33
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-33}

			God is my strength and power: and he maketh my way perfect.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָאֵ֥ל מָעוּזִּ֖י חָ֑יִל וַיַּתֵּ֥ר תָּמִ֖ים דַּרְכִּֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὁ ἰσχυρὸς ὁ κραταιῶν με δυνάμει, καὶ ἐξετίναξεν ἄμωμον τὴν ὁδόν μου·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] God is my strong fortress; And he guideth the perfect in his way.
					\item[NIV] It is God who arms me with strength and keeps my way secure.
					\item[NRSV] The God who has girded me with strength has opened wide my path.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:34} % *2SAMUEL-22-34
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-34}

			He maketh my feet like hinds’ feet: and setteth me upon my high places.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְשַׁוֶּ֥ה רַגְלַ֖י כָּאַיָּל֑וֹת וְעַ֥ל בָּמוֹתַ֖י יַעֲמִדֵֽנִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{τιθεὶς τοὺς πόδας μου ὡς ἐλάφων, καὶ ἐπὶ τὰ ὕψη ἱστῶν με·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He maketh his feet like hinds' feet, And setteth me upon my high places.
					\item[NIV] He makes my feet like the feet of a deer; he causes me to stand on the heights.
					\item[NRSV] He made my feet like the feet of deer, and set me secure on the heights.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:35} % *2SAMUEL-22-35
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-35}

			He teacheth my hands to war; so that a bow of steel is broken by mine arms.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְלַמֵּ֥ד יָדַ֖י לַמִּלְחָמָ֑ה וְנִחַ֥ת קֶֽשֶׁת־נְחוּשָׁ֖ה זְרֹעֹתָֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{διδάσκων χεῖράς μου εἰς πόλεμον, καὶ κατάξας τόξον χαλκοῦν ἐν βραχίονί μου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He teacheth my hands to war, So that mine arms do bend a bow of brass.
					\item[NIV] He trains my hands for battle; my arms can bend a bow of bronze.
					\item[NRSV] He trains my hands for war, so that my arms can bend a bow of bronze.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:36} % *2SAMUEL-22-36
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-36}

			Thou hast also given me the shield of thy salvation: and thy gentleness hath made me great.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתִּתֶּן־לִ֖י מָגֵ֣ן יִשְׁעֶ֑ךָ וַעֲנֹתְךָ֖ תַּרְבֵּֽנִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἔδωκάς μοι ὑπερασπισμὸν σωτηρίας μου, καὶ ἡ ὑπακοή σου ἐπλήθυνέν με.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Thou hast also given me the shield of thy salvation; And thy gentleness hath made me great.
					\item[NIV] You make your saving help my shield; your help has made me great.
					\item[NRSV] You have given me the shield of your salvation, and your help has made me great.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:37} % *2SAMUEL-22-37
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-37}

			Thou hast enlarged my steps under me; so that my feet did not slip.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תַּרְחִ֥יב צַעֲדִ֖י תַּחְתֵּ֑נִי וְלֹ֥א מָעֲד֖וּ קַרְסֻלָּֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{εἰς πλατυσμὸν εἰς τὰ διαβήματά μου ὑποκάτω μου, καὶ οὐκ ἐσαλεύθησαν τὰ σκέλη μου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Thou hast enlarged my steps under me; And my feet have not slipped.
					\item[NIV] You provide a broad path for my feet, so that my ankles do not give way.
					\item[NRSV] You have made me stride freely, and my feet do not slip;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:38} % *2SAMUEL-22-38
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-38}

			I have pursued mine enemies, and destroyed them; and turned not again until I had consumed them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֶרְדְּפָ֥ה אֹיְבַ֖י וָאַשְׁמִידֵ֑ם וְלֹ֥א אָשׁ֖וּב עַד־כַּלּוֹתָֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{διώξω ἐχθρούς μου καὶ ἀφανιῶ αὐτούς, καὶ οὐκ ἀναστρέψω ἕως συντελέσω αὐτούς·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I have pursued mine enemies, and destroyed them; Neither did I turn again till they were consumed.
					\item[NIV] “I pursued my enemies and crushed them; I did not turn back till they were destroyed.
					\item[NRSV] I pursued my enemies and destroyed them, and did not turn back until they were consumed.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:39} % *2SAMUEL-22-39
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-39}

			And I have consumed them, and wounded them, that they could not arise: yea, they are fallen under my feet.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וָאֲכַלֵּ֥ם וָאֶמְחָצֵ֖ם וְלֹ֣א יְקוּמ֑וּן וַֽיִּפְּל֖וּ תַּ֥חַת רַגְלָֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ θλάσω αὐτοὺς καὶ οὐκ ἀναστήσονται, καὶ πεσοῦνται ὑπὸ τοὺς πόδας μου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And I have consumed them, and smitten them through, so that they cannot arise: Yea, they are fallen under my feet.
					\item[NIV] I crushed them completely, and they could not rise; they fell beneath my feet.
					\item[NRSV] I consumed them; I struck them down, so that they did not rise; they fell under my feet.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:40} % *2SAMUEL-22-40
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-40}

			For thou hast girded me with strength to battle: them that rose up against me hast thou subdued under me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתַּזְרֵ֥נִי חַ֖יִל לַמִּלְחָמָ֑ה תַּכְרִ֥יעַ קָמַ֖י תַּחְתֵּֽנִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐνισχύσεις με δυνάμει εἰς πόλεμον· κάμψεις τοὺς ἐπανιστανομένους μοι ὑποκάτω μου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For thou hast girded me with strength unto the battle; Thou hast subdued under me those that rose up against me.
					\item[NIV] You armed me with strength for battle; you humbled my adversaries before me.
					\item[NRSV] For you girded me with strength for the battle; you made my assailants sink under me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:41} % *2SAMUEL-22-41
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-41}

			Thou hast also given me the necks of mine enemies, that I might destroy them that hate me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאֹ֣יְבַ֔י תַּ֥תָּה לִּ֖י עֹ֑רֶף מְשַׂנְאַ֖י וָאַצְמִיתֵֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ τοὺς ἐχθρούς μου ἔδωκάς μοι νῶτον, τοὺς μισοῦντάς με, καὶ ἐθανάτωσας αὐτούς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Thou hast also made mine enemies turn their backs unto me, That I might cut off them that hate me.
					\item[NIV] You made my enemies turn their backs in flight, and I destroyed my foes.
					\item[NRSV] You made my enemies turn their backs to me, those who hated me, and I destroyed them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:42} % *2SAMUEL-22-42
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-42}

			They looked, but there was none to save; even unto the LORD, but he answered them not.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִשְׁע֖וּ וְאֵ֣ין מֹשִׁ֑יעַ אֶל־יְהוָ֖ה וְלֹ֥א עָנָֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{βοήσονται καὶ οὐκ ἔστιν βοηθός· πρὸς Κύριον, καὶ οὐχ ὑπήκουσεν αὐτῶν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They looked, but there was none to save; Even unto Jehovah, but he answered them not.
					\item[NIV] They cried for help, but there was no one to save them— to the LORD, but he did not answer.
					\item[NRSV] They looked, but there was no one to save them; they cried to the LORD, but he did not answer them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:43} % *2SAMUEL-22-43
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-43}

			Then did I beat them as small as the dust of the earth, I did stamp them as the mire of the street, and did spread them abroad.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאֶשְׁחָקֵ֖ם כַּעֲפַר־אָ֑רֶץ כְּטִיט־חוּצ֥וֹת אֲדִקֵּ֖ם אֶרְקָעֵֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐλέανα αὐτοὺς ὡς χνοῦν γῆς· ὡς πηλὸν ἐξόδων ἐλέπτυνα αὐτούς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then did I beat them small as the dust of the earth, I did crush them as the mire of the streets, and did spread them abroad.
					\item[NIV] I beat them as fine as the dust of the earth; I pounded and trampled them like mud in the streets.
					\item[NRSV] I beat them fine like the dust of the earth, I crushed them and stamped them down like the mire of the streets.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:44} % *2SAMUEL-22-44
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-44}

			Thou also hast delivered me from the strivings of my people, thou hast kept me to be head of the heathen: a people which I knew not shall serve me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַֽתְּפַלְּטֵ֔נִי מֵרִיבֵ֖י עַמִּ֑י תִּשְׁמְרֵ֙נִי֙ לְרֹ֣אשׁ גּוֹיִ֔ם עַ֥ם לֹא־יָדַ֖עְתִּי יַעַבְדֻֽנִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ῥύσῃ με ἐκ μάχης λαῶν, φυλάξεις με εἰς κεφαλὴν ἐθνῶν. λαὸς ὃν οὐκ ἔγνων ἐδούλευσάν μοι·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Thou also hast delivered me from the strivings of my people; Thou hast kept me to be the head of the nations: A people whom I have not known shall serve me.
					\item[NIV] “You have delivered me from the attacks of the peoples; you have preserved me as the head of nations. People I did not know now serve me,
					\item[NRSV] You delivered me from strife with the peoples; you kept me as the head of the nations; people whom I had not known served me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:45} % *2SAMUEL-22-45
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-45}

			Strangers shall submit themselves unto me: as soon as they hear, they shall be obedient unto me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּנֵ֥י נֵכָ֖ר יִתְכַּֽחֲשׁוּ־לִ֑י לִשְׁמ֥וֹעַ אֹ֖זֶן יִשָּׁ֥מְעוּ לִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{υἱοὶ ἀλλότριοι ἐψεύσαντό μοι, εἰς ἀκοὴν ὠτίου ἤκουσάν μου·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The foreigners shall submit themselves unto me: As soon as they hear of me, they shall obey me.
					\item[NIV] foreigners cower before me; as soon as they hear of me, they obey me.
					\item[NRSV] Foreigners came cringing to me; as soon as they heard of me, they obeyed me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:46} % *2SAMUEL-22-46
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-46}

			Strangers shall fade away, and they shall be afraid out of their close places.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּנֵ֥י נֵכָ֖ר יִבֹּ֑לוּ וְיַחְגְּר֖וּ מִמִּסְגְּרוֹתָֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{υἱοὶ ἀλλότριοι ἀπορριφήσονται καὶ σφαλοῦσιν ἐκ τῶν συγκλεισμῶν αὐτῶν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The foreigners shall fade away, And shall come trembling out of their close places.
					\item[NIV] They all lose heart; they come trembling from their strongholds.
					\item[NRSV] Foreigners lost heart, and came trembling out of their strongholds.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:47} % *2SAMUEL-22-47
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-47}

			The LORD liveth; and blessed be my rock; and exalted be the God of the rock of my salvation.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חַי־יְהוָ֖ה וּבָר֣וּךְ צוּרִ֑י וְיָרֻ֕ם אֱלֹהֵ֖י צ֥וּר יִשְׁעִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ζῇ Κύριος καὶ εὐλογητὸς ὁ φύλαξ μου, καὶ ὑψωθήσεται ὁ θεός μου, ὁ φύλαξ τῆς σωτηρίας μου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Jehovah liveth; And blessed be my rock; And exalted be God, the rock of my salvation,
					\item[NIV] “The LORD lives! Praise be to my Rock! Exalted be my God, the Rock, my Savior!
					\item[NRSV] The LORD lives! Blessed be my rock, and exalted be my God, the rock of my salvation,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:48} % *2SAMUEL-22-48
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-48}

			It is God that avengeth me, and that bringeth down the people under me,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָאֵ֕ל הַנֹּתֵ֥ן נְקָמֹ֖ת לִ֑י וּמוֹרִ֥יד עַמִּ֖ים תַּחְתֵּֽנִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἰσχυρὸς Κύριος ὁ διδοὺς ἐκδικήσεις ἐμοί, παιδεύων λαοὺς ὑποκάτω μου,}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Even the God that executeth vengeance for me, And that bringeth down peoples under me,
					\item[NIV] He is the God who avenges me, who puts the nations under me,
					\item[NRSV] the God who gave me vengeance and brought down peoples under me,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:49} % *2SAMUEL-22-49
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-49}

			And that bringeth me forth from mine enemies: thou also hast lifted me up on high above them that rose up against me: thou hast delivered me from the violent man.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּמוֹצִיאִ֖י מֵאֹֽיְבָ֑י וּמִקָּמַי֙ תְּר֣וֹמְמֵ֔נִי מֵאִ֥ישׁ חֲמָסִ֖ים תַּצִּילֵֽנִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐξάγων με ἐξ ἐχθρῶν μου· καὶ ἐκ τῶν ἐπεγειρομένων μοι ὑψώσεις με, ἐξ ἀνδρὸς ἀδικημάτων ῥύσῃ με.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And that bringeth me forth from mine enemies: Yea, thou liftest me up above them that rise up against me; Thou deliverest me from the violent man.
					\item[NIV] who sets me free from my enemies. You exalted me above my foes; from a violent man you rescued me.
					\item[NRSV] who brought me out from my enemies; you exalted me above my adversaries, you delivered me from the violent.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:50} % *2SAMUEL-22-50
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-50}

			Therefore I will give thanks unto thee, O LORD, among the heathen, and I will sing praises unto thy name.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַל־כֵּ֛ן אוֹדְךָ֥ יְהוָ֖ה בַּגּוֹיִ֑ם וּלְשִׁמְךָ֖ אֲזַמֵּֽר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{διὰ τοῦτο ἐξομολογήσομαί σοι, Κύριε, ἐν τοῖς ἔθνεσιν, καὶ ἐν τῷ ὀνόματί σου ψαλῶ·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Therefore I will give thanks unto thee, O Jehovah, among the nations, And will sing praises unto thy name.
					\item[NIV] Therefore I will praise you, LORD, among the nations; I will sing the praises of your name.
					\item[NRSV] For this I will extol you, O LORD, among the nations, and sing praises to your name.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 22:51} % *2SAMUEL-22-51
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-22-51}

			He is the tower of salvation for his king: and sheweth mercy to his anointed, unto David, and to his seed for evermore.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִגְדּ֖וֹל יְשׁוּע֣וֹת מַלְכּ֑וֹ וְעֹֽשֶׂה־חֶ֧סֶד לִמְשִׁיח֛וֹ לְדָוִ֥ד וּלְזַרְע֖וֹ עַד־עוֹלָֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{μεγαλύνων σωτηρίας βασιλέως αὐτοῦ, καὶ ποιῶν ἔλεος τῷ χριστῷ αὐτοῦ, τῷ Δαυεὶδ καὶ τῷ σπέρματι αὐτοῦ ἕως αἰῶνος.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Great deliverance giveth he to his king, And showeth lovingkindness to his anointed, To David and to his seed, for evermore.
					\item[NIV] “He gives his king great victories; he shows unfailing kindness to his anointed, to David and his descendants forever.”
					\item[NRSV] He is a tower of salvation for his king, and shows steadfast love to his anointed, to David and his descendants forever.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{2 Samuel 23} % *2SAMUEL-23
	% -----
		\label{2SAMUEL-23}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:1} % *2SAMUEL-23-1
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-1}

			Now these be the last words of David. David the son of Jesse said, and the man who was raised up on high, the anointed of the God of Jacob, and the sweet psalmist of Israel, said,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאֵ֛לֶּה דִּבְרֵ֥י דָוִ֖ד הָאַֽחֲרֹנִ֑ים נְאֻ֧ם דָּוִ֣ד בֶּן־יִשַׁ֗י וּנְאֻ֤ם הַגֶּ֙בֶר֙ הֻ֣קַם עָ֔ל מְשִׁ֙יחַ֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יַֽעֲקֹ֔ב וּנְעִ֖ים זְמִר֥וֹת יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ οὗτοι οἱ λόγοι Δαυεὶδ οἱ ἔσχατοι Πιστὸς Δαυεὶδ υἱὸς Ἰεσσαί, καὶ πιστὸς ἀνὴρ ὃν ἀνέστησεν Κύριος ἐπὶ χριστὸν θεοῦ Ἰακώβ, καὶ εὐπρεπεῖς ψαλμοὶ Ἰσραήλ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now these are the last words of David. David the son of Jesse saith, And the man who was raised on high saith, The anointed of the God of Jacob, And the sweet psalmist of Israel:
					\item[NIV] These are the last words of David: “The inspired utterance of David son of Jesse, the utterance of the man exalted by the Most High, the man anointed by the God of Jacob, the hero of Israel’s songs:
					\item[NRSV] Now these are the last words of David: The oracle of David, son of Jesse, the oracle of the man whom God exalted, the anointed of the God of Jacob, the favorite of the Strong One of Israel:
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:2} % *2SAMUEL-23-2
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-2}

			The Spirit of the LORD spake by me, and his word was in my tongue.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ר֥וּחַ יְהוָ֖ה דִּבֶּר־בִּ֑י וּמִלָּת֖וֹ עַל־לְשׁוֹנִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{πνεῦμα Κυρίου ἐλάλησεν ἐν ἐμοί, καὶ ὁ λόγος αὐτοῦ ἐπὶ γλώσσης μου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The Spirit of Jehovah spake by me, And his word was upon my tongue.
					\item[NIV] “The Spirit of the LORD spoke through me; his word was on my tongue.
					\item[NRSV] The spirit of the LORD speaks through me, his word is upon my tongue.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:3} % *2SAMUEL-23-3
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-3}

			The God of Israel said, the Rock of Israel spake to me, He that ruleth over men must be just, ruling in the fear of God.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָמַר֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל לִ֥י דִבֶּ֖ר צ֣וּר יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל מוֹשֵׁל֙ בָּאָדָ֔ם צַדִּ֕יק מוֹשֵׁ֖ל יִרְאַ֥ת אֱלֹהִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{λέγει ὁ θεὸς Ἰσραήλ· ἐμοὶ ἐλάλησεν φύλαξ ἐξ Ἰσραήλ Παραβολὴν εἰπόν Ἐν ἀνθρώπῳ πῶς κραταιώσητε φόβον χριστοῦ;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The God of Israel said, The Rock of Israel spake to me: One that ruleth over men righteously, That ruleth in the fear of God,
					\item[NIV] The God of Israel spoke, the Rock of Israel said to me: ‘When one rules over people in righteousness, when he rules in the fear of God,
					\item[NRSV] The God of Israel has spoken, the Rock of Israel has said to me: One who rules over people justly, ruling in the fear of God,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:4} % *2SAMUEL-23-4
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-4}

			And he shall be as the light of the morning, when the sun riseth, even a morning without clouds; as the tender grass springing out of the earth by clear shining after rain.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּכְא֥וֹר בֹּ֖קֶר יִזְרַח־שָׁ֑מֶשׁ בֹּ֚קֶר לֹ֣א עָב֔וֹת מִנֹּ֥גַהּ מִמָּטָ֖ר דֶּ֥שֶׁא מֵאָֽרֶץ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐν θεῷ φωτὶ πρωίας ἀνατείλαι ἥλιος, τὸ πρωὶ οὐ Κύριος παρῆλθεν ἐκ φέγγους· καὶ ὡς ἐξ ὑετοῦ χλόης ἀπὸ γῆς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He shall be as the light of the morning, when the sun riseth, A morning without clouds, When the tender grass springeth out of the earth, Through clear shining after rain.
					\item[NIV] he is like the light of morning at sunrise on a cloudless morning, like the brightness after rain that brings grass from the earth.’
					\item[NRSV] is like the light of morning, like the sun rising on a cloudless morning, gleaming from the rain on the grassy land.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:5} % *2SAMUEL-23-5
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-5}

			Although my house be not so with God; yet he hath made with me an everlasting covenant, ordered in all things, and sure: for this is all my salvation, and all my desire, although he make it not to grow.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־לֹא־כֵ֥ן בֵּיתִ֖י עִם־אֵ֑ל כִּי֩ בְרִ֨ית עוֹלָ֜ם שָׂ֣ם לִ֗י עֲרוּכָ֤ה בַכֹּל֙ וּשְׁמֻרָ֔ה כִּֽי־כָל־יִשְׁעִ֥י וְכָל־חֵ֖פֶץ כִּֽי־לֹ֥א יַצְמִֽיחַ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{οὐ γὰρ οὗτος ὁ οἶκός μου μετὰ Ἰσχυροῦ; διαθήκην γὰρ αἰώνιον ἔθετό μοι, ἑτοίμην ἐν παντὶ καιρῷ, πεφυλαγμένην, ὅτι πᾶσα σωτηρία μου καὶ πᾶν θέλημα· ὅτι οὐ μὴ βλαστήσῃ ὁ παράνομος.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Verily my house is not so with God; Yet he hath made with me an everlasting covenant, Ordered in all things, and sure: For it is all my salvation, and all my desire, Although he maketh it not to grow.
					\item[NIV] “If my house were not right with God, surely he would not have made with me an everlasting covenant, arranged and secured in every part; surely he would not bring to fruition my salvation and grant me my every desire.
					\item[NRSV] Is not my house like this with God? For he has made with me an everlasting covenant, ordered in all things and secure. Will he not cause to prosper all my help and my desire?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:6} % *2SAMUEL-23-6
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-6}

			But the sons of Belial shall be all of them as thorns thrust away, because they cannot be taken with hands:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּבְלִיַּ֕עַל כְּק֥וֹץ מֻנָ֖ד כֻּלָּ֑הַם כִּֽי־לֹ֥א בְיָ֖ד יִקָּֽחוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὥσπερ ἄκανθα ἐξωσμένη πάντες οὗτοι, ὅτι οὐ χειρὶ λημφθήσονται·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But the ungodly shall be all of them as thorns to be thrust away, Because they cannot be taken with the hand;
					\item[NIV] But evil men are all to be cast aside like thorns, which are not gathered with the hand.
					\item[NRSV] But the godless are all like thorns that are thrown away; for they cannot be picked up with the hand;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:7} % *2SAMUEL-23-7
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-7}

			But the man that shall touch them must be fenced with iron and the staff of a spear; and they shall be utterly burned with fire in the same place.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאִישׁ֙ יִגַּ֣ע בָּהֶ֔ם יִמָּלֵ֥א בַרְזֶ֖ל וְעֵ֣ץ חֲנִ֑ית וּבָאֵ֕שׁ שָׂר֥וֹף יִשָּׂרְפ֖וּ בַּשָּֽׁבֶת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀνὴρ οὐ κοπιάσει ἐν αὐτοῖς· καὶ πλῆρες σιδήρου καὶ ξύλον δόρατος, καὶ ἐν πυρὶ καύσει, καὶ θήσονται αἰσχύνην αὐτῶν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But the man that toucheth them Must be armed with iron and the staff of a spear: And they shall be utterly burned with fire in their place.
					\item[NIV] Whoever touches thorns uses a tool of iron or the shaft of a spear; they are burned up where they lie.”
					\item[NRSV] to touch them one uses an iron bar or the shaft of a spear. And they are entirely consumed in fire on the spot.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:8} % *2SAMUEL-23-8
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-8}

			These be the names of the mighty men whom David had: The Tachmonite that sat in the seat, chief among the captains; the same was Adino the Eznite: he lift up his spear against eight hundred, whom he slew at one time.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֵ֛לֶּה שְׁמ֥וֹת הַגִּבֹּרִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר לְדָוִ֑ד יֹשֵׁ֨ב בַּשֶּׁ֜בֶת תַּחְכְּמֹנִ֣י ׀ רֹ֣אשׁ הַשָּׁלִשִׁ֗י ה֚וּא עֲדִינ֣וֹ הָֽעֶצְנִ֔י עַל־שְׁמֹנֶ֥ה מֵא֛וֹת חָלָ֖ל בְּפַ֥עַם אֶחָֽת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Ταῦτα τὰ ὀνόματα τῶν δυνατῶν Δαυείδ· Ἰεβόσθε ὁ Χαναναῖος ἄρχων τοῦ τρίτου ἐστίν· Ἀδεινὼν ὁ Ἀσωναῖος, οὗτος ἐσπάσατο τὴν ῥομφαίαν αὐτοῦ ἐπὶ ὀκτακοσίους στρατιώτας εἰς ἅπαξ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] These are the names of the mighty men whom David had: Josheb-basshebeth a Tahchemonite, chief of the captains; the same was Adino the Eznite, against eight hundred slain at one time.
					\item[NIV] These are the names of David’s mighty warriors: Josheb-Basshebeth, a Tahkemonite, was chief of the Three; he raised his spear against eight hundred men, whom he killed in one encounter.
					\item[NRSV] These are the names of the warriors whom David had: Josheb-basshebeth a Tahchemonite; he was chief of the Three; he wielded his spear against eight hundred whom he killed at one time.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:9} % *2SAMUEL-23-9
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-9}

			And after him was Eleazar the son of Dodo the Ahohite, one of the three mighty men with David, when they defied the Philistines that were there gathered together to battle, and the men of Israel were gone away:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאַחֲרָ֛יו אֶלְעָזָ֥ר דֹּד֖וֹ בֶּן־אֲחֹחִ֑י בִּשְׁלֹשָׁ֨ה הַגִּבֹּרִ֜ים עִם־דָּוִ֗ד בְּחָֽרְפָ֤ם בַּפְּלִשְׁתִּים נֶאֶסְפוּ־שָׁ֣ם לַמִּלְחָמָ֔ה וַֽיַּעֲל֖וּ אִ֥ישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ μετ᾽ αὐτὸν Ἐλεανὰν υἱὸς πατραδέλφου αὐτοῦ, υἱὸς Σουσεὶ τοῦ ἐν τοῖς τρισὶν δυνατοῖς μετὰ Δαυείδ· καὶ ἐν τῷ ὀνειδίσαι αὐτὸν ἐν τοῖς ἀλλοφύλοις συνήχθησαν ἐκεῖ εἰς πόλεμον, καὶ ἀνεβόησεν ἀνὴρ Ἰσραήλ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And after him was Eleazar the son of Dodai the son of an Ahohite, one of the three mighty men with David, when they defied the Philistines that were there gathered together to battle, and the men of Israel were gone away.
					\item[NIV] Next to him was Eleazar son of Dodai the Ahohite. As one of the three mighty warriors, he was with David when they taunted the Philistines gathered at Pas Dammim for battle. Then the Israelites retreated,
					\item[NRSV] Next to him among the three warriors was Eleazar son of Dodo son of Ahohi. He was with David when they defied the Philistines who were gathered there for battle. The Israelites withdrew,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:10} % *2SAMUEL-23-10
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-10}

			He arose, and smote the Philistines until his hand was weary, and his hand clave unto the sword: and the LORD wrought a great victory that day; and the people returned after him only to spoil.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ה֣וּא קָם֩ וַיַּ֨ךְ בַּפְּלִשְׁתִּ֜ים עַ֣ד ׀ כִּֽי־יָגְעָ֣ה יָד֗וֹ וַתִּדְבַּ֤ק יָדוֹ֙ אֶל־הַחֶ֔רֶב וַיַּ֧עַשׂ יְהוָ֛ה תְּשׁוּעָ֥ה גְדוֹלָ֖ה בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֑וּא וְהָעָ֛ם יָשֻׁ֥בוּ אַחֲרָ֖יו אַךְ־לְפַשֵּֽׁט׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{αὐτὸς ἀνέστη καὶ ἐπάταξεν ἐν τοῖς ἀλλοφύλοις ἕως οὗ ἐκοπίασεν ἡ χεὶρ αὐτοῦ, καὶ προσεκολλήθη ἡ χεὶρ αὐτοῦ πρὸς τὴν μάχαιραν· καὶ ἐποίησεν Κύριος σωτηρίαν μεγάλην ἐν τῇ ἡμέρᾳ ἐκείνῃ, καὶ ὁ λαὸς ἐκάθητο ὀπίσω αὐτοῦ πλὴν ἐκδιδύσκειν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He arose, and smote the Philistines until his hand was weary, and his hand clave unto the sword; and Jehovah wrought a great victory that day; and the people returned after him only to take spoil.
					\item[NIV] but Eleazar stood his ground and struck down the Philistines till his hand grew tired and froze to the sword. The LORD brought about a great victory that day. The troops returned to Eleazar, but only to strip the dead.
					\item[NRSV] but he stood his ground. He struck down the Philistines until his arm grew weary, though his hand clung to the sword. The LORD brought about a great victory that day. Then the people came back to him—but only to strip the dead.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:11} % *2SAMUEL-23-11
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-11}

			And after him was Shammah the son of Agee the Hararite. And the Philistines were gathered together into a troop, where was a piece of ground full of lentiles: and the people fled from the Philistines.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאַחֲרָ֛יו שַׁמָּ֥א בֶן־אָגֵ֖א הָרָרִ֑י וַיֵּאָסְפ֨וּ פְלִשְׁתִּ֜ים לַחַיָּ֗ה וַתְּהִי־שָׁ֞ם חֶלְקַ֤ת הַשָּׂדֶה֙ מְלֵאָ֣ה עֲדָשִׁ֔ים וְהָעָ֥ם נָ֖ס מִפְּנֵ֥י פְלִשְׁתִּֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ μετ᾽ αὐτὸν Σαμαιὰ υἱὸς Ἀσὰ ὁ Ἁρουχαῖος· καὶ συνήχθησαν οἱ ἀλλόφυλοι εἰς Θηρία, καὶ ἦν ἐκεῖ μερὶς τοῦ ἀγροῦ πλήρης φακοῦ, καὶ ὁ λαὸς ἔφυγεν ἐκ προσώπου ἀλλοφύλων.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And after him was Shammah the son of Agee a Hararite. And the Philistines were gathered together into a troop, where was a plot of ground full of lentils; and the people fled from the Philistines.
					\item[NIV] Next to him was Shammah son of Agee the Hararite. When the Philistines banded together at a place where there was a field full of lentils, Israel’s troops fled from them.
					\item[NRSV] Next to him was Shammah son of Agee, the Hararite. The Philistines gathered together at Lehi, where there was a plot of ground full of lentils; and the army fled from the Philistines.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:12} % *2SAMUEL-23-12
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-12}

			But he stood in the midst of the ground, and defended it, and slew the Philistines: and the LORD wrought a great victory.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּתְיַצֵּ֤ב בְּתוֹךְ־הַֽחֶלְקָה֙ וַיַּצִּילֶ֔הָ וַיַּ֖ךְ אֶת־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֑ים וַיַּ֥עַשׂ יְהוָ֖ה תְּשׁוּעָ֥ה גְדוֹלָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐστηλώθη ἐν μέσῳ τῆς μερίδος, καὶ ἐξείλατο αὐτὴν καὶ ἐπάταξεν τοὺς ἀλλοφύλους, καὶ ἐποίησεν Κύριος σωτηρίαν μεγάλην.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But he stood in the midst of the plot, and defended it, and slew the Philistines; and Jehovah wrought a great victory.
					\item[NIV] But Shammah took his stand in the middle of the field. He defended it and struck the Philistines down, and the LORD brought about a great victory.
					\item[NRSV] But he took his stand in the middle of the plot, defended it, and killed the Philistines; and the LORD brought about a great victory.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:13} % *2SAMUEL-23-13
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-13}

			And three of the thirty chief went down, and came to David in the harvest time unto the cave of Adullam: and the troop of the Philistines pitched in the valley of Rephaim.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֵּרְד֨וּ שְׁלֹשָׁ֜ה מֵהַשְּׁלֹשִׁ֣ים רֹ֗אשׁ וַיָּבֹ֤אוּ אֶל־קָצִיר֙ אֶל־דָּוִ֔ד אֶל־מְעָרַ֖ת עֲדֻלָּ֑ם וְחַיַּ֣ת פְּלִשְׁתִּ֔ים חֹנָ֖ה בְּעֵ֥מֶק רְפָאִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καὶ κατέβησαν τρεῖς ἀπὸ τῶν τριάκοντα καὶ κατέβησαν εἰς Καδὼν πρὸς Δαυεὶδ εἰς τὸ σπήλαιον Ὀδολλάμ· καὶ τάγμα τῶν ἀλλοφύλων, καὶ παρενέβαλον ἐν τῇ κοιλάδι Ῥαφαείμ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And three of the thirty chief men went down, and came to David in the harvest time unto the cave of Adullam; and the troop of the Philistines was encamped in the valley of Rephaim.
					\item[NIV] During harvest time, three of the thirty chief warriors came down to David at the cave of Adullam, while a band of Philistines was encamped in the Valley of Rephaim.
					\item[NRSV] Towards the beginning of harvest three of the thirty chiefs went down to join David at the cave of Adullam, while a band of Philistines was encamped in the valley of Rephaim.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:14} % *2SAMUEL-23-14
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-14}

			And David was then in an hold, and the garrison of the Philistines was then in Bethlehem.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְדָוִ֖ד אָ֣ז בַּמְּצוּדָ֑ה וּמַצַּ֣ב פְּלִשְׁתִּ֔ים אָ֖ז בֵּ֥ית לָֽחֶם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ Δαυεὶδ τότε ἐν τῇ περιοχῇ, καὶ τὸ ὑπόστημα τῶν ἀλλοφύλων τότε ἐν Βαιθλέεμ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David was then in the stronghold; and the garrison of the Philistines was then in Beth-lehem.
					\item[NIV] At that time David was in the stronghold, and the Philistine garrison was at Bethlehem.
					\item[NRSV] David was then in the stronghold; and the garrison of the Philistines was then at Bethlehem.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:15} % *2SAMUEL-23-15
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-15}

			And David longed, and said, Oh that one would give me drink of the water of the well of Bethlehem, which is by the gate!

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּתְאַוֶּ֥ה דָוִ֖ד וַיֹּאמַ֑ר מִ֚י יַשְׁקֵ֣נִי מַ֔יִם מִבֹּ֥אר בֵּֽית־לֶ֖חֶם אֲשֶׁ֥ר בַּשָּֽׁעַר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐπεθύμησεν Δαυεὶδ καὶ εἶπεν Τίς ποτιεῖ με ὕδωρ ἐκ τοῦ λάκκου τοῦ ἐν Βαιθλέεμ τοῦ ἐν τῇ πύλῃ; τὸ δὲ σύστεμα τῶν ἀλλοφύλων τότε ἐν Βαιθλέεμ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And David longed, and said, Oh that one would give me water to drink of the well of Beth-lehem, which is by the gate!
					\item[NIV] David longed for water and said, “Oh, that someone would get me a drink of water from the well near the gate of Bethlehem!”
					\item[NRSV] David said longingly, “O that someone would give me water to drink from the well of Bethlehem that is by the gate!”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:16} % *2SAMUEL-23-16
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-16}

			And the three mighty men brake through the host of the Philistines, and drew water out of the well of Bethlehem, that was by the gate, and took it, and brought it to David: nevertheless he would not drink thereof, but poured it out unto the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיִּבְקְעוּ֩ שְׁלֹ֨שֶׁת הַגִּבֹּרִ֜ים בְּמַחֲנֵ֣ה פְלִשְׁתִּ֗ים וַיִּֽשְׁאֲבוּ־מַ֙יִם֙ מִבֹּ֤אר בֵּֽית־לֶ֙חֶם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּשַּׁ֔עַר וַיִּשְׂא֖וּ וַיָּבִ֣אוּ אֶל־דָּוִ֑ד וְלֹ֤א אָבָה֙ לִשְׁתּוֹתָ֔ם וַיַּסֵּ֥ךְ אֹתָ֖ם לַֽיהוָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ διέρρηξαν οἱ τρεῖς δυνατοὶ ἐν τῇ παρεμβολῇ τῶν ἀλλοφύλων καὶ ὑδρεύσαντο ὕδωρ ἐκ τοῦ λάκκου τοῦ ἐν Βαιθλέεμ τοῦ ἐν τῇ πύλῃ, καὶ ἔλαβαν καὶ παρεγένοντο πρὸς Δαυείδ, καὶ οὐκ ἠθέλησεν πιεῖν αὐτό· καὶ ἔσπεισεν αὐτὸ τῷ κυρίῳ}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And the three mighty men brake through the host of the Philistines, and drew water out of the well of Beth-lehem, that was by the gate, and took it, and brought it to David: but he would not drink thereof, but poured it out unto Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] So the three mighty warriors broke through the Philistine lines, drew water from the well near the gate of Bethlehem and carried it back to David. But he refused to drink it; instead, he poured it out before the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] Then the three warriors broke through the camp of the Philistines, drew water from the well of Bethlehem that was by the gate, and brought it to David. But he would not drink of it; he poured it out to the LORD,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:17} % *2SAMUEL-23-17
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-17}

			And he said, Be it far from me, O LORD, that I should do this: is not this the blood of the men that went in jeopardy of their lives? therefore he would not drink it. These things did these three mighty men.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּ֡אמֶר חָלִילָה֩ לִּ֨י יְהוָ֜ה מֵעֲשֹׂ֣תִי זֹ֗את הֲדַ֤ם הָֽאֲנָשִׁים֙ הַהֹלְכִ֣ים בְּנַפְשׁוֹתָ֔ם וְלֹ֥א אָבָ֖ה לִשְׁתּוֹתָ֑ם אֵ֣לֶּה עָשׂ֔וּ שְׁלֹ֖שֶׁת הַגִּבֹּרִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπεν Ἵλεώς μοι, Κύριε, τοῦ ποιῆσαι τοῦτο· εἰ αἷμα τῶν ἀνδρῶν τῶν πορευθέντων ἐν ταῖς ψυχαῖς αὐτῶν πίομαι; καὶ οὐκ ἠθέλησεν πιεῖν αὐτό. ταῦτα ἐποίησαν οἱ τρεῖς δυνατοί.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he said, Be it far from me, O Jehovah, that I should do this: shall I drink the blood of the men that went in jeopardy of their lives? therefore he would not drink it. These things did the three mighty men.
					\item[NIV] “Far be it from me, LORD, to do this!” he said. “Is it not the blood of men who went at the risk of their lives?” And David would not drink it. Such were the exploits of the three mighty warriors.
					\item[NRSV] for he said, “The LORD forbid that I should do this. Can I drink the blood of the men who went at the risk of their lives?” Therefore he would not drink it. The three warriors did these things.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:18} % *2SAMUEL-23-18
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-18}

			And Abishai, the brother of Joab, the son of Zeruiah, was chief among three. And he lifted up his spear against three hundred, and slew them, and had the name among three.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַאֲבִישַׁ֞י אֲחִ֣י ׀ יוֹאָ֣ב בֶּן־צְרוּיָ֗ה ה֚וּא רֹ֣אשׁ הַשְּׁלֹשָׁ֔ה וְהוּא֙ עוֹרֵ֣ר אֶת־חֲנִית֔וֹ עַל־שְׁלֹ֥שׁ מֵא֖וֹת חָלָ֑ל וְלוֹ־שֵׁ֖ם בַּשְּׁלֹשָֽׁה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ Ἀβεισὰ ἀδελφὸς Ἰωάβ, υἱὸς Σαρουίας, αὐτὸς ἄρχων ἐν τοῖς τρισίν, καὶ αὐτὸς ἐξήγειρεν τὸ δόρυ αὐτοῦ ἐπὶ τριακοσίους τραυματίας· καὶ αὐτῷ ὄνομα ἐν τοῖς τρισίν·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Abishai, the brother of Joab, the son of Zeruiah, was chief of the three. And he lifted up his spear against three hundred and slew them, and had a name among the three.
					\item[NIV] Abishai the brother of Joab son of Zeruiah was chief of the Three. He raised his spear against three hundred men, whom he killed, and so he became as famous as the Three.
					\item[NRSV] Now Abishai son of Zeruiah, the brother of Joab, was chief of the Thirty. With his spear he fought against three hundred men and killed them, and won a name beside the Three.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:19} % *2SAMUEL-23-19
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-19}

			Was he not most honourable of three? therefore he was their captain: howbeit he attained not unto the first three.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִן־הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה֙ הֲכִ֣י נִכְבָּ֔ד וַיְהִ֥י לָהֶ֖ם לְשָׂ֑ר וְעַד־הַשְּׁלֹשָׁ֖ה לֹא־בָֽא׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐκ τῶν τριῶν ἐκείνων ἔνδοξος, καὶ ἐγένετο αὐτοῖς εἰς ἄρχοντα, καὶ ἕως τῶν τριῶν οὐκ ἦλθε.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Was he not most honorable of the three? therefore he was made their captain: howbeit he attained not unto the first three.
					\item[NIV] Was he not held in greater honor than the Three? He became their commander, even though he was not included among them.
					\item[NRSV] He was the most renowned of the Thirty, and became their commander; but he did not attain to the Three.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:20} % *2SAMUEL-23-20
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-20}

			And Benaiah the son of Jehoiada, the son of a valiant man, of Kabzeel, who had done many acts, he slew two lionlike men of Moab: he went down also and slew a lion in the midst of a pit in time of snow:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּבְנָיָ֨הוּ בֶן־יְהוֹיָדָ֧ע חַ֛יִל רַב־פְּעָלִ֖ים מִֽקַּבְצְאֵ֑ל ה֣וּא הִכָּ֗ה אֵ֣ת שְׁנֵ֤י אֲרִאֵל֙ מוֹאָ֔ב וְ֠הוּא יָרַ֞ד וְהִכָּ֧ה הָאֲרִ֛י בְּת֥וֹךְ הַבֹּ֖אר בְּי֥וֹם הַשָּֽׁלֶג׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ Βαναίας υἱὸς Ἰωδᾶε, ἀνὴρ αὐτὸς πολλοστὸς ἔργοις ἀπὸ Καβεσεήλ, καὶ αὐτὸς ἐπάταξεν τοὺς δύο υἱοὺς Ἀριὴλ τοῦ Μωάβ· καὶ αὐτὸς κατέβη καὶ ἐπάταξε τὸν λέοντα ἐν μέσῳ τοῦ λάκκου ἐν τῇ ἡμέρᾳ τῆς χιόνος.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And Benaiah the son of Jehoiada, the son of a valiant man of Kabzeel, who had done mighty deeds, he slew the two sons of Ariel of Moab: he went down also and slew a lion in the midst of a pit in time of snow.
					\item[NIV] Benaiah son of Jehoiada, a valiant fighter from Kabzeel, performed great exploits. He struck down Moab’s two mightiest warriors. He also went down into a pit on a snowy day and killed a lion.
					\item[NRSV] Benaiah son of Jehoiada was a valiant warrior from Kabzeel, a doer of great deeds; he struck down two sons of Ariel of Moab. He also went down and killed a lion in a pit on a day when snow had fallen.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:21} % *2SAMUEL-23-21
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-21}

			And he slew an Egyptian, a goodly man: and the Egyptian had a spear in his hand; but he went down to him with a staff, and plucked the spear out of the Egyptian’s hand, and slew him with his own spear.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְהוּא־הִכָּה֩ אֶת־אִ֨ישׁ מִצְרִ֜י אִ֣ישׁ מַרְאֶ֗ה וּבְיַ֤ד הַמִּצְרִי֙ חֲנִ֔ית וַיֵּ֥רֶד אֵלָ֖יו בַּשָּׁ֑בֶט וַיִּגְזֹ֤ל אֶֽת־הַחֲנִית֙ מִיַּ֣ד הַמִּצְרִ֔י וַיַּהַרְגֵ֖הוּ בַּחֲנִיתֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{αὐτὸς ἐπάταξεν τὸν ἄνδρα τὸν Αἰγύπτιον, ἄνδρα ὁρατόν· ἐν δὲ τῇ χειρὶ τοῦ Αἰγυπτίου δόρυ ὡς ξύλον διαβάθρας· καὶ κατέβη πρὸς αὐτὸν ἐν τῷ δόρατι καὶ ἥρπασεν τὸ δόρυ ἐκ τῆς χειρὸς τοῦ Αἰγυπτίου, καὶ ἀπέκτεινεν αὐτὸν ἐν τῷ δόρατι αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he slew an Egyptian, a goodly man: and the Egyptian had a spear in his hand; but he went down to him with a staff, and plucked the spear out of the Egyptian's hand, and slew him with his own spear.
					\item[NIV] And he struck down a huge Egyptian. Although the Egyptian had a spear in his hand, Benaiah went against him with a club. He snatched the spear from the Egyptian’s hand and killed him with his own spear.
					\item[NRSV] And he killed an Egyptian, a handsome man. The Egyptian had a spear in his hand; but Benaiah went against him with a staff, snatched the spear out of the Egyptian’s hand, and killed him with his own spear.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:22} % *2SAMUEL-23-22
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-22}

			These things did Benaiah the son of Jehoiada, and had the name among three mighty men.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֵ֣לֶּה עָשָׂ֔ה בְּנָיָ֖הוּ בֶּן־יְהוֹיָדָ֑ע וְלוֹ־שֵׁ֖ם בִּשְׁלֹשָׁ֥ה הַגִּבֹּרִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ταῦτα ἐποίησεν Βαναίας υἱὸς Ἰωδᾶε, καὶ αὐτῷ ὄνομα ἐν τοῖς τρισὶν τοῖς δυνατοῖς·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] These things did Benaiah the son of Jehoiada, and had a name among the three mighty men.
					\item[NIV] Such were the exploits of Benaiah son of Jehoiada; he too was as famous as the three mighty warriors.
					\item[NRSV] Such were the things Benaiah son of Jehoiada did, and won a name beside the three warriors.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{2 Samuel 23:23} % *2SAMUEL-23-23
		% % ---
			\label{2SAMUEL-23-23}

			He was more honourable than the thirty, but he attained not to the first three. And David set him over his guard.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִן־הַשְּׁלֹשִׁ֣ים נִכְבָּ֔ד וְאֶל־הַשְּׁלֹשָׁ֖ה לֹא־בָ֑א וַיְשִׂמֵ֥הוּ דָוִ֖ד אֶל־מִשְׁמַעְתּֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐκ τῶν τριῶν ἔνδοξος, καὶ πρὸς τοὺς τρεῖς οὐκ ἦλθεν, καὶ ἔταξεν αὐτὸν Δαυεὶδ εἰς τὰς ἀκοὰς αὐτοῦ. Καὶ ταῦτα τὰ ὀνόματα τῶν δυνατῶν Δαυεὶδ βασιλέως.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He was more honorable than the thirty, but he attained not to the first three. And David set him over his guard.
					\item[NIV] He was held in greater honor than any of the Thirty, but he was not included among the Three. And David put him in charge of his bodyguard.
					\item[NRSV] He was renowned among the Thirty, but he did not attain to the Three. And David put him in charge of his bodyguard.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

					\item[LXX] \gr{Ἀσαὴλ ἀδελφὸς Ἰωάβ, οὗτος ἐν τοῖς τριάκοντα· Ἐλεανὰν υἱὸς Δουδεὶ πατραδέλφου αὐτοῦ ἐν Βαιθλέεμ·}
